## 连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流 [[Back]](#toc)

关注连续视觉语言模型、连续视觉 token、视觉 VAE/ViT latent、flow matching 与多模态理解。

### [1] [SLAP: The Semantic Least Action Principle for Variational Video-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30750)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流

Importance: 值得关注 (76.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SLAP 提出语义最小作用原理，将视频语言模型中的时序插帧问题从概率生成范式转向变分力学范式，通过语义拉格朗日量在黎曼流形上建模潜视频轨迹，利用离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解边界值问题，实现无需像素级渲染的物体持久性保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频语言模型(LVLM)因稀疏帧采样导致严重的"时序间隙"，现有基于生成式幻觉（如潜扩散）或自回归外推的方法在长时序上难以保持语义一致性，存在物体消失和能量不稳定性问题。

Method: 提出语义最小作用原理(SLAP)，在经典力学与语义动力学之间建立严格的同构关系，将潜视频轨迹建模为黎曼流形上的路径，由语义拉格朗日量(Semantic Lagrangian)控制。将插帧任务形式化为边界值问题(BVP)，通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解，自然实现物体持久性保持而无需像素级渲染。

Result: 大量实验表明 SLAP 在视频语言建模的时序插帧任务上有效，能够保持语义一致性和物体持久性，优于现有基于生成式幻觉和自回归外推的方法。

Conclusion: SLAP 从概率生成到变分力学的范式转换有效解决了 LVLM 中的时序间隙问题，为视频语言建模提供了新的理论框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出语义最小作用原理(SLAP)，将视频插帧建模为黎曼流形上的变分路径优化问题，通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解边界值问题，属于连续视觉潜空间中的流建模。这与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流" 高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal latent reasoning via variational mechanics, aligning with top-weighted priority subtopics. Signals: continuous language model, continuous multimodal model, visual latent flow, flow matching, representation autoencoder

Abstract: In the era of Large Video-Language Models (LVLMs), the computational necessity of sparse frame sampling creates a fundamental ``temporal gap'', rendering models blind to critical causal transitions. Existing solutions relying on generative hallucination (e.g., latent diffusion) or autoregressive extrapolation often fail to maintain semantic consistency over long horizons, suffering from object vanishing and energetic instability. We propose a paradigm shift from probabilistic generation to variational mechanics with the \textbf{Semantic Least Action Principle (SLAP)}. Drawing a rigorous isomorphism between classical mechanics and semantic dynamics, we model the latent video trajectory as a path on a Riemannian manifold governed by a Semantic Lagrangian. By formulating the interpolation task as a Boundary Value Problem (BVP) solved via the discrete Euler-Lagrange equations, SLAP naturally enforces object persistence without pixel-level rendering. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of our proposed SLAP.

</details>


### [2] [How can embedding models bind concepts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31503)
*Arnas Uselis, Darina Koishigarina, Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流

Importance: 值得关注 (72.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言嵌入模型（如CLIP）中的概念绑定问题，发现场景嵌入可加性分解为物体表示，但CLIP的绑定函数复杂度高，导致跨模态绑定泛化失败；通过控制实验证明，在足够数据覆盖下，Transformer模型可以学习低复杂度的乘法交互绑定函数，实现系统性泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可以轻松判断多物体场景中哪个颜色属于哪个形状（概念绑定），但CLIP等视觉-语言嵌入模型无法做到这一点——它们能识别单个概念，但无法表示哪些概念构成哪个物体。本文旨在探究这一限制的根本原因以及是否可克服。

Method: 首先通过理论分析证明场景嵌入可加性分解为物体表示，解释为何单模态探针能恢复物体信息。然后通过控制Transformer模型从零训练的实验，研究数据覆盖度对绑定泛化的影响，并分析绑定函数的复杂度（线性 vs 乘法交互）。

Result: CLIP的绑定函数是高复杂度的，这阻止了图像和文本编码器学习共享的绑定机制来泛化到未见过的概念组合。但在控制实验中，当数据覆盖足够时，Transformer模型可以学习低复杂度的绑定函数，其特征是概念之间的乘法交互，从而实现系统性泛化。

Conclusion: CLIP的概念绑定限制并非根本性的，通过足够的数据覆盖和合适的模型设计，可以学习到具有系统性泛化能力的绑定机制。这为改进多模态嵌入模型的组合泛化能力提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 CLIP 等视觉-语言嵌入模型中的概念绑定（binding）机制，分析场景嵌入的可加性分解与绑定函数的复杂度，属于连续视觉语言模型（VLM）的表示学习问题，与 canonical subtopic "连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly investigates concept binding in CLIP, a core limitation for continuous multimodal models, and shows transformers can learn low-complexity binding for systematic generalization. Signals: concept binding, CLIP limitation, systematic generalization, multiplicative interactions, continuous_language_multimodal

Abstract: Humans easily determine which color belongs to which shape in multi-object scenes, an ability known as concept binding. Vision-language embedding models such as CLIP struggle with binding: they recognize individual concepts but fail to represent which concepts form which objects. Although CLIP behaves like a bag-of-concepts model in cross-modal retrieval, object information is recoverable from its image and text embeddings separately. We study this tension through the binding function, which maps concepts to scene embeddings. We find that scene embeddings decompose additively into object representations, explaining why uni-modal probes can recover object information. However, CLIP's binding function is high-complexity, which likely prevents the image and text encoders from learning a shared binding mechanism that generalizes to unseen concept combinations. We then ask whether this limitation is fundamental. We show that it is not. In controlled transformer models trained from scratch, binding generalization emerges with sufficient data coverage. These models learn low-complexity binding functions characterized by multiplicative interactions between concepts, enabling systematic generalization. Code is publicly available at .

</details>

## 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器 [[Back]](#toc)

面向理解与生成统一的连续视觉 token、VAE/ViT、RAE、DINO/SigLIP/MAE latent 与视觉表征重建。

### [3] [MergeTok: Unified Continuous and Discrete Visual Tokenization via Token Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30904)
*Luyuan Zhang, Siyuan Li, Zedong Wang, Qingsong Xie, Cheng Tan, Anna Wang, Yanhao Zhang, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器

Importance: 高优先级 (96.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MergeTok 是一种统一的视觉 tokenizer，通过 token merging 技术桥接连续 VAE 和离散 VQ 两种范式，在单一架构中同时实现高保真重建和语义组织良好的离散 token，兼容自回归和扩散生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉 tokenizer 分为连续 VAE（高保真重建但 latent 稠密纠缠、缺乏语义控制）和离散 VQ（支持自回归生成但梯度稀疏、训练不稳定、码本崩溃）两类，各有互补局限。作者希望设计一个统一的 tokenizer 架构，同时具备两者的优势。

Method: MergeTok 采用编码器-解码器架构，在编码阶段通过 token merging（聚类相似 token）建立结构先验，提供双重监督信号：(1) 在 VAE 分支施加 merged-token 语义对齐，正则化 latent 空间使其解耦且语义感知；(2) 导出分组约束，促进组内多样性和组间排他性，稳定 VQ 训练。最终产生语义组织的 token 表征，兼容自回归和扩散生成器。

Result: 在 ImageNet-256 上，MergeTok 在匹配 token 预算下取得了比强 VAE 和 VQ 模型更低的 rFID，重建和生成性能均具有竞争力，且生成的 token 表征具有语义组织性。

Conclusion: 单一架构可以赋予视觉 tokenizer 鲁棒的语义组织和生成器友好的离散性，统一了连续与离散两种 tokenizer 范式的优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MergeTok，在编码器-解码器架构中联合优化连续 VAE 和离散 VQ tokenizer，属于连续视觉 tokenizer 与表征自编码器方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续视觉 Tokenizer 与表征自编码器" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the user's top-priority continuous language/multimodal model subtopic with a novel unified tokenizer bridging continuous and discrete paradigms. Signals: continuous visual tokenizer, token merging, unified VAE and VQ, semantic-aware representation, autoregressive and diffusion compatible

Abstract: Most visual tokenizers for image generation are bifurcated into two families with complementary limitations: continuous VAEs offer high-fidelity reconstruction but suffer from dense, entangled latents that are poorly suited for semantic control, whereas discrete VQ-based models enable autoregressive generation yet struggle with gradient sparsity, unstable training, and codebook collapse. In this work, we introduce MergeTok, a unified tokenizer that jointly optimizes continuous (VAE) and discrete (VQ) tokenizers within a encoder-decoder architecture, leveraging token merging techniques as a semantic bridge. By clustering similar tokens during encoding, MergeTok establishes a structural prior that provides dual supervision signals: (i) it imposes merged-token semantic alignment in the VAE branch, regularizing its latent space toward disentangled, semantic-aware representations; (ii) it derives group-wise constraints, promoting intra-group diversity and inter-group exclusivity that stabilize VQ training. MergeTok shows competitive reconstruction and generation performance on ImageNet-256, with substantially lower rFID than strong VAE and VQ models under matched token budgets, while producing semantically-organized token representations compatible with both autoregressive and diffusion generators. This shows that a single architecture can endow visual tokenizers with robust semantic organization and generator-friendly discreteness.

</details>

### [4] [ELAN4D: Embodiment-Centric 4D Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Models via Plug-and-Play Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30484)
*Zeyuan He, Bowen Yang, Zhirui Fang, Keru Zhou, Lei Jiang, Jingjing Qian, Fan Mo, Junchi Yan, Philip Torr, Xiu Li, Li Jiang, Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (60.63) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ELAN4D 是一个以具身为中心的 4D 感知训练框架，通过前向运动学推导机器人关键点 3D 位移轨迹作为未来预测监督信号，以即插即用的辅助分支注入 VLA 动作专家，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0 及真实操控任务上显著提升了策略在分布外扰动下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略大多直接根据当前观测回归动作，缺乏对未来动力学的显式建模，导致在分布外扰动（如相机、背景、布局变化）下泛化能力不足。作者希望引入未来预测性时空监督来增强策略的鲁棒性。

Method: 利用前向运动学从本体感知状态推导机器人关键点（关节和末端执行器）的 3D 位移轨迹，无需外部跟踪器或重建。设计一个即插即用的辅助分支，包含轻量级轨迹解码器，通过梯度隔离将 4D 信号注入动作专家，同时保持预训练视觉-语言骨干不变。推理时丢弃轨迹解码器，不改变基础策略接口。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin2.0 和真实世界操控任务上，ELAN4D 一致优于强 VLA 基线，在分布外扰动（相机、背景、布局变化）下取得显著提升，整体性能达到最佳。

Conclusion: 以具身为中心的 4D 监督是一种有效且轻量的方法，能够构建更鲁棒和泛化的操控策略，且即插即用的设计使其易于集成到现有 VLA 框架中。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ELAN4D，通过未来机器人关键点轨迹作为预测性时空监督来增强 VLA 策略的鲁棒性和泛化能力，核心是面向机器人操控的具身世界建模与动作规划，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong robotics world model approach but outside user's core continuous multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: 4D supervision for VLA, embodiment-centric future prediction, plug-and-play adaptation, robust manipulation under distribution shift, forward kinematics-based tracking

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promise for robotic manipulation, yet most existing policies operate reactively by directly regressing actions from current observations, without explicitly modeling future dynamics. This limits their ability to generalize under out-of-distribution perturbations. To address this issue, we propose ELAN4D, an embodiment-centric, 4D-aware training framework that enhances VLA policies with future robot keypoint tracks as predictive spatio-temporal supervision. Using only forward kinematics from proprioceptive states, we derive 3D displacement tracks of robot keypoints, such as joints and the end-effector, with negligible preprocess cost. These tracks provide metric and compact supervision without requiring external trackers or reconstruction. A plug-and-play auxiliary branch with a lightweight track decoder injects this 4D signal into the action expert while preserving the pretrained vision-language backbone through gradient isolation. The track decoder is discarded during inference, leaving the base policy interface unchanged. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that ELAN4D consistently improves over strong VLA baselines, achieving the best overall performance and substantial gains under out-of-distribution perturbations, including camera, background, and layout shifts. These results highlight the effectiveness of embodiment-centric 4D supervision for building more robust and generalizable manipulation policies.

</details>


### [5] [Physics-informed Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning under Hybrid Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30503)
*Vittorio Giammarino, Anastasios Manganaris, Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO, stat.ML, eess.SY

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (54.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息目标条件强化学习（Pi-GCRL）方法，针对接触丰富操作任务中混合接触动力学导致的价值函数非光滑和模式依赖可控性问题，引入接触感知和分层公式，将物理信息归纳偏置选择性地应用于操作问题，有效提升了在接触丰富场景下的目标达成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息目标条件强化学习（Pi-GCRL）方法在导航和无物体目标到达任务中表现良好，但在接触丰富的操作任务中，接触交互导致的混合动力学、模式依赖可控性和非光滑价值景观使得这些方法直接应用时性能退化。本文旨在分析这些结构特性带来的挑战，并提出适用于接触丰富操作的改进方法。

Method: 本文首先分析了接触丰富操作中混合动力学对Pi-GCRL的影响，然后提出接触感知和分层两种物理信息公式：接触感知公式将接触信息显式融入价值函数学习；分层公式将任务分解为高层目标规划和低层接触感知控制，从而将物理信息归纳偏置选择性地应用于不同层级。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的接触感知和分层Pi-GCRL方法在接触丰富的操作任务中显著优于现有Pi-GCRL基线，能够有效处理混合接触动力学带来的挑战，实现更可靠的目标达成。

Conclusion: 本文为将物理信息目标条件强化学习扩展到接触丰富操作任务提供了原则性步骤，证明了接触感知和分层设计是处理混合接触动力学下价值学习退化的有效策略。

Classification reason: 该论文研究接触丰富的机器人操作任务中的目标条件强化学习，引入物理信息归纳偏置和接触感知分层公式，属于具身智能中的规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on contact-rich manipulation with physics-informed RL, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: physics-informed RL, goal-conditioned RL, contact-rich manipulation, hybrid dynamics, robotics

Abstract: Learning to reach arbitrary goals from sparse feedback requires agents to infer a rich notion of reachability across state--goal pairs. Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) tackles this challenge by learning policies that generalize across goals, but this generalization becomes increasingly difficult as the underlying dynamics become high-dimensional, hybrid, or contact-dependent. To address this issue, physics-informed GCRL (Pi-GCRL) introduces optimal-control-inspired inductive biases into goal-conditioned value learning. While Pi-GCRL methods have proven effective in navigation and object-free goal-reaching domains, their reliability in contact-rich tasks remains unclear, where contact interactions induce hybrid dynamics, mode-dependent controllability, and nonsmooth value landscapes. In this work, we show that these structural properties can cause existing Pi-GCRL methods to degrade when applied naively to contact-rich manipulation. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce contact-aware and hierarchical formulations that apply physics-informed inductive biases selectively across the manipulation problem. Our results provide a principled step toward extending Pi-GCRL to contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [6] [VLM-GLoc: Vision-Language Model Enhanced Monte Carlo Localization for Robust Semantic Global Localization in Cluttered Quasi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30506)
*Shivendra Agrawal, Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: VLM-GLoc提出一种层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位方法，利用开放词汇视觉语言模型作为统一语义观测前端，通过逆语义提议机制和文本到地图检索实现机器人在几何混淆、准静态环境（如杂货店、实验室）中的鲁棒全局定位，在两个真实场景中分别达到70%和74%的定位成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局定位方法在几何混淆（如平行货架）和准静态环境（如杂货店、办公室）中表现不佳，因为它们依赖独特的几何特征或领域特定的视觉管线，难以处理长尾语义分布和瞬态视觉杂乱。作者希望利用VLM的开放词汇语义理解能力来提升定位的鲁棒性和泛化性。

Method: 提出VLM-GLoc，一种层次化语义蒙特卡洛定位方法。核心包括：(1) 使用VLM作为统一语义观测前端，提取判别性文本特征；(2) 隐式质量过滤，自动排除模糊或动态物体；(3) 永久性推理，对半静态物体进行数据增强；(4) 逆语义提议机制，通过文本到地图检索初始化粒子。整体采用从粗到细的定位策略。

Result: 在两个真实环境（3500平方英尺杂货店+手机平台、3700平方英尺实验室+四足机器人）上评估，VLM-GLoc分别达到70%和74%的全局定位成功率，显著优于传统纯几何方法和领域特定基线方法。

Conclusion: VLM作为统一语义观测前端能够有效提升机器人在几何混淆、准静态环境中的全局定位性能，其开放词汇特性使其无需领域特定训练即可泛化到不同场景。该方法为将VLM集成到机器人定位系统中提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出VLM-GLoc，利用VLM增强的蒙特卡洛定位方法，解决机器人在杂乱准静态环境中的全局定位问题。核心是机器人定位与导航，属于具身智能中的世界模型用于规划控制范畴，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]（CANONICAL）。同时该方法使用VLM作为语义观测前端，也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Applies VLMs to robotics localization but is tangential to core priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM for semantic localization, quasi-static environments, inverse semantic proposal, text-to-map retrieval, robotics application

Abstract: Global localization in geometrically aliased, quasi-static environments such as grocery stores, offices, schools, and hospitals poses a significant challenge for mobile robots. Grocery stores with parallel aisles and a long tailed distribution of products, as well as offices and labs with repetitive furniture such as chairs, desks, monitors, and doors, exemplify common indoor environments that present geometric and even semantic ambiguity. Traditional approaches rely either on distinct geometric features or on domain-specific vision pipelines that struggle with long-tail semantic distributions and transient visual clutter. We present VLM-GLoc, a method for hierarchical semantic Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) that leverages open-vocabulary Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified semantic observation front-end. We hypothesize a three-fold benefit from VLMs: (1) extracting highly discriminative rich text features, (2) implicit quality filtering of blurry or dynamic objects, and (3) permanence reasoning for targeted data augmentation. We introduce an inverse semantic proposal mechanism that seeds particles via text-to-map retrieval. Evaluated across two real-world environments with different characteristics and two different platforms: a 3,500 sq. ft. grocery store with a cellphone and a 3,700 sq. ft. lab space with a quadruped, VLM-GLoc achieves 70% and 74% global localization success respectively, substantially outperforming traditional geometry-only and domain-specific baselines.

</details>

TL;DR: SSR 是一个端到端的基于第一人称视觉的人形机器人遍历框架，通过想象落脚点引导、等变潜空间对称增强和地形特定多判别器运动先验，实现了在开放世界中安全、稳定、高质量的人形机器人运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人形机器人部署到开放世界环境中面临巨大挑战：机器人需要利用视觉在异构地形上实现安全可靠的落脚点放置，同时产生协调自然的全身行为。现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: SSR 提出三个关键组件：(1) 想象落脚点引导（Imagined Foothold Guidance），学习建模即将到来的摆动脚接触并评估其支撑性，引导触地前摆动脚朝向稳定区域，减少边缘滑落；(2) 等变潜空间对称增强（Equivariant Latent-space Symmetry Augmentation），在高维视觉观测下高效诱导双侧协调行为；(3) 地形特定多判别器运动先验（Terrain-specific Multi-discriminator Motion Priors），鼓励跨场景的人类化行为。

Result: 在多种真实世界地形上（包括不同结构的楼梯、宽间隙、高平台等极端挑战）实现了安全、稳定、高质量的 locomotion，并在开放户外环境中实现了可靠的长时程遍历。

Conclusion: SSR 证明了端到端学习视觉引导的人形机器人遍历在开放世界中的可行性，为实际部署提供了有效框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于第一人称视觉的人形机器人全身运动控制与地形遍历，属于具身智能中的机器人规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics contribution but outside user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: Humanoid traversal, Egocentric vision, Imagined foothold guidance, Equivariant latent symmetry, Open-world deployment

Abstract: Extending humanoid traversal to the open world is key to practical deployment in human environments, but remains challenging. The robot must use vision to ensure safe and reliable foot placement on heterogeneous terrain under highly dynamic motion, while producing coordinated, natural whole-body behaviors. We propose SSR, an efficient end-to-end framework for egocentric vision-based humanoid traversal that jointly learns these capabilities. SSR introduces imagined foothold guidance, which learns to model forthcoming swing-foot contacts and evaluates their support to guide pre-touchdown swings toward stable regions, reducing edge slips. It further employs equivariant latent-space symmetry augmentation to efficiently induce bilateral coordination under high-dimensional visual observations, and uses terrain-specific multi-discriminator motion priors to encourage human-like behavior across scenes. Extensive experiments show that SSR achieves safe, stable, and high-quality locomotion on diverse real-world terrains, including stairs with varied structures and extreme challenges such as wide gaps and high platforms, while enabling reliable long-horizon traversal in open outdoor environments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出分层MPPI控制框架：1）先求解简化物体级问题（假设物体可直接驱动），得到物体轨迹作为参考；2）在联合机器人-物体规划问题中，以该物体轨迹为引导，使用MPPI进行机器人级规划。在6-DoF xArm6机械臂上进行物体推放任务验证。

Result: 仿真中任务成功率提升40%，控制频率提升26%；真实实验中成功率提升20%，计算开销与常规MPPI相当。

Conclusion: 物体信息引导的分层MPPI方法能有效提升非抓取操作中长时域规划的性能，在保持计算效率的同时显著提高任务成功率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种面向非抓取机器人操作的分层MPPI控制方法，利用物体级规划引导机器人级规划，属于机器人规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot manipulation control, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Non-prehensile manipulation, Model predictive path integral, Hierarchical planning, Real-world robot experiments, Long-horizon planning

Abstract: Long-horizon planning for non-prehensile robot manipulation is challenging due to underactuated and discontinuous interactions. We propose a hierarchical formulation of model predictive path integral (MPPI) control that guides robot-level planning with a separately computed object-level plan to achieve efficient long-horizon prediction. We first solve a simplified object-only problem, assuming the object can be actuated directly, and use the planned object trajectory as a reference in solving the joint robot-object planning problem. We evaluate our method in both simulation and hardware using a 6-DoF xArm6 manipulator to perform object pushing tasks in which the target object must reach a goal while avoiding static obstacles, necessitating non-myopic reasoning. Our object-informed MPPI increases task success by 40\% with a 26\% faster control frequency in simulation, and by 20\% in real experiments with similar computation as regular MPPI.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出Hide-and-Seek框架，通过轨迹级粗粒度监督（无需逐时间步标注）结合轨迹间与轨迹内对比学习，定位VLA模型执行过程中的故障指示性动作，实现多任务故障检测，在LIBERO、VLABench和真实机器人平台上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人部署中仍存在执行失败问题，现有故障检测方法要么依赖昂贵的动作重采样或外部模型，要么将轨迹级标签均匀传播到每个时间步从而模糊局部故障信号。需要一种无需逐时间步标注、能精确定位故障时刻的轻量级检测方法。

Method: 将VLA故障检测建模为粗粒度监督学习问题，设计轨迹间对比学习（区分成功/失败轨迹的整体表征）和轨迹内对比学习（在失败轨迹中区分故障时刻与正常时刻的动作表征），联合优化以从轨迹级标签中诱导出时间结构化的故障信号。结合保形预测（conformal prediction）实现准确性与及时性的权衡。

Result: 在LIBERO、VLABench和真实机器人平台上，针对OpenVLA、π₀、π₀.₅三种VLA策略，该方法在多任务故障检测中达到SOTA性能，在保形预测框架下实现了良好的准确率-及时性权衡，并泛化到未见任务。

Conclusion: Hide-and-Seek通过粗粒度监督和对比学习有效解决了VLA故障检测中缺乏细粒度标注的问题，为机器人系统的可靠部署提供了一种实用且高效的运行时监控方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的运行时故障检测，属于机器人具身智能中的执行监控与可靠性问题，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]这一规范子主题紧密相关，因为该子主题涵盖机器人具身智能中的规划、控制与策略学习，而故障检测是确保可靠执行的关键环节。

Importance reason: Strong world model and VLA reliability work, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: VLA runtime monitoring, trajectory-level failure detection, contrastive learning, world model robustness, real-world robot deployment

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural language instructions and generalize across diverse tasks, but they remain vulnerable to execution failures that compromise reliability in real-world deployment. Detecting such failures during execution is therefore critical for the robust deployment of embodied systems. Existing failure detection methods either rely on expensive action resampling or external models, while alternatives propagate trajectory-level labels uniformly across every timestep, obscuring localized failure signals. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Hide-and-Seek}, a framework that formulates VLA failure detection as a coarsely supervised learning problem. By combining inter-trajectory and intra-trajectory contrastive objectives, Hide-and-Seek localizes failure-indicative actions and induces temporally structured failure signals from trajectory-level supervision alone, without any step-level annotation. We evaluate Hide-and-Seek on LIBERO, VLABench, and a real-world robotic platform across three representative VLA policies: OpenVLA, $\pi_0$, and $\pi_{0.5}$.Our method achieves state-of-the-art multi-task failure detection performance with a practical accuracy--timeliness trade-off under conformal prediction, and generalizes well to both seen and unseen tasks.

</details>


### [10] [TARIC: Memory-Augmented Traversability-Aware Outdoor VLN under Interrupted Semantic Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31121)
*Tianle Zeng, Hanjing Ye, Jianwei Peng, Jingwen Yu, Hanxuan Chen, Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

TL;DR: 本文提出TARIC，一个面向户外VLN的统一框架，通过可通行性一致的执行引导和世界对齐的3D线索记忆，使机器人在语义线索中断的长距离导航中保持目标导向行为，在仿真和真实场景中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外长距离VLN中，语义线索（如目标地标）常因遮挡、稀疏或离开视野而中断，导致机器人退化为回溯、振荡或盲目探索。现有记忆方法在可通行性驱动的绕行场景下失效，因为记忆的线索方向可能不可通行，绕行延长了无线索阶段，使线索逐渐过时。因此需要一种能在长时间无线索阶段维持目标导向引导的统一框架。

Method: 提出TARIC框架：(1) 从可见性门控的目标或探索线索中提取语义方位，利用实时近场可通行性剖面将其映射为可执行航向，提供超越拒绝式安全过滤的目标一致可行引导；(2) 将间歇性2D证据提升为世界对齐的3D线索记忆，并采用不确定性感知的读出机制，确保机器人在移动过程中引导持续可达且稳定。

Result: 在四足和轮式平台上进行600-1000米路线评估。仿真中成功率比最强基线提升超过10个百分点；真实场景中成功率达40%，而最强基线仅17.5%，在长时间无线索阶段具有显著更高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 可通行性是维持目标导向引导的稳定性条件，而不仅仅是局部安全问题。TARIC通过可通行性一致的执行引导和3D线索记忆，有效解决了户外VLN中语义线索中断的挑战，在长距离导航中实现了更鲁棒的目标导向行为。

Classification reason: 该论文研究户外视觉语言导航（VLN），涉及四足/轮式机器人在长距离开放世界中的语义线索中断问题，提出基于3D记忆和可通行性感知的统一导航框架，属于具身智能中的机器人导航、规划与控制，因此映射到[具身世界模型与规划控制]（CANONICAL）。

Importance reason: Focuses on outdoor robot navigation, not core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: outdoor VLN, traversability-aware, memory-augmented, semantic cue interruption, quadrupedal/wheeled platforms

Abstract: Outdoor vision-language navigation (VLN) in long-range, open-world environments is frequently disrupted by semantic-cue interruptions, where informative goal cues become sparse, occluded, or leave the field of view. Once such cues disappear, agents enter a cue-free phase and often degrade into backtracking, oscillatory headings, or aimless exploration. While memory-based methods attempt to bridge these gaps, they often fail under traversability-driven detours: the remembered cue direction may be infeasible, forcing detours that prolong cue-free phases and gradually render robot-centric cues stale and implicit histories blurred. This makes traversability a stability condition for maintaining goal-directed guidance, rather than merely a local safety concern. We propose a unified outdoor VLN framework that survives semantic-cue interruptions by maintaining traversability-consistent executable guidance throughout prolonged cue-free phases. Specifically, our method extracts semantic bearings from visibility-gated goal or exploration cues and grounds them into executable headings using a real-time near-field traversability profile, providing goal-consistent feasible guidance beyond reject-only safety filtering. To prevent guidance degradation during detours, we lift intermittent 2D evidence into a world-aligned 3D cue memory with an uncertainty-aware readout mechanism, ensuring guidance remains continuously reachable and stable as the robot moves. We evaluate the framework on quadrupedal and wheeled platforms over 600--1000 m routes. Our method improves simulation success rate by over 10 percentage points over the strongest baseline and achieves a real-world success rate of 40%, compared to 17.5% for the strongest baseline, with substantially higher robustness during prolonged cue-free intervals.

</details>


### [11] [HARP-VLA: Human-Robot Aligned Representation Learning for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31234)
*Xiang Zhu, Puzhen Yuan, Yichen Liu, Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (63.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HARP提出一种人-机器人对齐的表征学习框架，通过有限配对数据和大量未配对数据训练对齐的视觉编码器和潜动作模型，使VLA模型能有效利用人类视频进行预训练，在CALVIN和真实机器人任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从大规模人类视频中学习可泛化的VLA模型面临跨具身差异（视觉观察和可执行动作不一致）的挑战。现有潜动作模型虽能缓解动作执行差异，但仍依赖视觉特征，而人-机器人视觉表征的不对齐会导致策略输入不一致和领域依赖的潜动作，阻碍人类视频的有效联合训练。

Method: HARP框架包含：(1) 利用有限配对人-机器人演示作为跨具身桥梁，大量未配对视频作为动力学监督数据；(2) 训练机器人适配的视觉编码器和潜动作模型，引入操作中心辅助线索和源相对配对判别对齐损失，使机器人表征向人类语义对齐同时保持配对级判别性；(3) 对齐后的视觉编码器和潜动作模型提供统一的视觉-动作表征，人类和机器人视频提供视觉-语言到潜动作监督，轻量级机器人动作头将潜动作映射为可执行命令。

Result: 在CALVIN ABC→D任务上达到4.481平均长度，真实机器人操作成功率比最强基线提升7.1%。特征可视化、仿真和真实实验均验证了人-机器人对齐的有效性和下游策略性能的提升。

Conclusion: HARP通过人-机器人对齐的表征学习有效弥合了跨具身差异，使VLA模型能够从人类视频中受益进行预训练，为利用大规模人类数据进行具身策略学习提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向人-机器人对齐的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型预训练框架，核心是使用人类视频进行具身策略学习，属于具身智能与机器人规划控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics paper but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords apply. Signals: world_model, human-robot alignment, representation learning, VLA pretraining, latent action model

Abstract: Learning generalizable vision-language-action (VLA) models from large-scale human videos is promising but challenging due to cross-embodiment discrepancies in both visual observations and executable actions. While latent action models reduce the action execution gap by learning action abstractions, they still rely on visual features. Thus, misaligned human and robot visual representations can lead to inconsistencies in policy inputs and induce domain-dependent latent actions, hindering effective co-training with human videos. To address this, we propose HARP, a human-robot aligned representation learning framework for more effective VLA pretraining from human videos. Specifically, HARP uses limited paired human-robot demonstrations as cross-embodiment bridges and abundant unpaired human and robot videos as a scalable dynamics supervision data source. It trains a robot-adapted visual encoder and a latent action model with manipulation-centric auxiliary cues and a source-relative pair-discriminative alignment loss, which adapts robot representations toward human semantics while preserving pair-level discrimination. The learned aligned vision encoder and latent action model provide a unified vision and action representation for VLA-style policy learning, where human and robot videos provide vision-language-to-latent-action supervision and a lightweight robot action head grounds latent actions into executable commands. Experiments on feature visualization, simulation, and realworld manipulation show improved human-robot alignment and downstream policy performance, achieving 4.481 average length on CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D and a 7.1\% realworld success rate gain over the strongest baseline.

<details>

Method: DeMaVLA 采用 VLM 骨干网络（如 SigLIP + Qwen2-VL）与动作专家（action expert）结合，使用流匹配（flow matching）进行连续动作生成。动作专家通过每隔一层剪枝 VLM 骨干网络构建，同时保持层间对齐，降低训练和推理成本。先在约5000小时真实双臂演示上预训练获取通用操作先验，再通过人类参与的 DAgger 数据聚合管道，在混合折叠数据上后训练。

Conclusion: DeMaVLA 展示了通过大规模真实世界数据预训练、高效动作生成架构和纠正学习，可以训练出跨类别、跨场景泛化的可变形物体操作 VLA 基础模型，为家庭机器人通用操作技能提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Real-world household robots require Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models that can acquire reusable manipulation skills across diverse objects, task conditions, and household environments. Deformable-object folding is a representative challenge, requiring robots to handle clothing items from random initial states across varying categories, geometries, materials, and scenes. However, existing VLA systems commonly train separate policies for different object categories, while naively mixed multi-task training often suffers from task interference and degraded performance. To move beyond category-specific folding policies, we introduce DeMaVLA, a VLA foundation model for generalizable Deformable Manipulation. DeMaVLA adopts a VLM backbone with an action expert and formulates continuous action generation using flow matching. To improve efficiency, the action expert is constructed by pruning every other transformer layer while preserving layer-wise alignment with the VLM backbone, reducing training and inference cost. DeMaVLA is first pre-trained on approximately 5,000 hours of selected real-world dual-arm demonstrations to acquire general manipulation priors. It is then post-trained on mixed folding data that aggregates self-collected demonstrations and corrective trajectories from real-robot failures across multiple folding tasks through a human-in-the-loop Data Aggregation~(DAgger) pipeline. Experiments show that DeMaVLA achieves competitive performance on RoboTwin and strong real-world results on our household folding benchmark. These results highlight the value of scalable real-world data, efficient action generation, and corrective learning for general-purpose VLA policies in deformable-object manipulation.

</details>

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，TA-WBC 相比基线方法实现了更大的可达空间、更小的跟踪误差和更少的意外摔倒，验证了其在复杂地形上执行移动操作任务的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将地形感知引入腿臂融合机器人全身控制的有效性，统一策略在跨地形移动操作任务中展现出优越性能，为具身机器人在人类环境中部署提供了有前景的方案。

Abstract: Legged manipulators integrate exceptional terrain adaptability along with mobile manipulation capabilities, which make them highly promising for deployment in human-centric environments. By coordinating the control of both legs and arms, a whole-body controller can significantly expand the operational workspace of legged manipulators. However, many existing whole-body controllers primarily depend on proprioception and do not incorporate the critical exteroception required for effective terrain topology perception. This limitation can hinder their ability to adapt to varying environmental conditions and navigate complex terrains effectively. In this paper, we introduce TA-WBC, a terrain-aware whole-body control framework for legged manipulators, which features a novel RL-based unified policy tailored to whole-body loco-manipulation tasks in various terrains. Specifically, we employ a hybrid exteroception encoder to extract terrain features, providing an essential basis for the robot to proactively adapt posture and footholds. Furthermore, to facilitate stable cross-terrain loco-manipulation, we propose a novel end-effector sampling method based on the foot contact plane, decoupling manipulation target from base fluctuations. Moreover, a dual-policy distillation module is introduced to integrate expansive whole-body motion with terrain adaptability without catastrophic forgetting. The simulation and real-world experiments validate the robustness of our proposed controller, which leads to a larger reachable space, less tracking error, and reduced unexpected stumbles. This unified policy highlights the promising capabilities of legged manipulators in performing loco-manipulation tasks across complex terrains.

</details>

Result: 在 Replica 数据集仿真中，相比 SOTA 辐射场基线方法，LiftNav 实现了 100% 的路径可行率和更短的轨迹长度。

Conclusion: LiftNav 通过语义 lifting 与 TSDF+GS 混合表示，有效结合了语义理解与安全规划，为未知室内环境中的自主导航提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向自主机器人的混合导航框架，结合TSDF+GS双地图与YOLO检测、3D lifting和B样条轨迹优化，实现语义导航与避障。核心是机器人路径规划与控制，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "具身世界模型与规划控制"。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics navigation with TSDF and Gaussian Splatting, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: Robotics navigation, TSDF+GS dual map, semantic lifting, B-spline optimization, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Autonomous robots in unknown indoor environments require both reliable collision avoidance and object-level understanding. Classical representations such as TSDF support safe planning but lack semantics, while photorealistic methods like Gaussian Splatting (GS) provide rich appearance yet suffer from soft geometry, limiting precise obstacle avoidance. We present LiftNav, a hybrid navigation framework built on GSFusion's TSDF+GS dual map, augmented with a real-time pipeline of YOLO-based detection, TSDF-based 3D lifting, and B-spline trajectory optimization. This design enables flexible semantic navigation without dense 3D embeddings. We further introduce a hinge-loss-based collision penalty that improves trajectory smoothness and safety. We evaluate our approach in a simulation using the Replica dataset. Compared against a state-of-the-art radiance field baseline we show a 100% feasibility rate and shorter trajectories.

</details>

Abstract: MLLM-powered embodied agents deployed in real-world environments encounter physical hazards. However, existing approaches lack explicit mechanisms for identifying hazards and reasoning about action-conditioned risks, leading agents to either miss risky interactions or over-identify risks. To address this, we propose EMBGuard, the first MLLM-based safety guardrail for embodied agents designed to decouple physical risk reasoning from agent policy. By evaluating a (visual observation, action) pair, EMBGuard identifies hazardous configurations and provides natural language explanations of potential risks. Alongside EMBGuard, we contribute EMBHazard, a training dataset of 15.1K action-conditioned pairs, and EMBGuardTest, a benchmark of 329 manually curated real-world scenarios spanning seven physical risk categories. Through compositional variation of hazards and actions, we generate diverse risky and benign scenarios that agents may encounter during planning. Despite its compact size (2B, 4B), EMBGuard achieves performance competitive with proprietary MLLMs (e.g., GPT-5.1, Gemini-2.5-Pro) while significantly reducing the false-positive rates that hinder real-time deployment. We make the code, data, and models publicly available at

Main category: cs.RO, eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出 REIS 框架，通过消除机器人推理中的时序冗余（连续观测产生相同动作/子目标），结合轻量场景门控、KV-steered affordance 路由和 deliberative reasoning，在 ALFRED 和真实机器人任务上显著降低推理延迟，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型和视觉语言模型的推理型机器人策略虽然具备强大的语义规划能力，但推理延迟过高，难以满足实时部署需求。作者观察到机器人推理工作负载中存在大量时序冗余——连续观测帧经常产生相同的动作和子目标，因此希望利用这一特性来减少不必要的推理。

Method: REIS 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 轻量场景门控（lightweight scene gating），快速判断当前观测是否需要重新推理；(2) KV-steered affordance 路由，利用 KV 缓存引导 affordance 预测，避免重复计算；(3) deliberative reasoning，仅在必要时触发深度推理。三者协同工作，在保持语义适应性的前提下最小化推理开销。

Result: 在 ALFRED 基准和真实机器人任务上的实验表明，REIS 显著抑制了推理开销，在保持竞争性任务性能的同时大幅降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: REIS 验证了利用时序冗余来加速具身机器人推理的有效性，为实时机器人部署提供了一种轻量高效的决策框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向具身机器人决策的推理加速框架 REIS，结合场景门控、KV 引导的 affordance 路由和 deliberative reasoning 来减少推理冗余，属于具身智能中的规划与控制，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Addresses inference efficiency for embodied world models, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: on-device robotic planning, inference redundancy elimination, KV-steered affordance routing, ALFRED benchmark, real-world robot tasks

Abstract: Reasoning-based robotic policies using large language and vision-language models achieve strong semantic planning capabilities but mostly suffer from a high inference latency that limits practical real-time deployment. In this work, we observe that robotic reasoning workloads contain substantial temporal redundancy, where consecutive observations frequently produce identical actions and subgoals. Based on this insight, we present REIS, a human cognition inspired robotic decision-making framework that minimizes unnecessary reasoning while preserving semantic adaptability. REIS combines lightweight scene gating, KV-steered affordance routing, and deliberative reasoning to accelerate robotic control under embodied constraints. Experiments on ALFRED, and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that REIS significantly suppresses reasoning overhead while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的端到端自动驾驶方法虽然能预测未来场景演化，但预测结果与可执行的轨迹规划之间耦合较弱，未来推理往往只是描述性的，难以直接转化为规划相关的运动生成。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和NAVSIM v2基准上，IDOL在同类方法中达到了最先进的性能。

Classification reason: 该论文提出IDOL框架，在BEV潜空间中利用逆动力学模型将未来预测与轨迹规划紧密结合，属于面向自动驾驶（具身智能）规划控制的世界模型方法，因此映射到[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong world model contribution for autonomous driving but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: world model, BEV latent space, inverse dynamics, end-to-end planning, autonomous driving

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has emerged as a compelling paradigm for learning planning directly from sensor observations, while recent world-model-based approaches further enrich this paradigm by enabling explicit reasoning about how the scene may evolve in the future. Yet future prediction alone does not guarantee better planning unless the predicted evolution can be converted into planning-relevant trajectory updates. Many current methods still forecast future scene states without explicitly decoding the motion implications hidden in state transitions. As a result, future reasoning often remains descriptively useful but only weakly coupled to executable motion generation. To address this limitation, we propose \mathbf{IDOL}, an inverse-dynamics-guided future prediction framework for world-model-based end-to-end planning in latent BEV space, where inverse dynamics serves as the key bridge between future prediction and trajectory optimization. IDOL first predicts multiple future latent scene states with a BEV world model, then applies an inverse dynamics model to adjacent latent futures to decode transition-aware trajectory features and recover planning-relevant motion deltas that explain how the latent world evolves over time. These inverse-dynamics-derived signals are used to optimize the planned trajectory, turning future forecasting from passive scene anticipation into actionable planning guidance. A lightweight closed-loop refinement module further improves long-horizon consistency by reusing the optimized trajectory for another round of future-aware reasoning. By introducing inverse dynamics into latent future reasoning, IDOL tightens the coupling between world modeling and planning. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM v1 and NAVSIM v2 benchmarks show that IDOL achieves state-of-the-art performance among comparable methods.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体在导航到目标物体附近后，无法确认是否找到了正确的实例——细微的属性差异（如"白色花卉"vs"白色条纹"）需要近距离多视角检查。现有基准缺乏对细粒度属性验证能力的评估。

Method: 形式化AIV为有限时域决策过程；构建PInVerify基准（3000个episode、18类物体、6扇区导航拓扑，含陷阱视图和不可达扇区）；设计无训练流水线（属性分解+可见性加权多视图追踪+多种NBV策略）和LoRA微调端到端智能体（SFT+GSPO）。

Result: 最佳MLLM基线（Qwen3-VL-8B）超过最佳嵌入基线（SigLIP2）4.9个百分点；GT-box消融显示+3.1个百分点的检测差距；主动视角选择策略未带来可靠增益；LoRA微调智能体（SFT+GSPO）达到85.6%。

Conclusion: PInVerify为具身AI中的主动细粒度语义验证提供了标准化基准，当前MLLM基线优于嵌入基线，但主动视角选择仍需进一步研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Active Instance Verification (AIV)任务，构建PInVerify基准，让具身智能体主动选择视角验证目标物体是否匹配细粒度描述。这属于具身智能体在环境中进行主动感知、决策与验证，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on embodied instance verification, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Embodied AI benchmark, Active Instance Verification, MLLM baselines, No reliable gain from active viewpoint selection, LoRA fine-tuning reaches 85.6%

Abstract: Embodied agents have made strong progress in navigating to target objects, but reaching the goal vicinity does not guarantee that the agent has found the correct instance: subtle attribute differences (e.g., "white floral" vs. "white striped") often require close-range, multi-view inspection. We address this gap with Active Instance Verification (AIV), a task in which an agent actively selects viewpoints around a candidate object to decide whether it matches a fine-grained natural-language description. We formalize AIV as a finite-horizon decision process and introduce PInVerify, an offline embodied benchmark for AIV: 3,000 evaluation episodes across 18 object categories, delivered as multi-view captures with a 6-sector navigation topology that exposes trap views (navigable but uninformative) and unreachable sectors. As reference baselines we build a training-free pipeline and a LoRA-fine-tuned end-to-end agent around open-source multimodal large language models (MLLMs) at on-device scale ($\leq$8B parameters), with attribute decomposition, a visibility-weighted multi-view tracker, and three next-best-view (NBV) strategies. In our evaluation across Qwen3-VL (4B/8B), SenseNova-SI-1.2-InternVL3-8B, CLIP, and SigLIP2, the best MLLM-based baseline exceeds the best embedding baseline by 4.9 pp; GT-box ablations show a +3.1 pp detection gap; and we do not observe reliable gains from active viewpoint selection within the tested NBV strategies. A LoRA-fine-tuned agent (SFT+GSPO) reaches 85.6%. PInVerify aims to support further work on active, fine-grained semantic verification in embodied AI. Code: .

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际强化学习环境中，观测往往远高于控制所需变量的维度。作者希望学习仅捕捉控制相关特征的表征，从而提升样本效率和泛化能力。他们通过 empowerment 目标来研究这一问题，因为 empowerment 最大化智能体对环境的控制力，天然倾向于忽略控制无关的信息。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了 empowerment 最大化框架下智能体学习到的表征结构。他们证明 empowerment 目标会诱导出两种表征：前向表征（forward representation）和后向表征（backward representation），分别捕捉状态中与控制和可预测性相关的互补方面。通过理论分析，他们证明这两种表征对控制无关的特征具有不变性，从而隐式地构建了一个以控制为中心的世界模型。

Result: 理论分析表明 empowerment 最大化能够学习到对控制无关特征不变的表征，且前向和后向表征捕捉了状态的不同方面。论文强调了交互式学习（而非被动数据集）对于学习这种有用不变性的关键作用，这一观点与因果学习文献高度一致。

Conclusion: empowerment 最大化是一种有效的控制相关表征学习框架，它通过交互隐式地构建了以控制为中心的世界模型。这项工作为理解强化学习中表征学习与控制的深层关系提供了理论视角，并强调了主动交互在表征学习中的核心地位。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过 empowerment 最大化（一种控制目标）来学习仅包含控制相关特征的表征，并指出这种交互式学习隐式地构建了一个以控制为中心的世界模型。这与世界模型方向下的"具身世界模型与规划控制"（embodied_world_model）高度契合，因为该子主题关注通过交互和动作学习环境模型，且论文强调通过交互（而非被动数据）学习控制相关的表征，与具身智能中的规划控制紧密相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model learning through control-centric representation, aligning with user's high-priority world_model direction and continuous multimodal reasoning. Signals: world model, empowerment, control-centric representation, forward/backward representations, interaction-based learning

Abstract: In many practical reinforcement learning environments, observations are far higher-dimensional than the variables that matter for control. In this work, we ask: can we learn representations that capture only control-relevant features of the environment? We study this question through the empowerment objective, which maximizes an agent's influence over the environment and is widely used for unsupervised skill learning. We show that empowerment agents induce two distinct representations -- forward and backward -- that capture complementary aspects of the state, and both of which are invariant to control-irrelevant features. Thus, empowerment maximization leads agents to learn an implicit, control-centric model of the world. Our analysis highlights the importance of learning representations through interaction rather than from passive datasets: interaction aimed at maximizing control is essential for learning useful invariance properties, a perspective that aligns closely with the causal learning literature.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型（Driving VLA）试图利用语言来改进端到端规划，但语言与动作之间存在鸿沟（language-action gap），导致语言输出难以直接指导或约束实际的轨迹规划。作者希望弥合这一差距，使语言决策可验证、可对齐，从而提升规划性能。

Method: DriveMA 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 元动作（meta-actions）——将未来自车运动总结为紧凑的语言域意图（如"左转"、"减速"等），通过轨迹标注管道从专家轨迹中自动构建，并可通过规则投影对生成的轨迹进行验证；(2) 动作中心监督训练——利用元动作的可验证性进行监督学习；(3) 回合级信用分配强化学习——通过密集奖励和精确信用分配，显式对齐高层语言决策与底层轨迹规划。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Dataset 基于视觉的端到端驾驶任务上，DriveMA 2B 模型取得了 8.060 的 Rater Feedback Score，4B 模型进一步提升至 8.079，均达到 SOTA。在 NAVSIM 上同样获得了有竞争力的闭环规划性能。

Conclusion: 即使是一个简单的元动作接口，只要使其可验证并针对语言-动作对齐进行优化，也能在驾驶规划任务上取得 SOTA 性能。这表明弥合语言-动作鸿沟是提升 Driving VLA 性能的关键方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DriveMA，一种驾驶视觉-语言-动作模型（Driving VLA），通过可验证的元动作（meta-actions）将高层语言决策与底层轨迹规划对齐，并采用强化学习进行规划控制。这属于具身智能中的自动驾驶规划与控制任务，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model and planning alignment, but lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, driving VLA, meta-actions, language-action alignment, reinforcement learning

Abstract: Driving Vision-Language-Action Models (Driving VLAs) aim to use language to improve end-to-end planning, but the language-action gap limits this promise. We propose DriveMA, a Driving VLA framework built on verifiable meta-actions, which summarize future ego motion into compact language-domain intentions and can be constructed from expert trajectories with a trajectory-grounded annotation pipeline and can be verified against generated trajectories through rule-based projection. DriveMA exploits this verifiability with action-centric supervised training and a data-efficient turn-level credit assignment reinforcement learning framework, explicitly aligning high-level decisions with low-level trajectory planning through dense rewards and precise credit assignment. DriveMA sets a new state of the art on the Waymo Open Dataset Vision-based E2E Driving, achieving a Rater Feedback Score of 8.060 with a 2B model and further improving it to 8.079 with a 4B model; it also obtains competitive closed-loop planning performance on NAVSIM. These results show that even a simple meta-action interface can achieve state-of-the-art planning when made verifiable and optimized for language-action alignment. Code, data, and models will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一种架构，将 Dreamer 风格的循环状态空间模型（RSSM）的潜状态分解为环境组件和队友组件，并学习一个辅助的 Theory-of-Mind（ToM）头，从部分轨迹中推断队友行为的潜嵌入（如角色、意图和预测动作）。这些队友潜变量用于条件化 actor 和 critic，使智能体能够想象并适应不同的协作伙伴。

Result: 论文提出了方法架构、基准测试和评估协议的设计方案，但尚未给出具体的实验数值结果，属于方法框架与理论提案阶段。

Conclusion: 该工作将世界模型定位为不仅是环境动力学的预测器，更是社会行为的模拟器，为可泛化、与人类兼容的 AI 开辟了新方向，有望支持部分可观测环境中的零样本与小样本协调。

Classification reason: 该论文将 Dreamer 风格的世界模型扩展到多智能体强化学习场景，将队友建模为世界模型中的结构化可学习组件，属于世界模型在具身智能与多智能体规划控制中的应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Extends world models to multi-agent social reasoning, but focuses on MARL rather than the user's priority of continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world model, multi-agent reinforcement learning, theory-of-mind, latent teammate modeling, Dreamer

Abstract: In cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), agents must coordinate with partners whose internal policies and intentions are not directly observable. While world models such as Dreamer have demonstrated strong generalization and sample efficiency in single-agent settings, their application to MARL remains limited by an inability to handle teammate-induced uncertainty. We propose a new perspective: treat teammates as structured, learnable components within the agent's world model. We introduce an architecture that factorizes the latent state of a Dreamer-style recurrent state-space model (RSSM) into environment and teammate components, and learns an auxiliary Theory-of-Mind (ToM) head to infer latent embeddings of partner behavior such as character, intent, and predicted actions from partial trajectories. These teammate latents condition the actor and critic, enabling the agent to imagine and adapt to diverse collaborators. We outline how this approach can support zero-shot and few-shot coordination in partially observable settings and propose a set of benchmarks and evaluation protocols to assess its impact. This work positions world models as not only predictors of environmental dynamics, but as simulators of social behavior, opening new directions for generalizable, human-compatible AI.

</details>

### [22] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe, Stepan Tretiakov, Cheng-Hsi Hsiao, Su Ann Low, Xingjian Li, Hassan Iqbal, Neel P. Bhatt, Ufuk Topcu, Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有观测预测型世界模型在具身AI中会产生视觉合理但物理错误的预测，因为不同物理系统可能视觉相同但在干预下表现不同。作者提出物理可行的世界模型应识别回答干预查询所需的最简物理抽象，由模块化组件（环境表征、潜状态估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询响应）组成，并由自主编排器动态组装。该方法在现有系统失败的查询上展示了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要关注预测未来观测（如视频帧预测），但视觉上合理的预测可能在物理上完全错误——不同物理系统可能看起来相同但在干预下行为迥异。这导致模型可能推荐不可行的动作、错误预测交互结果或认证不安全行为。因此需要一种新的世界模型设计原则，确保模型能正确回答干预查询。

Method: 提出物理可行世界模型的设计框架：模型应识别回答特定干预查询所需的最简物理抽象，包含模块化组件（环境表征、潜状态与参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询级响应），并由自主编排器根据查询动态组装兼容的学习组件和结构化组件。当封闭形式物理不可用或不确定时，转移模型可以是解析的、模拟的、学习的或混合的，但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 在现有系统无法正确回答的干预查询上展示了该方法的有效性，证明了物理可行世界模型相比纯观测预测模型在回答干预查询时的优势。

Conclusion: 正确的抽象不是最详细的世界模型，而是保留与查询相关区分的最简模型。该分解使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计，并为新世界模型的设计和现有模型的可行性测试提供了原则。

Classification reason: 论文核心论点是世界模型必须具有物理可行性（physically viable），能够回答干预查询（intervention queries），并强调物理结构建模而非仅预测观测。这与 [CANONICAL] 可交互与物理世界建模 高度吻合，该子主题关注物理一致的世界模型、交互式模拟器与环境动力学建模。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model physical viability for embodied AI, aligning with top-priority world_model direction and video generation world model subtopic. Signals: world model, physical viability, intervention query, embodied AI, modular components

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>


### [23] [PatchWorld: Gradient-Free Optimization of Executable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30880)
*Jiaxin Bai, Yue Guo, Yifei Dong, Jiaxuan Xiong, Tianshi Zheng, Yixia Li, Tianqing Fang, Yufei Li, Yisen Gao, Haoyu Huang, Zhongwei Xie, Hong Ting Tsang, Zihao Wang, Lihui Liu, Jeff Pan, Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PatchWorld 是一个无梯度的框架，通过反例引导的代码修复，将离线轨迹转化为可执行的 Python 世界模型（belief-state programs），用于部分可观测环境下的预测与规划。在 AgentGym 环境中，PatchWorld-Simple 无需 LLM 调用即可达到 76.4% 的宏平均成功率，并揭示了可执行世界模型中观测保真度与决策效用之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本智能体环境通常建模为 POMDP，但智能体无法访问模拟器的隐状态和转移动力学。作者希望探索能否从离线轨迹中诱导出可执行的代码作为世界模型，用于部分可观测条件下的预测与规划，从而替代黑盒预测模型。

Method: PatchWorld 是一个无梯度框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 从离线轨迹中诱导出可执行的 Python 信念状态程序（belief-state programs），该程序维护一个显式的信念状态并定义动作更新规则；(2) 通过反例引导的代码修复（counterexample-guided code repair）迭代改进程序，使其与观测数据一致。PatchWorld-Simple 是简化版本，使用更直接的信念状态表示。

Result: 在 7 个 AgentGym 环境中，PatchWorld-Simple 在基于代码的规划评分中达到最高，宏平均成功率为 76.4%，且世界模型预测模块内部不调用任何 LLM。实验还发现，人工指定的残差记忆偏置（residual-memory bias）能提高表面观测保真度，但会削弱决策效用，揭示了可执行世界模型中观测保真度与动作判别动力学之间的权衡。

Conclusion: PatchWorld 证明了从离线轨迹诱导可执行世界模型的可行性，为部分可观测环境下的可解释、可检查的规划提供了新范式。同时揭示了可执行世界模型中观测保真度与决策效用之间的内在权衡，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PatchWorld，通过离线轨迹诱导可执行的 Python 世界模型，用于部分可观测环境下的预测与规划。这属于世界模型方向中的可交互与物理世界建模（interactive_physical_world），因为其核心是构建可执行、可检查、可修补的符号化世界模型（belief-state programs），属于环境动力学建模与可交互模拟器范畴。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model research with a novel gradient-free, executable code approach, aligning with top user priority. Signals: world model, executable code, gradient-free, counterexample-guided repair, belief-state programs

Abstract: Text-agent environments are typically modeled as partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs), assuming that the simulator's latent state and transition dynamics are hidden from the agent. Yet little work has examined whether executable code can be induced to serve as a world model for prediction and planning under partial observability. We introduce PatchWorld, a gradient-free framework that turns offline trajectories into executable Python world models through counterexample-guided code repair. Instead of predicting the next observation with a black-box model, PatchWorld induces symbolic belief-state programs whose action updates can be inspected, replayed, and locally patched. Across seven AgentGym environments, PatchWorld-Simple achieves the highest code-based planning score among evaluated methods, reaching 76.4\% macro success in live one-step lookahead while invoking no LLM calls inside the world-model prediction module itself. We further find that a human-specified residual-memory bias improves surface observation fidelity but weakens decision utility. This exposes a tradeoff in executable world models, since improving observation fidelity can come at the expense of action-discriminative dynamics, and vice versa. Code is available at .

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型在实时游戏模拟、虚拟场景导航和具身AI训练中具有重要应用，但长交互轨迹的推理成本极高，主要瓶颈包括上下文记忆增长、二次注意力复杂度和重复去噪步骤。现有方法缺乏针对交互式场景中轨迹依赖特性的优化。

Method: 提出Light Interaction框架，核心洞察是交互过程天然支持轨迹依赖的自适应计算：(1) 自适应上下文管理——探索新区域时丢弃检索到的空间记忆，根据局部潜动态调整时间上下文；(2) 去噪缓存加速——当相机回到熟悉区域时复用早期步骤的模型输出；(3) 硬件-软件协同设计的3D块稀疏注意力，使用融合Triton内核实现高效计算。

Result: 在HY-WorldPlay和Matrix-Game-3.0两个基准上，Light Interaction实现最高2.59倍加速，无需模型重训练，同时保持有竞争力的视觉质量。

Conclusion: Light Interaction证明了交互式视频世界模型可以通过利用交互轨迹的特性（探索/回访、局部动态变化）实现显著的无训练推理加速，为实时交互式世界模型部署提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出交互式视频世界模型的推理加速框架，核心是用户控制的相机运动驱动的交互式世界模拟，属于可交互世界建模与神经仿真器范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for interactive video world models, a high-priority subtopic, with a training-free acceleration method. Signals: world model, efficient inference, video generation, training-free acceleration, interactive world model

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate video chunk by chunk in response to user-controlled camera movements, enabling applications such as real-time game simulation, virtual scene navigation, and embodied AI training. However, scaling to long interactive trajectories is prohibitively expensive due to growing context memory, quadratic attention complexity, and repeated denoising steps. We present Light Interaction, a training-free inference acceleration framework for interactive video world models. Our key insight is that interaction naturally enables trajectory-dependent adaptive computation: retrieved spatial memory can be discarded during novel exploration, temporal context can be adjusted according to local latent dynamics, and early-step model outputs can be reused when the camera revisits familiar regions. Based on this insight, Light Interaction combines adaptive context management, denoising cache acceleration, and hardware-software co-designed 3D block sparse attention with fused Triton kernels. Evaluated on HY-WorldPlay and Matrix-Game-3.0, Light Interaction achieves up to 2.59x speedup without model retraining while maintaining competitive visual quality.

</details>

*Yifei Yang, Zehua Fan, Huan Li, Aoqi Wang, Lida Huang, Haibao Yu, Haiyan Liu, Xuanyao Mao, Jason Bao, Liang Xu, Bingchuan Sun, Yan Wang*

TL;DR: AR Forcing 提出一种自回归训练策略，将扩散损失融入自回归训练循环中，使模型在训练时暴露于推理状态分布，从而缓解扩散导航世界模型在长时域预测中的训练-推理分布偏移问题，提升了多域导航任务中生成图像的一致性和轨迹预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的机器人导航世界模型通常使用并行监督训练，但在路径规划时采用自回归推理，导致训练与推理之间存在分布偏移，长时域预测性能不稳定。现有方法需要额外的判别器或分布匹配损失，增加了复杂度。

Method: 提出 AR Forcing 自回归训练策略：在每一步训练中，模型使用自身预测结果更新上下文，并优化单步噪声预测目标（标准扩散损失），从而显式地将模型暴露于推理状态分布。该方法不改变原始扩散框架和采样器，无需额外判别器或分布匹配损失。

Result: 在 RECON、SCAND、HuRoN、TartanDrive 等多个域导航数据集上，AR Forcing 相比强基线方法，在长时域导航中生成图像的一致性显著提升，预测轨迹的准确性更高，在复杂已知和未知环境中增强了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AR Forcing 通过简单的自回归训练策略有效解决了扩散世界模型的训练-推理分布偏移问题，提升了长时域导航预测的稳定性和准确性，且易于集成到现有扩散框架中。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AR Forcing，一种面向长时域机器人导航的自回归训练策略，用于扩散世界模型。核心是动作条件视频预测（导航轨迹图像序列生成），属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）范畴，因此映射到该 canonical subtopic。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model and autoregressive video generation for robotics, aligning with top priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: world model, autoregressive training, video generation, diffusion, long-horizon prediction

Abstract: The diffusion based robot navigation world models are typically trained using parallel supervision, while autoregressive inference is employed during path planning. This results in a distribution shift between training and inference, which destabilizes the performance over long-horizon prediction. We propose AR Forcing, an autoregressive training strategy, which integrates the standard diffusion loss into the autoregressive training loop. At each step, the model uses its own predictions to update the context and optimize the single step noise prediction objective, thereby explicitly exposing the model to the inference state distribution during training. Our method does not require additional discriminators or distribution-matching losses, retains the original diffusion framework and sampler, and is easy to integrate. Experiments on multi-domain navigation datasets (RECON, SCAND, HuRoN, TartanDrive) show that compared with strong baselines, AR Forcing improved the consistency of generated images during long-horizon navigation and the accuracy of predicted trajectories, enhancing robustness of the model in complex known and unknown environments. We will release the code soon.

</details>


### [26] [Robust Dreamer: Deviation-Aware Latent Gaussian Memory for Action-Controlled AR Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30855)
*Hanlin Chen, Jiaxin Wei, Xibin Song, Yifu Wang, Steve Wang, Hongdong Li, Pan Ji, Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: Robust Dreamer 提出了一种记忆增强的动作控制自回归视频生成框架，通过 Latent Gaussian Memory（潜高斯记忆）避免 Latent-RGB 循环导致的退化，并利用 Deviation Learning（偏差学习）与动态偏差存档来弥合训练-推理差距，在长时序交互式世界模拟中实现了 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作控制图像到视频生成方法在长自回归 rollout 中面临严重的视觉退化与 3D 不一致问题，主要源于两个障碍：(1) Latent-RGB Cycling——生成的潜变量反复解码为 RGB 再重新编码用于后续条件化，导致信息损失累积；(2) 训练-推理差距——训练时使用干净的记忆，而推理时记忆被预测噪声污染，模型无法适应。

Method: 提出 Robust Dreamer 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Latent Gaussian Memory——将生成过程中继承的扩散潜变量锚定到高斯原语上，通过潜空间高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）进行召回，提供密集、几何感知、视角对齐的条件化信息，避免反复 VAE 转换带来的退化；(2) Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive——通过一步近似合成 rollout 导致的潜偏差，按自回归阶段和去噪时间戳存储，并在训练时注入历史记忆，使生成器暴露于真实的 corrupted 记忆状态，学会内部纠错。

Result: 在 ScanNet、DL3DV 和 OmniWorldGame 三个数据集上的实验表明，Robust Dreamer 在长时序动作控制视频生成任务上取得了 SOTA 性能，显著优于现有方法，在视觉保真度和 3D 一致性方面均有明显提升。

Conclusion: Robust Dreamer 通过潜高斯记忆和偏差学习有效解决了动作控制自回归视频生成中的累积退化与训练-推理差距问题，为交互式世界模拟提供了一种鲁棒且高效的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出动作控制的图像到视频生成框架用于交互式世界模拟，属于动作条件视频生成与长时序视频建模，与 [CANONICAL] video_world_model（视频世界模型）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances action-controlled autoregressive video generation for world models with a novel memory mechanism to prevent drift. Signals: autoregressive video generation, world model, action-controlled, latent Gaussian memory, deviation learning

Abstract: Frame-wise action-controlled image-to-video generation is a promising paradigm for interactive world simulation, where each control signal should elicit an immediate visual response. However, maintaining visual fidelity and 3D consistency over long autoregressive rollouts remains challenging. Existing 3D-aware methods often suffer from catastrophic drift due to two impediments: information loss from \textit{Latent--RGB Cycling}, where generated latents are repeatedly decoded to RGB and re-encoded for future conditioning, and the training--inference gap induced by the \textit{error-free hypothesis}, where clean training memory fails to match prediction-corrupted inference memory. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{Robust Dreamer}, a memory-augmented framework built around how to design 3D memory and how to use it robustly. First, we introduce \textbf{Latent Gaussian Memory}, which anchors diffusion latents inherited from the generation process to Gaussian primitives and recalls them via latent-space Gaussian splatting. This provides dense, geometry-aware, view-aligned conditioning while avoiding accumulated degradation from repeated VAE conversion. Second, we propose \textbf{Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive}, which synthesizes rollout-induced latent deviations through a one-step approximation, stores them by autoregressive stage and denoising timestamp, and injects them into historical memory during training. This exposes the generator to realistic corrupted memory states and teaches internal correction before inference. Experiments on ScanNet, DL3DV, and OmniWorldGame demonstrate state-of-the-art long-horizon performance.

</details>


### [27] [DecMem: Towards Minute-Long Consistent World Generation with Decoupled Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31336)
*Zhenhao Yang, Xiaoshi Wu, Zhengyao Lv, Xiaoyu Shi, Xintao Wang, Pengfei Wan, Kun Gai, Kwan-Yee K. Wong*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控世界模型在长时序推理下难以保持细粒度的时空一致性，显式3D记忆和粗粒度帧级隐式建模均存在局限，朴素可学习记忆架构在长程外推中面临计算效率低下和注意力分散两大问题。

Method: 提出 DecMem 解耦记忆架构：1）Sparse Global Memory（稀疏全局记忆）通过稀疏注意力机制高效访问全局历史信息；2）Anchored Local Memory（锚定局部记忆）提供稳定的局部上下文用于高质量外推。两者解耦设计避免了注意力分散并提升了计算效率。

Result: 实验表明 DecMem 在长视频生成任务上显著优于当前最先进方法，能够实现分钟级可控长视频生成，在保真度和时空一致性上均取得领先性能。

Conclusion: DecMem 通过解耦记忆架构有效解决了长视频世界生成中的长期记忆与高效外推问题，为分钟级一致可控世界生成提供了可行的技术路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DecMem，一种解耦记忆架构用于分钟级可控长视频生成，核心是保持长时序下的时空一致性，属于动作条件视频生成与长时序视频建模，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型 (video_world_model) 高度匹配。同时论文也涉及视频生成技术，因此也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses long-horizon world model consistency with a novel decoupled memory architecture, highly relevant to your priority on video world models and continuous multimodal reasoning. Signals: world model, video generation, long video consistency, decoupled memory, attention dispersion

Abstract: Recent advances in video generative models have promoted rapid progress in controllable world models. However, maintaining fine-grained spatio-temporal consistency under long-horizon reasoning remains a key challenge. In this work, we move beyond explicit 3D memory and coarse frame-level implicit modeling, and propose a fine-grained, learnable, and scalable memory for consistent world generation. We first identify two fundamental limitations of naïve learnable memory architectures in long-horizon extrapolation, namely computational inefficiency and attention dispersion. Through a systematic analysis of attention dispersion, we propose DecMem, a decoupled memory architecture that employs Sparse Global Memory for efficient fine-grained access to global history and Anchored Local Memory for stable and high-quality extrapolation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecMem significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art methods. By ensuring precise and efficient long-term memory and achieving superior extrapolation capabilities, DecMem enables minute-level controllable long video generation with high fidelity and consistency.

</details>


### [28] [Subspace-Decomposed JEPAs: Disentangling Progression and Content in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31111)
*Lucas Thil, Jesse Read, Rim Kaddah, Guillaume Doquet*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SD-JEPA，将JEPA潜空间分解为正交的进度子空间（低维，通过余弦间隔三元组损失塑造）和内容子空间（高维，由SIGReg正则化）。证明两种抗坍缩力作用于不相交坐标，可加性组合。在控制基准上优于LeWM基线，1D角度进度坐标可作为场景感知指南针，在回溯、扰动时表现出语义合理的重定位，在40个held-out立方体片段上定位语义事件的AUROC提升达+0.18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA模型学习紧凑的潜世界模型，但潜空间中没有任何单一坐标被指定用于编码任务进度。这限制了潜空间的可解释性和对任务进展的感知能力。作者希望将潜空间分解为进度和内容两个正交子空间，使模型既能感知任务进度，又能保持内容表征的丰富性。

Method: 将JEPA潜空间分解为两个正交子空间：(1) 低维进度子空间，通过余弦间隔三元组损失（cosine-margin triplet loss）塑造，使潜变量按任务进度有序排列；(2) 高维内容子空间，由LeWM中的SIGReg目标正则化。证明两种抗坍缩力作用于不相交坐标，可加性组合。在多个控制基准上评估，并设计了子空间消融验证器来确认分解的有效性。

Result: SD-JEPA在大多数控制基准上以相同计算量优于LeWM基线，在Push-T任务上优于最强的非LeWM JEPA基线。1D角度进度坐标可作为场景感知指南针：随任务进度前进，在回溯时回归，在受控扰动下尖峰并重定位到语义合适的新任务阶段。在40个held-out立方体片段上，|Δθ_t|在定位语义事件方面比标准潜预测误差的AUROC高出+0.18（±1步容差下每片段胜率97.5%）。跨4个环境的线性探针显示8维进度子空间（占潜空间的4.2%）解释了72-95%的任务进度方差。

Conclusion: SD-JEPA通过将JEPA潜空间分解为正交的进度和内容子空间，实现了可解释的潜世界模型。进度子空间不仅提升了控制性能，还提供了超越传统预测误差的语义事件感知能力。该方法证明了在潜空间中显式分离任务进度与内容表征的有效性，为构建更具可解释性和可控性的世界模型提供了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出SD-JEPA，一种在潜空间中解耦任务进度与内容的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），属于世界模型中的潜空间世界建模与未来预测。虽然论文侧重潜空间分解而非视频生成，但其核心是学习潜世界模型用于控制与规划，与[视频世界模型]子主题（关注世界模型、动作条件预测）最为匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous latent representation research with a principled subspace decomposition that improves control and provides interpretable progression signals. Signals: world model, JEPA latent decomposition, progression subspace, cosine-margin triplet loss, SIGReg

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn compact latent world models by predicting future embeddings, but no single coordinate of the latent is designated to encode task progression. We carve the JEPA latent into two orthogonal subspaces with disjoint roles: a low-dimensional progression subspace shaped by a cosine-margin triplet loss, and a high-dimensional content subspace regularised by the existing SIGReg objective of LeWM. We prove that the two anti-collapse forces act on disjoint coordinates, so they compose additively rather than competing on the same dimensions. Our method, SD-JEPA improves over the LeWM baseline on the majority of its control benchmarks at matched compute, and outperforms the strongest non-LeWM JEPA baseline on Push-T; a subspace-ablation falsifier confirms the split is the load-bearing ingredient. Beyond planning, the resulting 1-D angular progression coordinate functions as a scene-aware compass on the latent. It advances with task progress, regresses when the agent backtracks, and under controlled perturbations both spikes and relocalises to a semantically appropriate new task-phase sector, separating the moment of surprise from its meaning in a way that prediction-error scalars cannot. Three quantitative tests back this up: $|\Delta\theta_t|$ outperforms the standard latent-prediction-error surprise at localising semantic events on 40 held-out cube episodes by up to +0.18 pooled AUROC (97.5% per-episode win rate at $\pm 1$-step tolerance); a within-episode linear probe across all four environments (40 episodes per env) shows the 8-dimensional progression subspace (4.2% of the latent) explains 72-95% of task-progress variance..

</details>

### [29] [Equivariant Latent Alignment via Flow Matching under Group Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30705)
*Sunghyun Kim, Jaehoon Hahm, Jeongwoo Shin, Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出 Residual Latent Flow，一种基于流匹配的框架，用于校正等变表示学习中潜空间的对齐偏差，从而提升在旋转群 SO(n) 下的新视角合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等变表示学习方法在构建潜空间时，往往存在潜对齐偏差（latent misalignment），即预期的群作用与实际潜空间中所需的变换之间存在不一致，导致学到的潜表示无法一致地保持群对称性所施加的等变关系，影响新视角合成的质量。

Method: 提出 Residual Latent Flow，利用流匹配（flow matching）框架学习一个残差流，对存在对齐偏差的潜表示进行校正，使其更好地满足底层群对称性（如旋转群 SO(n)）的等变关系。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著减少了潜对齐偏差，并在旋转群 SO(n) 下的新视角合成任务中取得了更优的视觉质量和一致性。

Conclusion: Residual Latent Flow 通过流匹配有效校正了等变潜空间中的对齐问题，为几何感知生成模型和等变表示学习提供了新的改进方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于流匹配的残差潜流（Residual Latent Flow）来校正等变潜空间中的对齐问题，提升新视角合成质量。核心应用是 novel view synthesis（新视角合成），属于 3D 生成与重建范畴，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。同时论文涉及连续潜空间中的 flow matching 等变对齐，与连续语言多模态方向下的 "连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流" 也有一定关联。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D equivariant latent alignment for novel view synthesis, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's key interests like autoregressive video/image generation or continuous language models. Signals: Equivariant representation learning, Flow matching for latent correction, Novel view synthesis, Group symmetry (SO(n)), Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Geometry-aware generative models and novel view synthesis approaches have shown strong potential in visual fidelity and consistency. In parallel, equivariant representation learning has emerged as a powerful framework for constructing latent spaces where analytically known group transformations could act directly, capturing geometric structure in data and enhancing both interpretability and generalization in novel view synthesis. However, we identify that existing approaches often suffer from latent misalignment, a discrepancy between the intended group action and the actually required transformations in the latent space. Consequently, the learned latents often fail to consistently preserve the equivariant relations imposed by the underlying group symmetry. To address this, we propose Residual Latent Flow, a flow-based framework that corrects the misaligned latents, thereby improving compliance with the underlying equivariance relation. Our comprehensive experiments show that our method significantly reduces latent misalignment and improves novel view synthesis quality, under rotation groups SO(n).

</details>

Result: 在30个专业室内设计案例上的实验表明，Function2Scene 生成的布局在满足功能需求方面显著优于最近的LLM-based场景合成基线方法，在成对比较中94.3%的情况下被人类评估者偏好。

Conclusion: Function2Scene 成功地将室内场景合成的范式从"放置合理的物体"转向"设计支持人类使用的空间"，通过功能规格驱动和迭代检查-修复机制，生成了更符合实际使用需求的3D室内布局。

Importance reason: Novel functional specification approach for 3D layout, but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: 3D indoor scene synthesis, functional design constraints, LLM-based iterative refinement, VLM visual assessment, tool-augmented check-and-repair

Abstract: Most text-driven 3D indoor scene synthesis methods generate rooms from object-centric prompts, asking what furniture should be placed rather than how the space is used. Yet in real interior design, a layout is judged by how well it supports its occupants, e.g., their activities and physical needs. We introduce Function2Scene, a framework for generating 3D indoor layouts from functional specifications, i.e., natural-language design briefs describing who will use a room and what they need to do there. Given such a specification, our system parses occupant personas and activities, derives a customized set of functional design constraints from a taxonomy of 17 criteria spanning spatial, ergonomic, activity, and environmental considerations, and uses these constraints to guide layout generation. Rather than relying on an LLM to directly produce a final scene, Function2Scene performs iterative evaluation and refinement through a tool-augmented check-and-repair loop, combining geometric measurements, LLM-based contextual reasoning, and VLM-based visual assessment. Experiments on 30 professionally written interior-design cases show that Function2Scene produces layouts that better satisfy functional requirements than recent LLM-based scene synthesis baselines, with our results preferred in 94.3% of pairwise comparisons. Our work reframes text-driven indoor scene synthesis from placing plausible objects to designing spaces that support human use.

</details>

Conclusion: Foundation VAE 可以作为可扩展的 CT 表征复用和忠实生成的实用接口，证明了自然图像预训练的 VAE 在医学 3D 任务中的巨大潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文使用 Foundation VAE（在自然图像/视频上预训练）进行 CT 三维重建、增强与生成，属于 3D 医学图像生成与重建范畴，与 canonical subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度相关。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) compress high resolution CT volumes into compact latents while preserving clinically relevant structure. However, training CT-specific VAEs from scratch or heavily fine-tuning them incurs substantial computational and engineering cost, and often degrades under heterogeneous scanners, protocols, and diseases. This paper makes a progressive stride toward training-free medical VAEs by leveraging a critical observation: a single Foundation VAE, pretrained at scale on natural images and videos, can serve as a unified interface for CT Reconstruction, Augmentation, and Generation. With both encoder and decoder frozen, the Foundation VAE reconstructs CT volumes with preserved anatomy while suppressing acquisition noise; training segmentation models on these reconstructions improves surface accuracy by 3.9% NSD on average for pancreatic tumor and lung tumor. Within the same Foundation VAE latent space, a conditional latent diffusion model achieves 3.9% lower average FVD with 36.2% higher CT CLIP score, and improves multi-disease generation faithfulness across 18 types by 2.76% AUC. These results demonstrate Foundation VAEs as a practical interface for scalable CT representation reuse and faithful CT generation. Our code and demo are available at .

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张RGB图像重建完整3D场景几何极具挑战性，尤其是推断被遮挡的隐藏结构。现有方法受限于逐射线约束或非结构化点云查询，难以恢复完整的场景几何。

Method: 提出VolFill框架：1）混合3D VAE将稀疏T-UDF网格压缩为紧凑潜表示；2）潜空间扩散Transformer（DiT）对该表示进行去噪以恢复完整场景；3）以几何基础模型（如DINOv2）提取的空间先验作为条件，增强鲁棒推理；4）支持直接表面提取和大规模占用查询。

Result: 在SCRREAM和NRGB-D数据集上的实验表明，VolFill在完整场景几何重建方面显著优于现有基线方法，能够有效恢复被遮挡区域的几何结构。

Conclusion: VolFill提供了一种结构化表示方法，支持直接表面提取和占用查询，为整体空间理解提供了稳健基础，是单视图3D场景重建的重要进展。

Classification reason: 该论文从单张RGB图像重建完整3D场景几何，属于3D场景生成与重建任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"（three_d_generation）高度匹配。同时其使用flow matching进行体积生成，与world_model方向也有一定关联。

Importance reason: Strong 3D reconstruction method using flow matching and diffusion transformer, but not directly aligned with top priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive video generation. Signals: volumetric flow matching, single-view 3D reconstruction, latent diffusion transformer, geometry foundation model priors, outperforms baselines on SCRREAM and NRGB-D

Abstract: Reconstructing the complete geometry of a scene from a single RGB image remains challenging - especially when inferring hidden structures where visual evidence is incomplete. We introduce VolFill, a generative framework that predicts the 3D structure of the complete scene rather than relying on traditional pixel-aligned regression. Our method utilizes a hybrid 3D VAE to compress sparse truncated unsigned distance function grids into a compact latent space, paired with a latent Diffusion Transformer that denoises this representation to recover the complete scene. We condition the generation on geometry foundation models, leveraging rich spatial priors for robust reasoning. Unlike existing methods limited by per-ray constraints or unstructured point-cloud queries, VolFill provides a structured representation that supports direct surface extraction and occupancy queries at scale. Extensive experiments on the SCRREAM and NRGB-D datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms current baselines, providing a robust foundation for holistic spatial understanding.

</details>
