TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人格（persona）的多元对齐评估框架，用合成认知画像替代单一评估函数，实现反映文化、人口和上下文多样性的生成式AI评测。研究发现这些模拟评估者在连续推理和随机扰动下会出现状态空间漂移和语义不一致，论证了需要嵌入动态调节机制来维持连贯的人格仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI的对齐评估主要依赖单一聚合基准，忽略了人类判断在文化、人口和上下文上的多样性。需要一种能够反映多元视角的评估框架。

Method: 提出状态空间约束仿真框架，用一组结构化的合成认知画像（persona）替代单一评估函数，在生成模型上实例化并维持这些评估者人格，进行多元视角的基准测试。通过序列推理和随机提示扰动分析评估者人格的稳定性。

Result: 现代生成架构能够以较高一致性实例化和维持评估人格，实现多元化的视角依赖评估。但分析发现，在序列推理和随机扰动下，人格连贯性会出现系统性退化，表现为状态空间漂移和语义不一致。

Conclusion: 静态对齐约束不足以维持长期稳健的评估行为，需要在生成系统中嵌入动态、可存活性驱动的调节机制来保持连贯的认知仿真。该工作为更自适应、更符合人类价值观的上下文敏感评估方法奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于人格（persona）的多元对齐评估框架，关注生成式AI的对齐与安全评测，属于多模态安全与对齐范畴。虽然论文不直接涉及多模态，但其讨论的对齐评估框架适用于多模态生成模型，且"多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）是现有CANONICAL subtopic中与对齐、安全评测最相关的条目。

Importance reason: Focuses on pluralistic alignment evaluation, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Persona-based evaluation, pluralistic alignment, generative AI evaluation, state-space drift, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Current alignment paradigms for generative artificial intelligence rely predominantly on monolithic benchmarking frameworks that reduce the plurality of human judgment to aggregated statistical baselines, thereby obscuring cultural, demographic, and contextual variability in evaluation. We introduce a state-space constrained emulation framework for AI evaluation that replaces singular assessment functions with a structured manifold of synthetic cognitive profiles representing diverse human perspectives. We show that modern generative architectures can instantiate and maintain these evaluative personas with high consistency, enabling a form of pluralistic, perspective-dependent benchmarking that more closely reflects real-world consensus variability. However, we further analyze the stability of these simulated evaluators under sequential inference and stochastic prompt perturbations, revealing systematic degradation in persona coherence that manifests as state-space drift and semantic inconsistency. These findings suggest that static alignment constraints are insufficient for sustaining robust evaluative behavior over time. Instead, we argue for the necessity of embedding dynamic, viability-driven regulatory mechanisms within generative systems to preserve coherent cognitive emulation. By framing persona-based evaluation as a structured dynamical system over latent representation manifolds, this study provides a foundation for more adaptive, human-aligned, and context-sensitive approaches to AI evaluation.

</details>


### [70] [Probing Collision Grounding in Vision-Language Models for Safe Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31196)
*Jun Wang, Xiaohao Xu, Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出TouchSafeBench，一个基于物理模拟的碰撞接地（collision grounding）评测基准，用于评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在人机协作场景中判断当前是否碰撞以及预警即将碰撞的能力。实验表明，当前最先进的VLM在该任务上平均Macro-F1低于50%，视觉流畅性不等于物理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全人机协作需要监控系统不仅能够描述场景，还能判断机器人是否与场景或人发生碰撞或即将碰撞。现有VLM缺乏对碰撞接地（将视觉观察与机器人几何、视角、场景布局、人体接近度和时序运动绑定）能力的系统评测。

Method: 基于Habitat 3.0构建TouchSafeBench，包含2,940个模拟室内共现场景（社交导航和社交重排），提供多视角RGB-D观测、俯视轨迹图、标定相机元数据和模拟器接触标签。定义两个任务：当前安全状态分类和即将碰撞预警。评测多个前沿/机器人VLM及九种视觉表征。

Result: 最佳模型平均Macro-F1低于50%；显式深度信息未被自动转化为机器人-身体碰撞证据；机器人-场景接触比人-机器人接触更难检测。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在碰撞接地任务上远不可靠，视觉流畅性不代表物理可问责性。可靠的机器人安全监控需要显式绑定视角、机器人形态、度量几何和未来碰撞的表征。

Classification reason: 论文关注VLM在机器人安全碰撞检测中的可靠性，属于多模态安全评测范畴，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）中的安全评测方向高度吻合。同时涉及具身场景中的世界建模，因此也匹配世界模型方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM safety for robotics, which is outside your priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous language models, video generation, or world models. Signals: collision grounding benchmark, VLM safety evaluation, low model performance, not aligned with priority subtopics, no autoregressive or diffusion generation

Abstract: Safe human--robot collaboration requires more than visual description: a monitor must determine whether the robot body is safely separated, already colliding with the scene or a person, or about to collide. We call this capability collision grounding: binding visual observations to robot body geometry, camera viewpoint, scene layout, human proximity, and temporal motion in order to infer present and imminent contact. We introduce TouchSafeBench, a physics-grounded benchmark for evaluating collision grounding in vision-language models (VLMs). Built in Habitat~3.0, TouchSafeBench contains 2,940 simulated indoor co-presence episodes across social navigation and social rearrangement, with synchronized multi-view RGB-D observations, top-down trajectory maps, calibrated camera metadata, and simulator-derived contact labels. We study two deployment-facing tasks: classifying the current safety state and warning about imminent collision before contact. Across three frontier or robotics-oriented VLMs and nine visual representations, current models remain far from reliable: the best average Macro-F1 stays below 50\%, explicit depth is not automatically transformed into robot-body collision evidence, and robot--scene contact is consistently harder than human-contact risk. TouchSafeBench reveals a central limitation of embodied VLMs: visual fluency does not imply physical accountability. Reliable robot safety monitors will need representations that explicitly bind viewpoint, robot morphology, metric geometry, and future collision. We will release the benchmark upon acceptance.

</details>


### [71] [Immuno-VLM: Immunizing Large Vision-Language Models via Generative Semantic Antibodies for Open-World Trustworthiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30745)
*Xiang Fang, Wanlong Fang, Wei Ji*

TL;DR: Immuno-VLM 受生物免疫负选择机制启发，利用大语言模型的生成推理能力主动生成"语义抗体"（近分布异常文本描述），在潜空间中约束已知类别的决策边界，从而解决VLM在开放世界中高置信度误判未知异常的问题，在ImageNet-1K和四个OOD基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在零样本识别中因缺乏显式负知识，会将未知异常强制拟合到已知类别并给出高置信度预测，导致开放世界部署中的可信赖性悖论。传统开放集识别方法依赖被动密度估计或像素空间异常生成，效率低且效果有限。

Method: 借鉴生物免疫负选择原理，利用LLM的生成推理能力主动"幻觉"出近分布异常（如相似物、上下文异常）的文本描述作为"语义抗体"，在VLM的高维潜空间中用这些抗体约束已知类别的决策边界，实现开放世界中的异常检测与可信分类。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和四个具有挑战性的OOD基准数据集上，Immuno-VLM取得了新的SOTA结果，显著优于传统开放集识别方法。

Conclusion: 通过将生物免疫负选择机制适配到高维潜空间，结合LLM的生成推理能力主动构建语义抗体，有效解决了VLM在开放世界中的语义傲慢问题，为多模态模型的可信赖部署提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文关注VLM在开放世界部署中的可信赖性问题，提出基于免疫负选择的框架来检测未知异常（开放集识别/OOD检测），属于多模态安全与防御范畴，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM safety via semantic antibodies, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous models or generation. Signals: Novel bio-inspired OOD detection, leverages LLM for generative outlier descriptions, addresses open-world trustworthiness, SOTA on OOD benchmarks, tangential to user's core interests

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models have achieved unprecedented success in zero-shot recognition by aligning visual features with broad semantic concepts. However, this semantic abstraction creates a critical vulnerability in open-world deployment: the ``Hubris of Semantics'', where models force-fit unknown anomalies into known categories with high confidence due to the lack of explicit negative knowledge. To address this \textit{Open-World Trustworthiness Paradox}, we propose \textbf{Immuno-VLM}, a bio-inspired framework that adapts the biological principle of \textbf{Immunological Negative Selection} to high-dimensional latent spaces. Departing from traditional Open-Set Recognition methods that rely on passive density estimation or inefficient pixel-space outlier generation, Immuno-VLM leverages the generative reasoning of Large Language Models to actively hallucinate ``Semantic Antibodies'', textual descriptions of near-distribution outliers (e.g., look-alikes, contextual anomalies) that effectively bound the decision space of known experiments on ImageNet-1K and four challenging OOD benchmarks reveal that Immuno-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [72] [Does Visual Information Play a Decisive Role in Vision-Language-Action Model Driving Behavior?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31041)
*Jingtao He, Hongliang Lu, Xiaoyun Qiu, Yixuan Wang, Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结构化的多层级视觉扰动框架，用于系统分析VLA（视觉-语言-动作）自动驾驶模型对视觉信息的依赖程度。框架沿通道级退化、信息级破坏和结构级修改三个维度组织受控视觉扰动，并在开环轨迹预测和交互式闭环安全评估下测试模型行为响应。实验发现评估方式不同会导致不同的依赖模式，且模型在不同抽象层级的视觉信息上存在不均匀的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶模型在自动驾驶中展现出潜力，但对其驾驶行为如何依赖于视觉信息缺乏深入理解。当前的评估协议主要关注聚合性能指标，缺乏结构化的诊断方法来量化视觉-行为依赖关系。

Method: 提出一个多层级视觉扰动框架，包含三个互补维度：通道级退化（如颜色通道扰动）、信息级破坏（如语义信息遮挡）和结构级修改（如空间结构变换）。将该框架应用于VLA驾驶系统，在开环轨迹预测和交互式闭环安全评估两种设置下评估行为响应。

Result: 实验揭示了评估方式依赖的依赖模式，以及模型在不同抽象层级的视觉信息上存在不均匀的视觉依赖。开环和闭环评估下模型对视觉扰动的敏感度不同，表明单一评估协议不足以全面刻画视觉-行为依赖关系。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁对VLA驾驶模型进行更结构化的分析和更原则性的设计，以更好地理解视觉信息如何塑造驾驶行为，从而开发更安全、更鲁棒的自动驾驶系统。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLA驾驶模型对视觉信息的依赖程度，通过多层级视觉扰动框架系统分析视觉-行为依赖关系，属于多模态安全与鲁棒性范畴。虽然不直接涉及越狱或对抗攻击，但其核心是对视觉扰动的行为响应分析，与多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）中对抗扰动、鲁棒性评测的主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Provides structured analysis of visual grounding in VLA driving models, but the subtopic is outside user's priority areas. Signals: VLA driving model, visual perturbation framework, open-loop/closed-loop evaluation, uneven visual grounding, safety analysis

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising capability in autonomous driving, highlighting the potential of unified multimodal architectures for jointly modeling perception and planning. However, how current VLA-based driving behavior is grounded in visual information remains poorly understood. Existing evaluation protocols mainly focus on aggregate performance metrics, lacking structured and practical diagnostics to quantify visual-behavior dependency. In this work, we introduce a structured multi-level visual perturbation framework to analyze visual-behavior dependency in VLA-based driving models systematically. The framework organizes controlled visual perturbations along three complementary dimensions: channellevel degradation, information-level disruption, and structurelevel modification. We apply it to VLA-based driving systems and evaluate behavioral responses under both open-loop trajectory prediction and interactive closed-loop safety evaluation. Experimental results reveal evaluation-dependent dependency patterns and uneven visual grounding across abstraction levels. These findings call for more structured analyses and principled design of VLA driving models to better understand how visual information shapes behavior and develop safer, more robust systems.

</details>


### [73] [Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31556)
*Arnau Marin-Llobet, Simon Henniger, Mahzarin R. Banaji*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.CY, cs.AI, cs.HC

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现，即使经过对齐训练，VLM在面对性别模糊的输入图像时仍会系统性偏向男性输出，且内部表征与输出之间存在解耦——模型内部编码了女性关联，但输出时被抑制。作者提出LALS指标用于逐层分析视觉token的语义关联，揭示了从中间层到输出层的"不对称过滤"机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法主要关注性别清晰可见的情况，但对实践中常见的模糊输入（如全副武装的工人、背影）中模型如何处理性别偏见缺乏研究。作者希望揭示VLM在模糊输入下的真实内部表征与输出行为之间的差异。

Method: 提出LALS（Latent Association Leaning Score），一种零样本度量方法，将视觉token激活投影到模型文本嵌入空间，逐token逐层测量概念关联。在15个职业、800+张性别模糊图像上对4种VLM进行实验，并进行颜色消融实验。

Result: 模型内部表征与输出系统性解耦：内部编码女性关联但输出男性。逐层分析发现不对称过滤——男性信号从输入到输出逐层增强，女性信号在中间层达到峰值后在被生成前被抑制。颜色消融表明服装颜色等文化负载视觉线索会进一步调节内部关联。

Conclusion: VLM的对齐训练在模糊输入下存在盲区，模型内部仍编码了被抑制的性别关联信息，这为多模态模型的安全对齐与公平性评估提出了新的挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在模糊输入下对女性表征的抑制问题，属于多模态模型的社会偏见与公平性安全议题，与[多模态安全与攻防]（multimodal_safety_attack）中涉及的对抗攻击、安全评测方向高度相关，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on gender bias in VLM outputs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: VLM gender bias, ambiguous input, internal-external decoupling, LALS metric, asymmetric filter

Abstract: Alignment teaches vision-language models (VLMs) to avoid expressing demographic biases, and when gender is clearly visible they largely succeed. Far less is known about ambiguous inputs (a worker in full gear, a figure seen from behind) cases common in practice yet rarely studied. We find that minimal prompting pressure exposes occupation-gender defaults when prompting ambiguous input images, with models collapsing to male even for strongly female-stereotyped occupations. But do these outputs reflect what models actually encode internally? We introduce LALS (Latent Association Leaning Score), a zero-shot metric that projects visual-token activations into the model's text-embedding space to measure concept associations per token and layer. Across 15 occupations, over 800 gender-ambiguous images, and four VLMs, internal representations and outputs are systematically decoupled: models often encode a female association internally yet output male. Layer-wise analysis reveals an asymmetric filter -- male signal amplifies end-to-end while female signal peaks mid-network and is suppressed before generation -- and a color ablation shows that culturally loaded visual cues such as clothing color further modulate these internal associations.

</details>

### [74] [What Makes LVLMs Hallucinate Less? Unveiling the Architectural Factors Behind Hallucination Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30911)
*Yusheng He, Jizhe Zhou, Xia Du, Zheng Lin, Jun Luo, Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统探究了LVLM架构设计（语言基础、视觉表征、语义对齐三个维度）对三种幻觉（共现、相似性、不确定性）的影响，提出了细粒度幻觉基准CoSimUE，发现模型参数规模对幻觉缓解效果有限，而联合增强视觉保真度与对齐质量最为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM幻觉问题严重制约其可靠性，现有工作多关注改进内部组件，但缺乏从架构设计层面系统理解幻觉成因的研究。

Method: 将架构设计分解为语言基础(LF)、视觉表征(VR)和语义对齐(SA)三个维度，将幻觉分为共现型、相似型和不确定型三类。提出CoSimUE基准，通过受控文本扰动和随机扰动构建细粒度幻觉场景，在7个设计方面进行实验映射。

Result: 1) 模型参数规模对三类幻觉缓解效果有限；2) 更大更好的语言基础可减少共现幻觉；3) 更强的视觉编码器和更高分辨率缓解相似性错误；4) 有效的对齐策略减轻不确定性幻觉；5) 联合增强视觉保真度与对齐质量效果最佳。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统建立了架构设计与幻觉鲁棒性之间的关联，为开发可靠高效的LVLM提供了实用指导。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究LVLM幻觉与架构设计的关系，属于多模态幻觉缓解方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性" 完全匹配。

Importance reason: Systematic study of LVLM hallucination robustness is valuable but not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: LVLM hallucination, architectural factors, CoSimUE benchmark, cross-dimensional analysis, limited scaling impact

Abstract: Hallucination remains one of the key challenges undermining the reliability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). But what makes an LVLM hallucinate less? Many existing efforts focus on improving internal components of the model. We argue that hallucination fundamentally stems from how the model architecture is designed. To investigate this, we factor the architecture design into three dimensions: Linguistic Foundation (LF), Visual Representation (VR), and Semantic Alignment (SA), and categorize hallucinations into Co-occurrence, Similarity, and previously overlooked Uncertainty types. Building on this formulation, we propose CoSimUE, a benchmark that creates fine-grained hallucination scenarios through controlled textual perturbations and random perturbations, enabling mapping between design choices and hallucination behaviors. Experiments across 7 design aspects show that: 1) the widely emphasized scaling of model parameters has only limited impact on reducing all three types of hallucinations; 2) larger and better-trained language foundations can reduce co-occurrence hallucinations; 3) stronger visual encoders and higher resolutions mitigate similarity errors; 4) effective alignment strategies alleviate uncertainty hallucinations. 5) Furthermore, cross-dimensional analysis reveals that jointly enhancing visual fidelity and alignment quality yields the most comprehensive improvements. This study provides the first systematic exploration linking architecture-level design to hallucination robustness, offering practical guidance for developing reliable and efficient LVLMs.

</details>


### [75] [BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31483)
*Shefayat E Shams Adib, Ahmed Alfey Sani, Ekramul Alam Esham, Ajwad Abrar, Ishmam Tashdeed, Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BenHalluEval 是首个针对孟加拉语大语言模型的细粒度幻觉评测框架，覆盖生成式问答、孟英混合问答、摘要和推理四个任务，构建了12,000个幻觉候选样本，并提出双轨校准指标 BenHalluScore，在7个LLM上评测发现模型间幻觉校准差异显著，思维链提示未能持续改善幻觉判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语作为全球第六大语言，尚无系统性的大语言模型幻觉评测工作。现有幻觉评测主要针对英语等资源丰富语言，低资源语言的幻觉问题缺乏关注。

Method: 构建覆盖四个任务（GQA、孟英混合QA、摘要、推理）的幻觉评测框架，利用GPT-5.4生成12,000个涵盖12种任务特定幻觉类型的候选样本。提出双轨评测协议：Track A在真实实例上测量假阳性率，Track B在幻觉候选上测量检测率。提出BenHalluScore双轨校准指标联合惩罚两种失败模式。评测7个LLM（推理型、多语言型、孟加拉语专用型），并测试思维链提示作为缓解策略的效果。

Result: BenHalluScore在模型和任务间范围为7.72%至55.42%，表明幻觉校准存在显著差异。思维链提示改变了响应分布，但未能持续改善幻觉判别能力。单轨评测和仅提示方法在低资源语言场景下效果不足。

Conclusion: BenHalluEval建立了首个孟加拉语专用幻觉基准，揭示了低资源语言场景下幻觉评测的独特挑战，并指出单轨评测和仅提示方法不足以有效评估和缓解幻觉。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了孟加拉语大语言模型的幻觉评测框架，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态幻觉与可靠性子主题。虽然论文聚焦于纯文本LLM而非多模态，但"多模态幻觉与可靠性"子主题涵盖幻觉评测、事实性与可靠性，且该子主题是[CANONICAL]条目，应优先复用。

Importance reason: Focuses on Bengali hallucination evaluation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks technical novelty in core areas. Signals: Low-resource language benchmark, hallucination evaluation, no continuous/multimodal model focus, no video/image generation, no world model

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at .

</details>

*Haolin Deng, Xin Zou, Zhiwei Jin, Chen Chen, Haonan Lu, Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO)，通过将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中，解决视觉偏好 DPO 中因配分函数不匹配导致的理论不一致问题；并引入 Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist) 正则化器以及对比样本编辑策略生成硬负样本，在五个基准上取得最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准文本 DPO 因缺乏显式视觉监督难以缓解多模态幻觉；现有视觉偏好 DPO 方法存在配分函数不匹配的理论缺陷，且依赖粗粒度负样本易导致捷径学习。

Method: 提出 IC-VCO 框架：(1) 将对比图像置于共享多图像上下文中，确保偏好优化目标的数学严谨性；(2) 引入 Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist)，一个可靠性门控的正则化项，促进多图像对比训练与单图像推理之间的一致性；(3) 设计对比样本编辑策略，通过精确语义扰动生成硬负样本。

Result: 在五个多模态幻觉评测基准上，IC-VCO 取得最佳综合性能，对比样本编辑策略的有效性也得到验证。

Conclusion: IC-VCO 通过理论严谨的视觉对比优化和硬负样本生成，有效缓解了多模态幻觉，为视觉偏好对齐提供了更可靠的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于缓解多模态幻觉（multimodal hallucination），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination），因此直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Addresses multimodal hallucination with a novel contrastive optimization, but falls outside the user's top priority subtopics. Signals: Multimodal hallucination, Visual preference DPO, In-context contrastive optimization, Hard negative generation, VLM reliability

Abstract: Multimodal hallucination remains a persistent challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Standard textual Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) often fails to mitigate it due to a lack of explicit visual supervision. While existing works introduce visual preference DPO by contrasting original images against negative ones, they suffer from a theoretically inconsistent objective caused by partition function mismatches and rely on coarse-grained negatives that could enable shortcut learning. In this work, we propose In-Context Visual Contrastive Optimization (IC-VCO). By placing contrastive images within a shared multi-image context, IC-VCO ensures a mathematically rigorous objective. We further introduce Visual Contrast Distillation (VCDist), an auxiliary reliability-gated regularizer that encourages consistency between multi-image contrastive training and single-image inference. Finally, we propose a contrastive sample editing strategy that generates hard negatives via precise semantic perturbations. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate IC-VCO's best overall performance and the effectiveness of our sample editing strategy. Code and data are available at .

</details>


### [77] [YARD: Y-Architecture Register Decoding for Efficient Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31429)
*Ting Chen, Geng Li, Guohao Chen, Yu Hu, Guan Huang, Mai Chen, Langsheng Lei, Jun Du*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: YARD提出了一种无需训练的Y-Architecture Register Decoding框架，通过在中间解码层分支并替换patch级视觉token为register token来构建视觉退化分支，从而提供可靠的对比解码信号以缓解LVLM幻觉，同时避免二次前向传播带来的高延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练自由的对比解码方法存在两个问题：完全丢弃视觉token过于极端，会引发语言幻觉；而破坏输入图像的方式对视觉证据的控制粗糙且需要两次完整前向传播，推理延迟高。需要一种更高效且有效的幻觉缓解方法。

Method: YARD基于"可靠的文本到视觉对齐主要出现在中间解码层"这一观察，在中间层进行分支：共享浅层计算，在关键阶段分支出退化分支。退化分支中将patch级视觉token替换为register token（保留全局图像语义但缺乏细粒度局部证据），从而提供图像感知但局部欠对齐的对比信号。Y-Architecture避免了第二次完整前向传播。

Result: 在生成式和判别式幻觉基准上，YARD在多个LVLM上一致达到最先进的幻觉缓解效果，同时显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: YARD通过巧妙的Y-Architecture设计，在不增加训练开销的前提下有效缓解了LVLM的幻觉问题，兼顾了效果与效率，为对比解码类方法提供了新的设计思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究大视觉语言模型（LVLM）中的幻觉缓解问题，提出Y-Architecture Register Decoding（YARD）方法，属于多模态幻觉缓解方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）完全匹配。

Importance reason: Addresses hallucination in LVLMs with efficient decoding, but falls outside user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: Training-free contrastive decoding, Y-architecture reduces latency, Focus on hallucination mitigation, Not aligned with user's top-weighted continuous multimodal or video generation topics, No boost keywords matched

Abstract: Contrastive decoding (CD) seeks to mitigate hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of a standard model and a visually degraded model. However, existing training-free CD methods suffer from sub-optimal degraded branches: completely dropping visual tokens is too extreme and induces language hallucinations, while corrupting input images offers coarse control over visual evidence and suffers from high inference latency due to requiring two full forward passes. To address these dilemmas, we propose YARD, a training-free Y-Architecture Register Decoding framework. Motivated by the observation that reliable text-to-vision grounding predominantly emerges in the middle decoder layers, YARD constructs the degraded branch internally by sharing shallow-layer computations and branching exactly at this critical stage. For the degraded branch, YARD replaces patch-level visual tokens with register tokens, which preserve global image semantics but lack fine-grained local evidence. This image-aware yet locally under-grounded design provides a faithful contrastive signal without extreme modality mismatch, while the Y-architecture strictly avoids a costly second forward pass. Extensive experiments on generative and discriminative hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that YARD consistently achieves state-of-the-art hallucination mitigation across multiple LVLMs, alongside a significant reduction in inference latency.

</details>


### [78] [Fighting Numerical Hallucinations via Data-centric Compilation for Online Financial QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31064)
*Hao Chen, Xing Tang, Qirui Liu, Weijie Shi, Shiwei Li, Fuyuan Lyu, Weihong Luo, Xiku Du, Xiuqiang He*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.IR

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (49.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出数据为中心的推理编译器（DCRC），通过对抗数据构建、多阶段训练和编译-执行推理流程，解决在线金融QA中LLM的数值推理幻觉问题，在离线基准和真实部署中均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融QA场景中LLM存在严重的数值推理幻觉问题，现有RAG方法面临噪声敏感、计算脆弱和可审计性危机三大挑战，而模型中心的方法（单独优化检索器或生成器）无法系统性解决这些问题。

Method: 提出DCRC框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 对抗数据构建——合成含可控噪声的训练样本以增强鲁棒性；(2) 多阶段训练——训练数据中心的编排智能体（DSA），使其具备显式证据审计和程序合成能力；(3) 编译-执行推理——DSA将用户查询和检索文档转化为可验证、可执行的推理程序，确保数值推理的忠实性。

Result: 在离线金融QA基准上取得显著提升，并在真实在线金融QA系统中部署验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 数据中心的编译范式能够有效缓解LLM在金融QA中的数值推理幻觉，通过显式程序化推理和证据审计提升了系统的可靠性和可审计性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于金融QA场景中的数值推理幻觉问题，属于多模态/大模型幻觉缓解与可靠性提升范畴，与[多模态幻觉与可靠性]（multimodal_hallucination）这一CANONICAL子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial QA numerical hallucinations, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: financial QA, numerical hallucination, data-centric compilation, RAG limitations, offline benchmarks

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly advanced online data services, particularly in the domain of financial question answering (FinQA). However, such systems remain susceptible to numerical reasoning hallucinations, which critically undermine reliability in high-stakes financial applications. Although retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has been widely adopted to ground responses in external knowledge, it introduces three persistent challenges: noise sensitivity, calculation fragility, and an auditability crisis. Existing model-centric approaches, which primarily focus on optimizing either the retriever or generator in isolation, still struggle to address these issues in an integrated manner. In this work, we pioneer a data-centric paradigm and propose a novel framework, the Data-centric Reasoning Compiler (DCRC). The framework operates through three cohesive phases: (1) adversarial data construction, which synthesizes training examples with controlled noise to teach robustness; (2) multi-stage training that cultivates a Data-centric Structuring Agent (DSA) capable of explicit evidence auditing and program synthesis; and (3) a compile-and-execute inference process, where the DSA transforms user queries and retrieved documents into verifiable, executable reasoning programs. This data-driven framework ensures faithful numerical reasoning by design. We conduct extensive experiments on established offline benchmarks and further validate our framework through deployment in a real-world online financial QA system.

</details>

### [79] [The Flip Side of RLHF: On-Policy Feedback for Reward Model Self-Supervised Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30888)
*Xiaobo Wang, Tong Wu, Min Tang, Jiaqi Li, Qi Liu, Zilong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (79.09) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SAVE 框架，利用价值函数对在线策略生成的响应进行评分，将其转化为监督信号用于奖励模型的自监督训练，解决了 RLHF 中静态 RM 无法适应策略演化的问题。在六个基准上验证了有效性，并兼容多种 RL 算法和策略骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 中奖励模型（RM）通常基于静态偏好数据训练，但随着策略模型不断演化，静态 RM 难以适应新策略的分布偏移，导致奖励信号不准确。获取多样且可靠的偏好数据成本高昂，亟需一种能利用在线策略反馈来自我改进 RM 的方法。

Method: 提出 SAVE（Self-supervised reward model improvement via Value-Anchored On-policy feedback）框架。核心思想是：利用 RM 中的价值头（value head）对当前策略生成的在线响应进行评分，以 prompt 特定的价值头作为自适应锚点，计算 RM 优势（advantage），过滤模糊样本，并通过对比学习目标更新 RM。整个过程无需额外人工标注或外部评判模型。

Result: 在六个多样化基准上进行了严格实验验证，SAVE 在所有数据集上均取得优于基线方法的结果。在三种不同的 RL 算法（GRPO、RLOO、GSPO）和不同策略骨干上均保持一致的性能提升，证明了方法的通用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SAVE 框架通过价值锚定的在线策略反馈，实现了奖励模型的自监督持续改进，有效缓解了静态 RM 与演化策略之间的分布不匹配问题，为 RLHF 中 RM 训练提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SAVE 框架，通过价值函数生成在线策略反馈来改进奖励模型（RM）训练，属于 RLHF/偏好学习/对齐方向。虽然论文主要针对语言模型，但其方法（on-policy RM 自监督改进）与多模态对齐中的 RLHF/DPO/偏好学习范式高度相关，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on RLHF reward model improvement, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's key interests in continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: RLHF, reward model, on-policy feedback, self-supervised, alignment

Abstract: Building strong reward models (RMs) for language model alignment is bottlenecked by the cost and difficulty of acquiring diverse and reliable preference data from human annotation or judge models. It is dramatically worse as the policy evolves beyond the static RM training. Therefore, we propose SAVE (Self-supervised reward model improvement via Value-Anchored On-policy feedback), a framework that grades on-policy responses as feedback by using the value function for on-policy RM training. SAVE naturally converts the reward-graded on-policy responses into supervision with a prompt-specific value head as an adaptive anchor. It computes RM advantages and filters ambiguous samples to update the RM via a contrastive objective. The effectiveness of SAVE for enhancing RM training is strongly validated through rigorous empirical evaluation across six diverse benchmarks. It achieves outperforming results across all datasets while maintaining consistent improvements across three RL algorithms (GRPO, RLOO, GSPO) and different policy backbones.

</details>


### [80] [UniScale: Adaptive Unified Inference Scaling via Online Joint Optimization of Model Routing and Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30898)
*Kaiyu Huang, Xingyu Wang, Mingze Kong, Zhubo Shi, Yuqian Hou, Hong Xu, Zhongxiang Dai, Minchen Yu, Qingjiang Shi*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (64.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniScale 提出统一推理扩展（UIS）框架，将模型路由和测试时扩展（TTS）联合建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，通过 LinUCB 在线学习推理策略，在动态推理场景中实现更细粒度的质量-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将模型路由（在不同规模模型间切换）和测试时扩展（在固定模型内调整推理计算量）作为两个独立维度处理，导致模型路由只能提供粗粒度的离散性能变化，而单模型 TTS 存在容量上限且收益递减。两者分离的设计限制了动态推理环境中的自适应能力。

Method: 提出统一推理扩展（UIS）框架，将模型路由和 TTS 统一到单一优化空间中。在此基础上设计 UniScale 在线框架，将自适应 UIS 建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，使用 LinUCB 算法学习推理策略。框架包含效率感知学习和成本建模，以在高维动作空间中实现稳定可扩展的优化。

Result: 实验表明，UniScale 能够有效利用 UIS 空间中的协同效应，在多样化的动态推理场景中实现更细粒度且一致更优的质量-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 将模型路由与测试时扩展统一优化是提升 LLM 推理效率的有效方向，UniScale 通过在线学习框架实现了这一目标，为动态推理环境中的自适应计算分配提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM推理时质量-成本权衡的在线优化，将模型路由与测试时扩展统一建模为上下文多臂老虎机问题，属于推理策略对齐与优化范畴。虽然论文聚焦于纯文本LLM而非多模态，但其提出的统一推理扩展框架（UIS）和在线学习策略（LinUCB）可自然迁移至多模态LLM的推理优化场景，因此映射到"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel unified inference scaling framework that jointly optimizes model routing and test-time scaling, directly addressing efficiency and quality trade-offs in LLM deployment. Signals: Unified inference scaling, online learning via LinUCB, quality-cost trade-off, dynamic inference, model routing + test-time scaling

Abstract: In real-world deployments of large language models (LLMs), balancing inference quality and computational cost has become a central challenge. Existing approaches tackle this trade-off along two largely independent dimensions: model routing, which switches among models of different scales to match request complexity, and test-time scaling (TTS), which adjusts inference-time compute within a fixed model for fine-grained control. However, this decoupled design introduces inherent limitations. Model routing yields coarse-grained, discrete performance changes due to the sparse set of model scales, while single-model TTS often encounters capacity ceilings and exhibits diminishing returns as compute increases. Moreover, treating the two mechanisms separately restricts adaptability in dynamic inference environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Unified Inference Scaling (UIS), which unifies model routing and TTS in a single optimization space. Building on this formulation, we propose UniScale, an online framework that models adaptive UIS as a contextual multi-armed bandit problem and learns inference policies via LinUCB. The framework incorporates efficiency-aware learning and cost modeling to ensure stable and scalable optimization over high-dimensional action spaces. Evaluation shows that UniScale effectively exploits the synergy in the UIS space to deliver a fine-grained and consistently better quality-cost trade-off across diverse, dynamic inference scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [Attend to Evidence: Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention Supervision for Multimodal RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30912)
*Ruina Hu, Chen Wang, Lai Wei, Jionghao Bai, Bin Yu, Weiran Huang, Kai Wang, Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 EASE（Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention），通过将标注的视觉证据区域转化为平滑的视觉 token 注意力目标，在 RLVR 训练中引导模型关注相关图像区域，从而提升 VLM 在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理等任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RLVR 方法仅使用最终答案的奖励信号进行优化，无法区分基于视觉证据的正确回答与依赖语言先验或运气猜测的回答。对于需要视觉定位的问题，这种仅基于结果的奖励无法引导模型关注正确的图像区域。

Method: EASE 将人工标注的证据区域转换为平滑的视觉 token 注意力目标，在 RL 训练中仅对高奖励轨迹施加响应到图像的注意力引导。标注仅在训练阶段作为特权标签使用，推理时仅需原始图像和问题。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，EASE 相比 DAPO 在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理基准上平均提升 2.5~3.1 分。诊断和消融实验表明 EASE 能更好地将视觉注意力与标注证据区域对齐。

Conclusion: EASE 通过引入视觉证据过程监督有效增强了多模态 RLVR，使模型在需要视觉定位的任务上表现更优，且推理时无需额外标注，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EASE 方法，在 RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）框架中引入视觉证据过程监督，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" (multimodal_rl_alignment)。

Importance reason: Improves VLM grounding via attention supervision, but is not in a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous or generative models. Signals: RLVR for VLM, spatial attention supervision, evidence grounding, process reward, multimodal understanding

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves vision-language models (VLMs) by optimizing outcome rewards derived from final answers. However, such outcome-only rewards do not tell the model which image regions justify an answer. For questions that require visual grounding, these rewards cannot distinguish responses supported by relevant visual evidence from those produced by language-prior shortcuts or lucky guesses. We introduce EASE (Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention), which augments multimodal RLVR with visual-evidence process supervision. EASE converts annotated evidence regions into a smoothed visual-token target and uses it to guide response-to-image attention during RL training, but only on high-reward trajectories. The annotations are used solely as privileged training labels, while inference requires only the original image and question. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-4B, and Qwen3-VL-8B, EASE raises average scores over DAPO by 2.5 to 3.1 points on perception, hallucination, visual math, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Diagnostics and ablations show that EASE better aligns visual attention with annotated evidence regions.

</details>


### [82] [SCOPE: Self-Play via Co-Evolving Policies for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31433)
*Wai-Chung Kwan, Aryo Pradipta Gema, Joshua Ong Jun Leang, Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (83.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SCOPE 是一种无数据的自博弈框架，通过协同进化 Challenger（生成文档基础任务）和 Solver（多轮检索回答）两个策略，并使用冻结初始模型作为自评判器，在开放任务上提升语言模型性能，在 8 个基准上最高提升 +10.4 分，并在 7 个保留的短问答基准上最高提升 +13.8 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈方法需要规则可检查的答案（如数学题），无法处理开放任务（如文档问答），后者通常依赖人工策划的提示或前沿模型作为评判器。作者希望设计一种无需外部监督、无需人工数据、无需强模型评判器的自博弈框架，使语言模型能在开放任务中自我提升。

Method: SCOPE 框架包含两个协同进化的策略：Challenger 从文档中生成开放任务，Solver 通过多轮检索回答这些任务。冻结的初始模型副本作为自评判器，从源文档编写任务评分标准，并据此对 Solver 的回答进行评分。Challenger 和 Solver 交替使用 GRPO 进行优化，Challenger 被鼓励生成 Solver 当前能力边界附近的任务，从而持续推动 Solver 进步。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5、Qwen3、OLMo-3 三个 7-8B 指令微调模型上，SCOPE 在 8 个开放任务基准上最高提升 +10.4 分，匹配或超越使用约 9K 策划提示训练的 GRPO_data。在 7 个保留的短问答基准上最高提升 +13.8 分，全面超越 GRPO_data。消融实验表明：协同进化 Challenger 对保持任务难度前沿至关重要；检索和合成能力的提升贡献因任务而异；评分标准生成质量是自评判的瓶颈。

Conclusion: SCOPE 证明了无需外部监督、无需人工数据即可通过自博弈提升语言模型在开放任务上的能力，且能泛化到短问答任务。协同进化的任务生成策略是保持训练有效性的关键，而自评判的评分标准生成质量是当前主要瓶颈，为未来改进指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SCOPE，一种基于自博弈（self-play）的无数据框架，通过协同进化两个策略（Challenger 和 Solver）来训练语言模型处理开放任务，并使用冻结的初始模型作为自评判器。这属于多模态/语言模型的强化学习与对齐范畴（self-play 是一种无监督对齐/训练方法），因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Novel self-play framework for open-ended tasks, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Self-play without external data, Co-evolving policies for open-ended tasks, Uses frozen model as self-judge, Improves retrieval and synthesis, No direct link to continuous/multimodal generation

Abstract: Self-play can train language models without external supervision. However, existing methods require rule-checkable answers, leaving open-ended tasks dependent on curated prompts or frontier-model judges. We introduce SCOPE, a data-free self-play framework for open-ended tasks that co-evolves two policies: a Challenger that generates document-grounded tasks, and a Solver that answers them through multi-turn retrieval. A frozen copy of the initial model serves as the self-judge, which writes task-specific rubrics from the source document and grades Solver responses against them. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, OLMo-3), SCOPE improves open-ended performance by up to +10.4 points on eight benchmarks and matches or exceeds GRPO_data trained on ~9K curated prompts. Although trained only on open-ended tasks, SCOPE also improves held-out short-form QA by up to +13.8 points on seven held-out benchmarks, surpassing GRPO_data on all three models. Ablations show that co-evolving the Challenger is necessary to keep tasks near the Solver's frontier, that gains arise from improvements in both retrieval and synthesis with the relative contribution varying by task, and that rubric generation quality is the bottleneck for self-judging.

</details>


### [83] [Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior for Personalized User Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30873)
*Jabin Koo, Hoyoung Kim, Minwoo Jang, Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.DC, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (49.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedVPA-GP，一种联邦变分偏好对齐框架，通过 Gumbel-Softmax 先验和联邦混合先验解决本地数据稀缺导致的 posterior collapse 问题，并引入正交损失显式分离偏好原型，实现个性化用户偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习对齐框架通常强制使用单一奖励模型，平均化了用户间冲突的偏好（如 helpfulness vs. harmlessness）。变分偏好学习（VPL）虽能实现个性化，但在去中心化场景中面临后验坍塌问题，原因是本地数据极度稀缺且异构。因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能解耦多样化偏好的联邦对齐方法。

Method: 提出 FedVPA-GP 框架：(1) 引入 Federated Mixture Prior，使客户端能够利用聚合的总体分布作为动态先验，稳定变分推断；(2) 引入 Orthogonal Loss，在潜空间中显式强制分离偏好原型；(3) 使用 Gumbel-Softmax 先验进行离散偏好建模，避免后验坍塌。

Result: 在 HH-RLHF 数据集上的实验表明，FedVPA-GP 显著优于单一奖励模型的基线方法，成功解耦了冲突的用户意图，并支持动态偏好切换。

Conclusion: FedVPA-GP 通过联邦混合先验和正交损失有效解决了联邦场景下变分偏好学习的后验坍塌问题，实现了隐私保护下的个性化偏好对齐，为联邦 LLM 对齐提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究联邦学习中基于变分偏好学习（VPL）的个性化LLM对齐，属于多模态/语言模型对齐范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的偏好学习、RLHF/DPO方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on federated preference alignment for LLMs, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Federated learning, preference alignment, posterior collapse, Gumbel-Softmax, orthogonal loss

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving pathway for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs); however, existing frameworks typically enforce a monolithic reward model, inevitably averaging out inherently conflicting user preferences (e.g., helpfulness vs. harmlessness). While Variational Preference Learning (VPL) offers a pathway to personalization, adapting it to decentralized settings presents a fundamental challenge: posterior collapse driven by severe local data scarcity and heterogeneity. In this paper, we propose Federated Variational Preference Alignment with Gumbel-Softmax Prior (FedVPA-GP), a framework designed to disentangle diverse preferences without compromising privacy. To stabilize variational inference, we introduce a Federated Mixture Prior that enables clients to leverage the aggregate population distribution as a dynamic prior. Furthermore, we incorporate an Orthogonal Loss that explicitly enforces the separation of preference prototypes in the latent space. Experiments on the HH-RLHF dataset demonstrate that FedVPA-GP significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, successfully disentangling conflicting user intents and enabling dynamic preference switching.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_reasoning_cot'></div>

## 多模态推理与思维链 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态 CoT、视觉推理、数学/代码/科学推理、推理时计算扩展。

### [84] [Diversity Matters: Revisiting Test-Time Compute in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30713)
*Yijie Tong, Yifan Hou, Shaobo Cui, Antoine Bosselut, Mrinmaya Sachan*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MM, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (60.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）中的测试时计算（TTC）策略，发现特征启发式方法无效，多数投票在单模型场景下增益有限，原因是预测多样性不足。为此提出基于熵的TTC方法（ETTC），在多模型集成中利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型，理论上证明其优于多数投票，实验表明小模型可协同增强大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时计算（TTC）策略在纯语言大模型中已被证明能有效提升推理能力，但其在视觉语言模型（VLM）中的应用和收益尚未被充分探索。本文旨在系统研究TTC在VLM中的效果，并解决现有方法因预测多样性不足而性能受限的问题。

Method: 首先对7个VLM和6个基准进行系统实验，分析特征评分和多数投票两种TTC策略。理论分析表明多数投票在单模型场景下收益有限的原因是输出高度相关、缺乏多样性。提出Entropy-based TTC（ETTC），基于预测熵选择最置信的预测结果：单模型时退化为多数投票，多模型集成时利用置信度差异优先选择更强模型。给出了ETTC优于多数投票的理论证明。

Result: 实验表明：特征启发式方法在VLM中无效；多数投票在单模型场景下仅带来微弱提升；ETTC在多模型集成中持续优于多数投票和最佳单模型。关键发现是小模型可以协同增强大模型，实现标准策略无法达到的集成增益。

Conclusion: 多样性是VLM测试时计算有效性的关键因素。基于熵的ETTC方法能够有效利用多模型集成中的置信度差异，实现优于多数投票的推理性能，且小模型与大模型的协同集成具有显著潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文系统研究视觉语言模型（VLM）的测试时计算（TTC）策略，分析特征评分和多数投票等方法，并提出基于熵的TTC方法（ETTC）来提升多模型集成的推理性能。这属于多模态推理与推理时计算扩展范畴，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Provides systematic analysis and a principled entropy-based method for test-time compute in VLMs, but does not directly address the user's priority subtopics. Signals: VLM test-time compute, diversity analysis, entropy-based ensemble, multi-model synergy, not continuous language/multimodal

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) strategies have emerged as a lightweight approach to boost reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, their application and benefits for vision-language models (VLMs) remain underexplored. We present a systematic study of TTC across seven VLMs and six benchmarks, specifically analyzing feature-based scoring and majority voting methods. We find that feature heuristics fail and voting yields only modest gains in single-model settings. We theoretically show that this limitation stems from a lack of prediction diversity: when outputs are highly correlated, voting provides little benefit. In contrast, multi-model ensembles offer richer diversity, yet standard majority voting fails to account for varying model capabilities. To address this, we propose Entropy-based TTC (ETTC), which selects the most confident prediction based on predictive entropy. Our method reduces to majority voting in the single-model case, but in model ensembles, it leverages confidence disparities to prioritize stronger models. We prove that ETTC outperforms majority voting under mild assumptions and empirically demonstrate that it consistently surpasses both voting and the best individual model. Crucially, our results show that smaller models can synergistically enhance larger ones, unlocking ensembling gains not achievable with standard strategies.

</details>


### [85] [Seeing Isn't Knowing: Do VLMs Know When Not to Answer Spatial Questions (and Why)?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30557)
*Yue Zhang, Zun Wang, Han Lin, Yonatan Bitton, Idan Szpektor, Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (61.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialUncertain框架，系统评估VLM在遮挡和视角歧义两种观测挑战下的空间推理能力。实验发现前沿VLM存在两个一致失败模式：一是过度自信回答（遮挡下准确率约30%，视角歧义下低于10%），二是无法识别哪些额外视角能解决歧义。呼吁从答案正确性转向评估模型何时应拒绝回答及如何寻求可靠证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准假设观测信息充分可靠，仅关注模型能否给出正确答案，忽略了模型是否能够识别问题不可回答的情况。然而真实环境中遮挡和视角歧义普遍存在，模型需要具备"知道何时不知道"的能力。

Method: 构建SpatialUncertain评估框架，设计两类观测挑战：(1) 遮挡——隐藏目标信息；(2) 视角歧义——产生误导性视觉线索。对每种配置设计在干净观测下可回答但在挑战下需拒绝回答的空间问题，并评估模型能否识别哪些额外视角能解决歧义。

Result: 在多个前沿开源和闭源VLM上，遮挡条件下平均准确率约30%，视角歧义条件下低于10%。即使提供额外视角，部分模型在识别可靠证据视角上的表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: VLM在空间推理中存在严重的过度自信问题，无法可靠识别何时应拒绝回答。未来研究应超越答案正确性，关注模型的不确定性感知和主动寻求可靠证据的能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在空间推理任务中何时应当拒绝回答（abstention），属于多模态推理范畴。虽然聚焦于空间推理的不确定性，但核心是视觉推理能力评估，与[多模态推理与思维链]（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一CANONICAL subtopic高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong evaluation of VLM spatial reasoning limitations, but only tangentially touches continuous multimodal models and lacks direct alignment with top priority subtopics. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning, occlusion and perspective ambiguity, overconfident answering, abstention evaluation, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for vision-language models (VLMs) deployed in real-world environments. However, visual observations are inherently limited representations of a 3D world: occlusion can render objects invisible, and perspective can make geometric properties misleading. Despite this, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks typically assume that observations are sufficient and reliable, focusing on whether models produce correct answers rather than whether they recognize when a question cannot be answered and what additional observations would be needed. In this work, we challenge this assumption by constructing a controlled evaluation framework, SpatialUncertain, and introducing two types of observation challenges: (1) occlusion, which hides target information, and (2) perspective ambiguity, which produces misleading visual cues. For each configuration, we design spatial questions that are answerable under clean observations but require abstention under the introduced challenges. We further evaluate whether models can identify which additional viewpoints would resolve perspective ambiguity. Our results across a diverse set of frontier open- and closed-source VLMs reveal two consistent failure modes. First, models are prone to overconfident answering, attempting to solve spatial reasoning tasks even when visual evidence is incomplete or misleading, with average accuracy around 30\% under occlusion and below 10\% under perspective ambiguity. Second, even when additional views are available, some models perform near random chance in identifying which would provide reliable evidence. Together, our findings call for moving beyond answer correctness toward evaluating whether models know when to abstain and how to seek reliable evidence.

</details>


### [86] [LARK: Learnability-Grounded Trajectory Selection for Efficient Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30651)
*Tianrun Yu, Kaixiang Zhao, Chih-Chun Chen, Amanda Hughes, Taylor W. Killian, Fenglong Ma, Weitong Zhang, Porter Jenkins*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: LARK 提出了一种基于可学习性的推理轨迹选择方法，通过定义可学习性因子 ρ 来量化学生模型从轨迹中学习的效率，并引入 χ² 正则化选择策略平衡可学习性与分布覆盖，在多个基座模型和推理任务上优于现有数据选择基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法多依赖启发式规则（如轨迹质量或模型置信度），但忽略了轨迹对学生模型是否可学习这一关键因素。作者希望设计一种能主动选择学生模型可高效学习的轨迹的方法，从而提升蒸馏效率。

Method: LARK 的核心是定义一个可学习性因子 ρ，刻画学生模型训练损失下降的速率。为高效估计该速率并保持泛化，引入可学习性代理（learnability proxy）和 χ² 正则化选择策略，在可学习性与分布覆盖之间取得平衡，并提供了估计误差的理论保证。

Result: 在多个基座模型和推理任务上，LARK 一致优于现有数据选择基线。诊断分析表明 LARK 分数能预测下游训练效用，且 LARK 选择的轨迹能诱导更快的监督微调损失下降。

Conclusion: LARK 验证了基于可学习性的轨迹选择在推理蒸馏中的有效性，为数据高效蒸馏提供了新视角，未来可扩展到更多模态和更复杂的推理场景。

Classification reason: 该论文研究推理蒸馏中的轨迹选择方法，属于多模态/大语言模型推理能力蒸馏与效率优化，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）紧密相关，因为其核心是提升学生模型的推理能力，且方法适用于多模态推理场景。

Importance reason: Focuses on reasoning distillation efficiency, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: reasoning distillation, trajectory selection, learnability, data efficiency, student model training

Abstract: We study trajectory selection for reasoning distillation, where teacher-generated reasoning trajectories are selectively used as supervision for a student model. Existing methods rely on heuristics such as trajectory quality or model confidence, but they often overlook whether a trajectory is learnable by the student. In this paper, we present LARK, a learnability-grounded method for reasoning trajectory selection. LARK selects trajectories that the student can learn efficiently while preserving the generalization of the full training distribution. At the core of LARK is a learnability factor $\rho$, which characterizes the rate at which the student's training loss decreases. To estimate this rate efficiently and maintain generalization, we introduce a learnability proxy and a $\chi^2$-regularized selection policy that balances learnability and distributional coverage, both with strong theoretical guarantees on their estimation error. Empirically, LARK consistently outperforms data selection baselines across multiple base models and reasoning tasks. Diagnostic analyses show that the LARK score predicts downstream training utility and that LARK-selected trajectories induce faster supervised fine-tuning loss reduction. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [87] [The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404)
*Xudong Zhang, Jian Yang, Shengkai Wang, Jiangpeng Tian, Shaowen Chen, Xian Wei, Ke Li, Xiong You*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双干预框架，系统研究LLM在导航规划任务中处理文本化空间表征时的语言归纳偏置。实验发现：拓扑信息是鲁棒规划的坚实盾牌；语言格式是把双刃剑，效果取决于模型大小和压缩程度；语义信息是致命弱点——错误语义会系统性破坏规划过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的导航系统通常将空间信息（拓扑图、语义栅格图）转化为文本描述作为输入，但文本化空间表征的语言结构及其包含的上下文特征（拓扑、几何、语义）对LLM行为的影响被忽视，被视为中性的工程决策。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出双干预框架：(1) 表征干预——改变语言格式和语言压缩程度，观察对导航规划的影响；(2) 上下文干预——结合上下文特征组合与冲突探测，明确LLM处理不同上下文线索时的偏好与弱点。在多种空间推理任务和多个模型规模上进行实验。

Result: 发现一致模式：拓扑信息是稳健规划的坚实盾牌；语言格式是双刃剑，效果取决于模型大小、任务需求和压缩程度；语义信息是致命弱点，错误语义线索会系统性破坏规划过程。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的导航系统中，有效的文本化空间表征应保持拓扑完整性、根据模型能力校准表征压缩程度、确保语义正确性，而非简单采用单一表征形式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究LLM在导航规划中的空间推理能力，属于多模态推理范畴（空间推理是视觉推理的重要子领域），因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"。

Importance reason: Focuses on LLM spatial reasoning bias, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: LLM navigation planning, linguistic inductive bias, spatial reasoning, topological vs semantic cues, model scale effects

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based navigation systems commonly construct explicit spatial representations (e.g., topological graphs, semantic raster maps) and translate them into textual descriptions as LLMs' inputs. However, the linguistic structures of such text-based spatial representations and the choices of contextual features (e.g., topology, geometry) they contain are often treated as neutral engineering decisions rather than key factors that shape LLMs' behavior. To fill the gap, we propose a dual-interventional framework that disentangles linguistic structures from different contextual cues to evaluate the linguistic inductive bias of LLMs for navigation planning. In the framework, representation intervention varies the linguistic format and the degree of linguistic compression, clarifying when linguistic representations support or inhibit navigation planning. Context intervention, combined with contextual feature combination and conflict probing, explicitly clarifies the preferences and weaknesses of LLMs when processing different contextual cues. Experiments across diverse spatial reasoning tasks and multiple model scales reveal a consistent pattern: topological information is a sturdy shield and the backbone of robust planning; linguistic format is a double-edged sword whose effect depends on model size, task demands, and the compression level; and semantic information is a fatal Achilles' heel -- incorrect semantic cues can systematically derail the planning process. Overall, our study shows that effective text-based spatial representations in LLM-based navigation should preserve topological integrity, calibrate representational compression to model capacity, and ensure semantic correctness, rather than simply adopting a single representation. Our code is publicly available at .

</details>


### [88] [iVGR: Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning for MLLMs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31096)
*Chang-Bin Zhang, Yujie Zhong, Qiang Zhang, Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

TL;DR: 本文提出 iVGR 框架，通过强化学习将视觉定位能力内化到 MLLM 的文本推理过程中，使模型在推理时无需显式输出目标框即可实现精细视觉定位，在细粒度评测基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉定位 Chain-of-Thought 方法在推理阶段强制要求模型输出显式目标框，反而会干扰答案预测的主任务，导致性能下降。作者希望将视觉定位能力内化到文本推理过程中，避免显式定位带来的干扰。

Method: 提出 iVGR 框架，采用双流训练策略：一个文本流（textual stream）与一个高质量视觉定位流（visually grounded stream）通过一致性奖励（consistency reward）进行对齐，利用强化学习将定位能力迁移到文本推理中，使模型在推理时无需显式定位即可准确感知。

Result: 在多个细粒度评测基准上，iVGR 显著优于现有基线方法，同时保持支持工具辅助推理工作流的灵活性。

Conclusion: 视觉定位能力可以通过强化学习内化到文本推理过程中，无需在推理时强制显式定位，这为 MLLM 的细粒度感知提供了一种更高效、更灵活的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 MLLM 的视觉定位推理与 Chain-of-Thought，属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances multimodal reasoning via RL, aligning with priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models and MLLMs. Signals: multimodal large language model, reinforcement learning, visual grounding, chain-of-thought, fine-grained perception

Abstract: While visually grounded Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has emerged as a promising paradigm to enhance fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), its efficacy during the inference phase remains underexplored. In this work, we empirically find that mandating explicit object boxes in visually grounded CoT during inference often degrades performance compared to standard textual CoT, which reasons without explicit visual grounding. We hypothesize that the visual localization capability can be internalized into the textual CoT and that the mandatory explicit grounding introduces unnecessary interference with the model's primary objective of answer prediction. To address this problem, we propose Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning (\textbf{iVGR}), a novel reinforcement learning framework that transfers localization capabilities into the textual reasoning process. We employ a dual-stream training strategy, where a textual stream is aligned with a high-quality visually grounded stream via a proposed consistency reward, enabling the model to localize accurately without explicit grounding during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines on fine-grained benchmarks, while maintaining the flexibility to support tool-assisted inference workflows.

</details>

### [89] [Learning Whom to Trust: Market-Feedback Adaptive Retrieval for Frozen LLMs in Event-Driven Financial RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31201)
*Zijie Zhao, Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

TL;DR: 本文提出一种市场反馈自适应的金融RAG方法，通过贝叶斯源记忆（Bayesian Source Memory）根据已实现的残差收益反馈动态调整检索来源权重，在冻结LLM的情况下显著提升了事件驱动金融预测的F1分数和下游投资组合夏普比率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融RAG中，传统方法仅按文本相关性排序证据，但金融市场的有用证据来源取决于事件类型、预测周期和市场环境。作者希望让检索层能够根据市场反馈自适应调整，学习"信任谁"而非仅"如何读"。

Method: 提出Frozen Reader with Source Memory框架：1) 对每个公司-新闻锚点，检索相关金融新闻和SEC文件段落；2) 附加市场上下文卡片；3) 使用冻结的LLM进行多时间跨度残差收益预测；4) 通过外部贝叶斯源记忆（Bayesian Source Memory）根据已实现的残差收益反馈更新各检索来源的权重，实现检索层的自适应调整。

Result: 在89只纳斯达克股票的FNSPID数据集上，Frozen Reader with Source Memory相比无记忆的冻结阅读器，宏F1从0.438提升至0.471，下游投资组合夏普比率从0.52提升至0.84。有监督LoRA阅读器在静态RAG上略有提升，但未超过冻结源记忆阅读器。

Conclusion: 在金融RAG中，学习从何处检索（检索层自适应）与学习如何阅读（LLM微调）同等重要，甚至更为关键。所提出的贝叶斯源记忆方法提供了一种简单、模块化的市场反馈适应途径。

Classification reason: 该论文研究金融领域的检索增强生成（RAG），核心贡献在于通过市场反馈自适应调整检索层（Bayesian source memory），属于多模态/跨模态检索的范畴。论文涉及文本（新闻、SEC文件）检索与LLM结合，因此映射到[多模态检索]（multimodal_retrieval）这一规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on financial RAG with market feedback, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Financial RAG, Bayesian source memory, event-driven prediction, frozen LLM, market feedback adaptation

Abstract: Financial retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rank evidence by textual relevance, but in financial markets the useful evidence source depends on event type, forecast horizon, and market context. We study news-triggered event-impact prediction as a point-in-time financial RAG problem. For each company-news anchor, the system retrieves related financial news and SEC filing passages, appends a pre-decision market-context card, and predicts multi-horizon residual-return signals. Our method keeps the large language model (LLM) reader frozen and adapts the retrieval layer through an external Bayesian source memory updated from matured residual-return feedback. On a fixed 89-stock Nasdaq-oriented universe derived from the FinRL-DeepSeek/FNSPID task, using original FNSPID news and point-in-time EDGAR filing passages, Frozen Reader with Source Memory improves held-out macro-F1 from 0.438 to 0.471 and downstream portfolio Sharpe from 0.52 to 0.84 relative to Frozen Reader with No Memory. A supervised LoRA reader improves static RAG modestly, but does not improve over the frozen source-memory reader. These results suggest that, for financial RAG, learning where to retrieve from can be as important as learning how to read, offering a simple, modular route to market-feedback adaptation.

</details>


### [90] [Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30968)
*WenZhang Wei, Zhipeng Gui, Dehua Peng, Tiandi Ye, Huayi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 本文提出变分适配器 VACSR，将图文匹配中因细粒度语义标注稀缺导致的二值分类问题重新建模为变分推理问题，通过构建跨模态相似度的潜空间并引入正则化缓解过拟合，在图像-文本检索、域泛化和 base-to-novel 泛化任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数图文匹配和多类图像分类数据集缺乏细粒度的跨模态匹配标注，迫使连续的相似度空间被压缩为二值分类边界，导致假负样本并损害跨模态任务的泛化性能。现有方法仅建模模态内歧义性，忽略了标注本身的缺陷。

Method: 提出变分适配器 VACSR，将具有细粒度语义稀缺的图文匹配问题形式化为变分推理问题，构建跨模态相似度的潜空间，利用正则化技术缓解对二值标注的过拟合。

Result: 在图像-文本检索、域泛化和 base-to-novel 泛化任务上，VACSR 均取得了有效且鲁棒的结果，验证了方法的泛化能力。

Conclusion: VACSR 通过变分推理框架有效缓解了二值标注带来的假负样本问题，提升了跨模态相似度表征的泛化性能，为细粒度语义稀缺场景下的图文匹配提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究图文匹配与跨模态相似度表征，属于跨模态检索的核心问题，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on cross-modal retrieval with binary annotation correction, not aligned with user's priority on generation or continuous models. Signals: Variational inference for similarity, Image-text retrieval, Domain generalization, Not generation-focused, No continuous token or autoregressive signal

Abstract: The core of vision-language models lies in measuring cross-modal similarity within a unified representation space. However, most image-text matching or multi-class image classification datasets lack fine-grained cross-modal matching annotations, forcing the continuous similarity space into binary classification boundaries. This compression induces false negative samples and significantly impairs the generalization performance of cross-modal tasks. While prior research has attempted to mitigate this by modeling intra-modal ambiguity, it often overlooks inherent annotation flaws, leading to suboptimal uncertainty allocation. To address these challenges, we propose a Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation (VACSR). This approach reformulates image-text matching with fine-grained semantic scarcity as a variational inference problem. It constructs a latent space for cross-modal similarity and uses regularization techniques to mitigate overfitting to binary annotations. Experiments on image-text retrieval, domain generalization, and base-to-novel generalization demonstrate the proposed method's effectiveness and robust generalization ability.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Classification: Dynamic Adapter Routing for Continual Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31229)
*Alicja Dobrzeniecka, Filip Szatkowski, Sebastian Cygert, Szymon Lukasik, Bartlomiej Twardowski*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出持续多模态检索（CMR）的新评测框架，发现标准类增量学习方法在此场景下效果有限，进而提出动态适配器路由（DAR）方法，通过原型路由选择适配器并组合，在跨域持续检索任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的持续学习研究主要聚焦于分类任务（类增量学习），而检索作为视觉语言模型的核心功能，其持续更新问题尚未被充分探索。现有方法在检索场景下的评估设置可能无法捕捉检索特有的动态特性。

Method: 提出 Dynamic Adapter Routing (DAR) 方法：基于原型（prototype）路由机制动态选择适配器（adapter），并通过模型组合（model averaging）融合多个适配器的输出，实现持续多模态检索中的知识积累与迁移。

Result: 在跨域持续多模态检索评测中，DAR 方法显著优于标准类增量学习方法和已有的检索适配方法，并在分布外（OOD）评估中展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 持续多模态检索具有不同于类增量学习的独特挑战，标准 CIL 方法难以带来有效提升；DAR 方法通过动态适配器路由有效应对这些挑战，为该方向提供了新的基线和方法论基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究持续多模态检索（continual multimodal retrieval），属于多模态检索范畴，与 canonical subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）直接匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on continual multimodal retrieval, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks strong alignment with user's key boost keywords. Signals: Continual multimodal retrieval, dynamic adapter routing, prototype-based routing, out-of-distribution generalization, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: While retrieval is a core function of vision-language models, continually updating these models for retrieval tasks remains critically underexplored. Existing work often approaches continual retrieval through the lens of class-incremental learning (CIL), evaluating both standard CIL methods and retrieval-oriented adaptations in settings that may not fully capture the retrieval-specific dynamics. To address this, we introduce a new, principled evaluation framework for continual multimodal retrieval (CMR) spanning diverse visual domains, and systematically evaluate common approaches within this setting. Our empirical analysis shows that standard CIL methods fail to yield meaningful gains in our more challenging scenario. Therefore, we propose Dynamic Adapter Routing (DAR), a novel approach based on adapters selected through prototype-based routing and combined via model achieves superior performance over the previous baselines and demonstrates strong generalization under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results highlights the unique challenges of CMR and encourages further research in this direction.

</details>

*Gyu-Hwung Cho, Youngjune Lee, Kiyoon Jeong, Siyoung Lee, Sanggyu Han, Hervé Dejean, Stéphane Clinchant, Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.IR

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出V-SPLADE，一种推理时无需神经编码的稀疏检索器，用于大规模视觉文档检索。通过引入caption-gated token supervision（利用VLM生成标题作为词汇线索），解决了视觉稀疏表示难以捕获文档图像中词汇内容的问题，在六个基准上显著优于同规模稠密检索和BM25基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法要么使用VLM稠密或多向量模型（检索质量好但推理时需要神经查询编码），要么使用OCR或标题的BM25（避免查询编码但需要耗时的文本提取或生成）。缺少一种可部署的、能对视觉文档进行词汇索引且推理时无需神经编码的检索系统。

Method: 提出V-SPLADE，一种推理时无需神经编码的稀疏检索器。核心创新是caption-gated token supervision：在训练时利用VLM生成的标题作为词汇线索，通过门控机制激活检索相关的词汇维度，使视觉稀疏表示能更好地捕获文档图像中的词汇内容。该方法在训练时使用VLM标题监督，推理时仅需稀疏索引和查询词频统计，无需任何神经编码。

Result: 在六个视觉文档检索基准上，V-SPLADE的NDCG@5平均比同规模稠密基线高13.8个百分点，比OCR或标题BM25基线高6.3个百分点。在1870万文档的大规模语料上，R@5比同规模稠密基线提升一倍以上，通过分数融合可进一步提升其他检索器最多2.4个百分点的R@5。

Conclusion: V-SPLADE成功填补了视觉文档检索中"推理时无需神经编码"这一缺失的服务范式，证明了推理时无编码的稀疏检索可以达到甚至超越稠密检索的效果，为大规模视觉文档检索提供了高效可部署的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉文档检索（visual-document retrieval），提出V-SPLADE稀疏检索器，属于跨模态/多模态文档检索范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态检索"（multimodal_retrieval）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on retrieval efficiency for visual documents, not on core generative or continuous multimodal modeling priorities. Signals: Inference-free sparse retrieval, visual document search, production-scale, caption-gated token supervision, no neural encoding at serving time

Abstract: As large-scale visual-document corpora such as arXiv papers and enterprise PDFs continue to grow, visual-document retrieval has gained increasing attention; yet it still lacks a deployable system that lexically indexes visual documents to serve queries without neural encoding at scale. Existing methods either achieve strong retrieval quality with VLM-based dense or multi-vector models but require neural query encoding at serving time, or avoid query encoding with OCR- or caption-based BM25 at the cost of time-consuming text extraction or generation. To fill this missing serving regime, we present V-SPLADE, an inference-free sparse retriever for visual-document retrieval. However, such inference-free multimodal learned sparse retrieval systems remain underexplored and have not yet shown dense-level effectiveness under high sparsity. We attribute this limitation to a lexical grounding problem: visual sparse representations often fail to capture the lexical content embedded in document images. To address this problem, we introduce caption-gated token supervision, a training-only signal that uses VLM-generated captions as lexical cues to activate retrieval-relevant vocabulary dimensions. With this supervision, V-SPLADE improves average NDCG@5 across six visual-document retrieval benchmarks by +13.8pp over the same-scale dense baseline and by up to +6.3pp over OCR- or caption-based BM25 baselines. On an 18.7M-document corpus, it more than doubles R@5 over the same-scale dense baseline and further improves competing retrievers through score fusion by up to +2.4pp R@5. Code will be released soon at .

</details>
