### [93] [TeachObs: A Human-Validated Benchmark for Multimodal Teaching Observation and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30673)
*Yeil Jeong, Youngjin Yoo, Seobin Sohn, Hyejin Han, Jinseo Lee, Scott Howard, Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TeachObs 是一个人工验证的多模态课堂教学观察基准，包含30个公开课程视频（8个国家）、5158个15秒场景片段，由7位研究者标注39个二值观察编码（20个视觉+19个非视觉），并构建了基于信度和流行度的金标签。同时有3位专家进行课程级评分。基于该基准评估了5个前沿视觉LLM在三个赛道上的表现，发现没有单一模型在所有赛道上一致最优，且模型倾向于高估程序清晰的课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂视频中包含丰富的可观察教学行为，但其教学和视觉信号很少以适合模型评估的形式组织。现有基准缺乏对课堂教学视频的多模态细粒度标注和系统评估框架。

Method: 收集30个来自8个国家的公开课程视频，分割为5158个15秒场景片段。7位研究者对每个片段标注39个二值观察编码（20个视觉编码如手势、板书、指向、视觉材料；19个非视觉编码如指令、监控、提问、反馈、反思）。基于Krippendorff's alpha构建信度和流行度感知的金标签。3位专家对30个课程进行课程级评分。评估5个视觉LLM在三个赛道（纯文本片段编码、文本+帧片段编码、LLM-as-judge课程级评估）上的表现。

Result: 没有单一模型在所有三个赛道上一致最优；添加中间帧会同时增加每个场景的真实归因和虚假归因；模型评估相对于专家评分者高估了程序清晰的课程。

Conclusion: TeachObs 支持细粒度标注基准测试和完整课程评估，展示了AI系统在课堂视频分析中的辅助能力以及专家判断在多样化科目、课堂形式和标注难度下仍然必要的领域。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向课堂教学视频的多模态评测基准 TeachObs，包含细粒度标注和课程级评估，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on a niche educational benchmark, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal teaching observation, Classroom video benchmark, Vision LLM evaluation, Low priority subtopic match, No boost keywords

Abstract: Classroom videos contain observable teaching practices, but their pedagogical and visual signals are rarely organized in forms suitable for model evaluation. We present \textit{TeachObs}, a human-validated benchmark for multimodal teaching observation in classroom videos. \textit{TeachObs} includes 30 public lesson videos from eight countries divided into 5,158 fixed 15-second scenes. Seven researchers annotated each scene with 39 binary observation codes, covering 20 visual codes, such as gesture, board work, pointing, and visual materials, and 19 nonvisual codes, such as instruction, monitoring, questioning, feedback, and reflection. Gold segment labels are constructed using reliability- and prevalence-aware rules based on Krippendorff's alpha. In addition to segment-level labels, three expert raters produced lesson-level ratings and qualitative evaluations of instructional design, instructional delivery, learner response, learning materials, and lesson closure across the 30 lessons, with rater coverage detailed in the body. Using these two human reference layers, we evaluate five vision-capable frontier LLMs across three tracks - text-only segment coding, text + frame segment coding, and lesson-level coverage scored under an LLM-as-judge protocol - and find that no single model consistently outperforms others across all three tracks, that adding a mid-frame inflates both true and false attributions per scene, and that model evaluations over-rate procedurally clear lessons relative to expert raters. \textit{TeachObs} therefore supports both fine-grained annotation benchmarking and whole-lesson evaluation, showing where AI systems can assist classroom video analysis and where expert judgment remains necessary across varied subjects, classroom formats, and annotation difficulty levels.

</details>


### [94] [MineExplorer: Evaluating Open-World Exploration of MLLM Agents in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30931)
*Tianjie Ju, Yueqing Sun, Zheng Wu, Wei Zhang, Yaqi Huo, Xi Su, Qi Gu, Xunliang Cai, Gongshen Liu, Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MineExplorer 是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）智能体在 Minecraft 开放世界中探索能力的基准。它通过过滤依赖特定游戏知识的原子任务、构建 ReAct 风格的多跳任务，并采用多智能体合成工作流生成可靠实例，揭示了开放世界探索对当前 MLLM 智能体仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于游戏和具身智能的基准通常将交互压缩为短时任务，或与特定游戏机制的成功纠缠在一起，难以评估 MLLM 智能体在动态开放世界中的持续探索能力。因此需要一个新的基准来系统评估开放世界探索能力。

Method: 首先过滤原子任务，去除依赖 Minecraft 特定知识的任务以反映通用开放世界推理。然后围绕 ReAct 风格能力公式组织基准，将原子任务组合成隐式多跳任务。采用多智能体合成工作流联合设计任务图、沙盒场景和基于规则的里程碑评估器，以构建可靠实例。

Result: 人类评估表明多智能体合成工作流比单智能体基线产生更可靠的实例。实验显示开放世界探索仍具挑战性：强模型能处理许多单跳任务，但在需要协调隐藏先决条件的长轨迹任务中性能急剧下降。任务难度与智能体完成度相关，更大的模型或思维模式并不一致地带来更好性能。

Conclusion: MineExplorer 基准有效揭示了当前 MLLM 智能体在开放世界探索中的局限性，特别是长轨迹多跳推理的挑战，为未来研究提供了有价值的评估平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MineExplorer 基准，用于评估 MLLM 智能体在 Minecraft 开放世界中的探索能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Evaluates MLLM agents in open-world exploration but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: MLLM agent benchmark, Minecraft open-world, multi-agent synthesis, ReAct-style tasks, limited to multimodal understanding evaluation

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong capabilities in perception, reasoning, and action generation. However, their ability to sustain exploration in dynamic open worlds remains unclear. Existing embodied and game-based benchmarks often compress interaction into short-horizon tasks or entangle success with domain-specific game mechanics. In this paper, we introduce MineExplorer benchmark for evaluating open-world exploration capabilities of MLLM agents in Minecraft. We first filter atomic tasks whose solutions rely heavily on Minecraft-specific knowledge to better reflect general open-world reasoning. Then we organize the benchmark around a ReAct-style capability formulation and compose atomic tasks into implicit multi-hop tasks. To further construct reliable instances, MineExplorer uses a multi-agent synthesis workflow that jointly designs task graphs, sandbox scenes, and rule-based milestone evaluators. Human evaluation shows that the multi-agent synthesis workflow produces significantly more reliable instances than a single-agent baseline. Experiments with advanced MLLM agents show that open-world exploration remains challenging, as strong models can handle many single-hop tasks but degrade sharply when hidden prerequisites must be coordinated over longer trajectories. Further analysis finds that task difficulty tracks agent completion, and larger models or thinking modes do not consistently translate into better performance. Code and dataset are available at .

</details>


### [95] [BilliardPhys-Bench: Benchmarking Physical Reasoning and Visual Dynamics of Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30900)
*Ben Wang, Xiaogang Li, Ruochen Gao, Peiyao Xiao, Chengliang Xu, Zeyu Wang, Zichao Chen, Bing Zhao, Hu Wei*

Main category: physics.app-ph, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 BilliardPhys-Bench，一个基于合成台球环境的物理推理评测基准，用于评估多模态大模型在单帧图像中预测物体运动与交互的能力。基准包含碰撞预测、墙壁反弹和最终位置估计三个任务，并发现模型存在"静态偏差"——当物理结果难以推断时倾向于预测无交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型在静态图像识别上表现良好，但在直观物理推理方面仍然薄弱。现有评测缺乏对模型从单帧图像预测物体运动与交互能力的系统性评估，因此需要专门的物理推理基准来揭示模型的不足。

Method: 设计了一个程序化生成的合成台球环境，包含摩擦和弹性碰撞的物理模拟。基准包含三个子任务：(1) 球与球碰撞预测，(2) 墙壁反弹推理，(3) 运动停止后最终球位估计。使用程序引擎随机生成不同时间步长和场景复杂度的场景，评估了 GPT、Claude、Gemini、Qwen 系列的最新 MLLM。

Result: 随着模拟时间增加和场景几何复杂度上升，模型性能显著下降。发现了一致的失败模式"静态偏差"（stasis bias）：当正确物理结果难以推断时，模型倾向于预测无交互发生。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大模型在视觉动力学推理方面存在明显不足，尤其是在长时序和复杂场景下。研究指出了多模态架构需要更好的物理归纳偏置，为未来改进提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个物理推理评测基准 BilliardPhys-Bench，用于评估多模态大模型在台球物理场景中的视觉动力学推理能力。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 canonical 子主题。同时，该工作也涉及物理世界建模与推理，与 world_model 方向下的 interactive_physical_world 子主题相关。

Importance reason: Benchmark reveals static bias in MLLMs but is a physics benchmark, not directly advancing the user's priority subtopics. Signals: physical reasoning benchmark, multimodal LLM evaluation, stasis bias, synthetic billiards, not continuous language or video generation

Abstract: Current multimodal models handle static image recognition well, but intuitive physical reasoning remains a weakness. Predicting how objects will move and interact from a single image is still difficult for these systems. We present BilliardPhys-Bench, a benchmark for physical reasoning in synthetic billiards environments. Its procedural engine generates randomized scenarios with friction and elastic collisions. The benchmark tests three abilities: (1) predicting ball-to-ball collisions, (2) reasoning about wall bounces, and (3) estimating final ball positions after motion stops. We evaluate recent MLLMs from the GPT, Claude, Gemini, and Qwen families. Performance drops as simulation time increases and scene geometry grows more complex. We also observe a consistent failure mode we call "stasis bias": when the correct physical outcome is harder to infer, models tend to predict no interaction. These findings show where current MLLMs break down on visual dynamics and point toward the need for better physical inductive biases in multimodal architectures.

</details>


### [96] [GraphARC: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Graph-Based Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31031)
*Saku Peltonen, August Bøgh Rønberg, Andreas Plesner, Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GraphARC 是一个基于图结构的抽象推理基准，将 ARC 的少样本变换学习范式推广到图数据上，涵盖局部、全局和层次化图变换。评估显示当前语言模型在图属性问答上表现尚可，但在完整图变换任务上存在明显的"理解-执行鸿沟"，且在大规模实例上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抽象推理基准（如 ARC）局限于网格或文本格式，缺乏对图结构数据的系统评测。图结构数据在现实世界中广泛存在（如知识图谱、分子图、社交网络），需要一个能够评估模型在图上的抽象推理能力的标准化基准。

Method: GraphARC 将 ARC 的少样本变换学习范式推广到图数据上。每个任务包含少量输入-输出图对，模型需推断变换规则并应用于新测试图。基准覆盖局部（如节点/边添加删除）、全局（如图同构变换）和层次化（如子图合并）三类图变换。支持跨不同图族和大小的规模化生成，便于系统评估泛化能力。

Result: 在多个 SOTA 语言模型上的评估显示：模型能回答图属性相关问题，但在完整图变换任务上表现不佳，存在"理解-执行鸿沟"；在更大图实例上性能进一步下降，暴露出扩展性瓶颈。

Conclusion: GraphARC 提供了一个结合节点分类、链接预测和图生成的统一评测框架，揭示了当前模型在图结构抽象推理上的局限性，可作为未来图基础模型的有前景的测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 GraphARC，一个基于图结构的抽象推理基准，属于多模态/多领域评测基准范畴。虽然不直接涉及视觉或语言模态，但作为抽象推理评测基准，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark) 主题高度契合，该主题涵盖评测基准、新数据集与评测协议。

Importance reason: GraphARC is a new graph-based reasoning benchmark, but it is not aligned with the user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or efficient inference. Signals: Graph-based abstract reasoning benchmark, Not in priority subtopics, No boost keywords matched, Negative keywords not present, Low personal relevance to user's configured direction weights

Abstract: Relational reasoning lies at the heart of intelligence, but existing benchmarks are typically confined to formats such as grids or text. We introduce GraphARC, a benchmark for abstract reasoning on graph-structured data. GraphARC generalizes the few-shot transformation learning paradigm of the Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC). Each task requires inferring a transformation rule from a few input-output pairs and applying it to a new test graph, covering local, global, and hierarchical graph transformations. Unlike grid-based ARC, GraphARC instances can be generated at scale across diverse graph families and sizes, enabling systematic evaluation of generalization abilities. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models on GraphARC and observe clear limitations. Models can answer questions about graph properties but often fail to solve the full graph transformation task, revealing a comprehension-execution gap. Performance further degrades on larger instances, exposing scaling barriers. More broadly, by combining aspects of node classification, link prediction, and graph generation within a single framework, GraphARC provides a promising testbed for future graph foundation models.

</details>


### [97] [FAM-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Condition-Aware Food-as-Medicine Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31410)
*Mingyang Mao, Bhargav Rishi Medisetti, Utkarsh Grover, Tanvir Ibrahim, Wenyan Li, Tingting Zhang, Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (46.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FAM-Bench 是一个面向"食物即药物"场景的多模态基准，包含 2500 个营养专家验证的实例，覆盖 13 种饮食相关健康状况，通过菜肴适宜性评估和比较分析两个任务，评测语言和视觉语言模型在健康约束下的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食物 AI 基准主要评估菜肴识别、食谱理解、营养估算或一般营养问答，缺乏对"特定食物是否适合特定健康状况"这一健康感知决策层的系统评测。

Method: 构建了包含 2500 个营养专家验证实例的多模态基准 FAM-Bench，覆盖 13 种饮食相关健康状况。设计两个互补任务：(1) 菜肴级适宜性评估——模型根据图像和配料列表判断菜肴是否适合某种健康状况；(2) 比较菜肴分析——模型对四个候选菜肴按条件适宜性排序。

Result: 论文未在摘要中报告具体实验结果，但指出该基准为语言和视觉语言模型在基于证据的健康感知推理方面提供了标准化测试平台。

Conclusion: FAM-Bench 填补了食物 AI 在健康感知决策评测方面的空白，为评估模型在临床营养约束下的多模态推理能力提供了标准化基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向"食物即药物"场景的多模态评测基准 FAM-Bench，包含菜肴适宜性评估和比较分析两个任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Niche food-health benchmark with limited relevance to core continuous multimodal or generative world model priorities. Signals: domain-specific benchmark, food-as-medicine, health-constrained reasoning, multimodal evaluation, low alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Food-as-Medicine requires models to reason beyond what a dish is or what nutrition it contains: they must decide whether a concrete food choice is appropriate for a specific health condition. Existing food AI benchmarks primarily evaluate dish recognition, recipe understanding, nutrient estimation, or general nutrition question answering, leaving this health-aware decision layer largely untested. We introduce FAM-Bench, a multi-modal Food-as-Medicine benchmark with 2500 nutrition-expert-verified instances across 13 diet-related health conditions. The benchmark contains two complementary tasks: dish-level suitability assessment, where models judge whether a dish is suitable for a condition from its image and ingredient list, and comparative dish analysis, where models rank four candidate dishes by condition-specific suitability. Both tasks require integrating ingredient evidence, visual preparation cues, and clinical nutrition constraints, providing a standardized testbed for grounded health-aware reasoning in language and vision-language models.

</details>


### [98] [A Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31351)
*Yi Zhao, Siqi Wang, Zhe Hu, Yushi Li, Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VIABLE，首个面向视障辅助（VIA）场景的 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测基准，包含 30 万+ 判断样本和 12 种失效模式分类体系。系统评测 7 个 VLM 裁判后发现现有模型在有效性、公正性和稳定性三个维度均不可靠，最强的 GPT-5.4 单故障诊断准确率仅 52.6%，自偏好率高达 94.2%。为此提出 VIA-Judge-Agent，通过视觉证据提取和分类引导工作流提升诊断准确率和下游 VIA 响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 AI 的视障辅助（VIA）任务依赖人工评估，成本高昂。VLM-as-a-Judge 范式在通用领域有潜力，但在 VIA 场景中是否可信尚不清楚，因此需要系统评测 VLM 裁判在 VIA 任务上的可靠性。

Method: 构建 VIABLE 基准，包含 300K+ 判断样本，覆盖三个场景，提出 Effectiveness-Impartiality-Stability 评估框架和 12 种失效模式分类。系统评测 7 个不同规模的 VLM 裁判。提出 VIA-Judge-Agent，一种模型无关的推理时增强方法，结合视觉证据提取和分类引导工作流来提升裁判性能。

Result: 现有 VLM 裁判在 VIA 场景中全面不可靠：GPT-5.4 单故障诊断准确率仅 52.6%，自偏好率 94.2%；开源模型存在严重偏见且对抗脆弱。VIA-Judge-Agent 能有效提升诊断准确率和下游 VIA 响应质量。

Conclusion: VLM-as-a-Judge 在视障辅助场景中尚不可靠，需要专门的评测基准和增强方法。VIA-Judge-Agent 提供了一种有效的推理时增强方案，为未来 VIA 场景的自动评估提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 VIABLE，首个面向视障辅助（VIA）场景的 VLM-as-a-Judge 评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM-as-a-Judge for visually impaired assistance, which is a niche evaluation benchmark not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: VLM-as-a-Judge benchmark, visually impaired assistance, failure taxonomy, low diagnostic accuracy, self-preference bias

Abstract: AI-based Visually Impaired Assistance (VIA) remains challenging, largely due to the high cost of human evaluation. The VLM-as-a-Judge paradigm may offer a promising alternative, although it has mostly been studied in general domains. We therefore ask whether such judges can be trusted for VIA tasks. To investigate this question, we introduce VIABLE (Visually Impaired Assistance Benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation), the first benchmark for VLM-as-a-Judge evaluation in VIA. VIABLE contains over 300K judgment samples across three scenarios and introduces an Effectiveness--Impartiality--Stability framework with a 12-mode failure taxonomy. Based on VIABLE, our systematic study of seven judges across different model scales shows that existing models are largely unreliable across all evaluation axes. The strongest judge, GPT-5.4, achieves only 52.6% single-failure diagnostic accuracy, yet exhibits the highest self-preference rate at 94.2%; while open-source judges are strongly biased and adversarially fragile. To address these issues, we propose VIA-Judge-Agent, a model-agnostic inference-time harness that augments judges with visual evidence extraction and a taxonomy-guided workflow. It enables positive improvements in diagnostic accuracy and downstream VIA responses more preferred by BLV users. Data and code are available at:

</details>


### [99] [ERGeoBench:A Comprehensive Benchmark for Embodied Reasoning and Geo-localization in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31251)
*Kaiwen Xue, Tao Wei, Guoxin Zhang, Zhonghong Ou, Kaoyan Lu, Yu Feng, Yifan Zhu, Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (68.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ERGeoBench是一个面向具身地理定位的多模态大模型诊断基准，包含2207个全球分布的街景全景图，在单视图、全景视图和具身视图三种渐进设置下评估模型的感知、空间推理、常识推理和地理定位能力。实验表明当前MLLM能推断高层地理语义，但在细粒度感知、度量定位和跨视图空间一致性上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在具身智能方面展现出潜力，但具身地理定位（embodied geo-localization）由于缺乏细粒度评测而未被充分探索。现有基准无法系统评估模型在主动获取观测（通过偏航、俯仰、缩放等操作）条件下的地理定位能力。

Method: 构建了包含2207个全球分布街景全景图的ERGeoBench基准，设计了单视图、全景视图和具身视图三种渐进式评测设置，从基础感知、空间意识、常识推理和地理定位推理四个互补能力维度对MLLM进行系统评估。

Result: 当前领先的闭源和开源MLLM能推断高层地理语义（如国家/城市级别），但在细粒度感知操作、度量定位和跨视图空间一致性上表现不佳。地理定位能力与其他三个能力维度高度相关，表明准确定位依赖于感知、空间推理和常识推理的整合。

Conclusion: ERGeoBench为诊断和推进类人具身地理定位提供了统一框架，揭示了当前MLLM在该任务上的关键瓶颈，并为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了ERGeoBench，一个面向具身地理定位的多模态大模型诊断基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。论文评估了MLLM在感知、空间推理、常识推理和地理定位四个维度的能力，完全符合该规范子主题的定义。

Importance reason: Benchmark for embodied geo-localization is niche and not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or generation. Signals: Embodied geo-localization benchmark, MLLM evaluation, fine-grained perception, spatial reasoning, not generation-focused

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential as embodied agents, yet embodied geo-localization remains underexplored due to the lack of fine-grained evaluation. We introduce ERGeoBench, a diagnostic benchmark for vision-driven embodied geo-localization. ERGeoBench evaluates models under three progressive settings -- single-view, panorama-view, and embodied-view -- where agents may actively acquire observations through sequential changes in yaw, pitch, and zoom. The benchmark contains 2,207 globally distributed street-view panoramas and measures four complementary capabilities: foundational perception, spatial awareness, common sense reasoning, and geo-localization reasoning. Evaluations of leading proprietary and open-source MLLMs show that current models can infer high-level geographic semantics, but still struggle with fine-grained perceptual operations, metric localization, and spatial consistency across views. We further observe that geo-localization is strongly correlated with the other capability dimensions, suggesting that accurate localization depends on integrated perception, spatial reasoning, and commonsense inference rather than isolated visual recognition. Overall, ERGeoBench provides a unified framework for diagnosing and advancing human-like embodied geo-localization. Project Page:

</details>


### [100] [SVI-Bench: A Dynamic Microworld for Strategic Video Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31529)
*Yulu Pan, Han Yi, Seongsu Ha, Md Mohaiminul Islam, Benjamin Zhang, Lorenzo Torresani, Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (59.19) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SVI-Bench 是一个大规模基准，利用团队体育作为动态微世界，结合真实多智能体交互的复杂性与规则的可验证性，构建了约 35K 小时广播视频、15M 标注动作、15K 小时解说等密集语料，设计了从动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟到智能体综合的 9 个渐进式任务。评测发现现有最强模型在感知任务上约 73%，但在智能体任务上仅 5%，揭示了当前多模态模型在战略视频智能上的能力断层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准要么使用真实视频但缺乏因果和战略问题的可验证真值，要么使用合成环境但牺牲了真实多智能体系统的复杂性。作者希望填补这一空白，构建一个兼具真实复杂性和可验证性的战略视频智能评测基准。

Method: 利用团队体育（篮球、足球、冰球）作为动态微世界，通过数据引擎将原始比赛数据转化为密集交叉引用的语料库，包含约 35K 小时广播视频、15M 标注动作、15K 小时解说、23K 比赛报告和 103K 结构化统计记录。设计了 9 个任务，按四层递进体系组织：动态场景理解、因果推理、战略模拟和智能体综合。

Result: 强多模态和智能体基线在感知任务上表现尚可（细粒度动作 QA 约 73%），但在每个更高认知层级上性能急剧下降。智能体任务最难：最强模型在需要自主收集和整合 180 万片段语料证据时仅达 5% 准确率。

Conclusion: 当前多模态模型在战略视频智能上存在显著能力断层，从感知到因果推理、模拟和自主决策的每个层级都面临巨大挑战，SVI-Bench 为这一方向提供了系统化的评测平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SVI-Bench，一个面向战略视频智能（从感知到因果推理、模拟到策略规划）的多层次评测基准，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for strategic video intelligence but lacks direct alignment with your priority subtopics on continuous models and generation. Signals: Large-scale benchmark, strategic video intelligence, capability cliff, multimodal evaluation, team sports microworld

Abstract: True video intelligence demands more than recognizing what is visible: it requires reasoning about why events unfold, predicting what would change under different conditions, and deciding what to do next. We refer to this progression, from perception through causal reasoning and simulation to strategic planning, as Strategic Video Intelligence (SVI). No existing benchmark evaluates this capability stack: in-the-wild videos lack verifiable ground truth for causal and strategic questions, while synthetic environments sacrifice the complexity of real multi-agent systems. To bridge this gap, we introduce SVI-Bench, a large-scale benchmark that leverages team sports as a dynamic microworld, combining the complexity of real-world multi-agent interaction (10-22 agents making coordinated decisions under adversarial pressure) with the verifiability of explicit rules and definitive outcomes. SVI-Bench comprises approximately 35K hours of broadcast video, 15M annotated actions, 15K hours of expert commentary, 23K game reports, and 103K structured statistical records across basketball, soccer, and hockey, all constructed via a data engine that transforms raw game data into a dense, cross-referenced corpus. We organize evaluation into 9 tasks spanning a progressive four-pillar hierarchy: Dynamic Scene Understanding, Causal Reasoning, Strategic Simulation, and Agentic Synthesis. Evaluating strong multimodal and agentic baselines, we find a capability cliff: models perform competently on perceptual tasks, achieving approximately 73% on fine-grained action QA, but degrade sharply at each successive cognitive level. Agentic tasks prove hardest: the strongest model achieves only 5% accuracy when required to autonomously gather and integrate evidence across a corpus of 1.8M clips.

</details>


### [101] [nuReasoning: A Reasoning-Centric Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Tail Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31572)
*Zhiyu Huang, Johnson Liu, Rui Song, Zewei Zhou, Ruining Yang, Yun Zhang, Tianhui Cai, Hanyin Zhang, Mingxuan Gao, Valeria Xu, Jiali Chen, Yishan Shen, Yiluan Guo, Tony, Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: nuReasoning 是一个大规模真实世界自动驾驶推理数据集与基准，包含 20,000 个 20 秒长的视频片段，覆盖多城市多传感器数据，提供空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理的人工验证标注。实验表明，在该数据集上微调 VLM 可显著提升驾驶问答性能，且将推理监督融入 VLA 训练能改善规划表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集和基准主要关注感知、预测或规划，缺乏对长尾场景中推理能力的充分监督。自动驾驶系统需要在复杂长尾场景中运用常识、理解空间关系、推断交互并做出安全决策，因此需要一个专门面向推理的自动驾驶数据集和基准。

Method: 基于 nuScenes/nuPlan 数据，构建了 20,000 个 20 秒长的视频片段，包含多摄像头图像、LiDAR、HD 地图和物体标注，并添加了人工验证的推理标注（空间推理、决策推理、反事实推理）。支持推理评估和规划评估两种任务。在 VLM 微调和 VLA 训练中验证了推理监督的有效性。

Result: 在 nuReasoning 上微调 VLM 显著提升了驾驶特定问答性能；将推理监督融入 VLA 训练后，即使推理时关闭文本推理输出，规划性能仍得到改善。

Conclusion: nuReasoning 为评估和改进长尾场景中鲁棒、可解释、推理驱动的自动驾驶系统提供了基础数据集和基准，证明了推理监督对驾驶性能的积极作用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 nuReasoning，一个面向长尾自动驾驶场景的推理数据集与评测基准，包含空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理标注，并支持推理评估与规划评估。这属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on autonomous driving reasoning, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Autonomous driving reasoning dataset, Long-tail scenarios, VLM fine-tuning for driving, Planning improvement via reasoning, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.

</details>


### [102] [SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31597)
*Olaf Dünkel, Basavaraj Sunagad, Haoran Wang, David T. Hoffmann, Christian Theobalt, Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SOCO是一个新的语义对象对应（Semantic Object Correspondence）基准，包含100个类别、超过100万对应标注对，并引入对应类型分类学和关键点语言描述。实验表明视觉基础模型编码了强语义结构但跨类别对应能力弱，LVLM在文本引导定位上优于视觉参考跨图像匹配，且对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测下游密集任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型的评测缺乏对结构化对象理解（特别是部件级语义对应）的系统评估，已有的语义对应基准存在标注不一致、类别覆盖有限、缺乏语言描述等问题，无法全面评测模型在跨实例、跨类别、大外观/视角/几何变化下的部件匹配能力。

Method: 构建了SOCO基准：定义对应类型分类学（实例级、类别级、功能级对应），在100个类别上标注功能上有意义的关键点，生成超过100万对应对，并为每个关键点提供语言描述。基于此基准系统评测了多种视觉基础模型（DINOv2、MAE、CLIP等）和LVLM（GPT-4o、LLaVA等）的语义对应能力。

Result: (i) 视觉基础骨干编码了强语义结构，但跨相关类别传递对应能力差，仅部分捕获对象部件位置；(ii) LVLM在文本提示的部件定位上强于视觉参考跨图像匹配，揭示了语言引导定位与细粒度视觉对应之间的差距；(iii) 对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测分割、跟踪、3D姿态估计和3D检测等密集下游任务的表现。

Conclusion: SOCO可作为评估视觉和多模态基础模型结构化、部件级表征质量的基准，揭示了当前模型在细粒度语义对应方面的局限性，并为未来研究提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了SOCO基准，用于评测视觉基础模型和大视觉语言模型（LVLM）在语义对象对应（semantic object correspondence）方面的细粒度部件级理解能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for vision foundation models but tangential to user's focus on continuous multimodal models and generation. Signals: Semantic correspondence benchmark, Vision foundation model evaluation, LVLM part-level understanding, Dense task prediction, Not generation or continuous models

Abstract: Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-efficient_multimodal'></div>

### [103] [PEEK: Picking Essential frames via Efficient Knowledge distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31029)
*Killian Steunou, Anas Filali Razzouki, Khalil Guetari, Mounîm A. El-Yacoubi, Yannis Tevissen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 普通相关 (59.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PEEK 是一种高效的动态帧采样方法，通过知识蒸馏将教师模型对帧重要性的排序能力迁移到轻量级时序模型，仅依赖视觉内容即可选择视频中最具信息量的帧用于视频字幕生成。在 ActivityNet Captions 和 MSR-VTT 上，PEEK 在低帧预算下优于现有方法，且计算开销极小（仅增加 5.2% 的字幕生成时间）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型只能处理有限数量的帧，帧选择成为高效视频字幕生成的关键瓶颈。现有方法多采用均匀采样（内容无关）或自适应采样（计算昂贵），缺乏一种既准确又高效的帧选择方案。

Method: PEEK 首先使用一个更强的教师模型（基于视频-语言模型）对视频帧进行字幕条件的重要性排序，然后将这种排序能力通过知识蒸馏迁移到一个轻量级的时序模型（仅基于视觉特征），使得推理时无需字幕信息即可快速预测帧重要性，从而动态选择最关键的帧。

Result: 在 ActivityNet Captions 上，PEEK 在 16 种配置中赢得 14 种，尤其在仅选择 1-2 帧时表现最佳。在 MSR-VTT 的零样本评估中，低帧预算下迁移效果最好。效率方面，PEEK 仅增加 5.2% 的字幕生成时间，远低于 CSTA（65.4%）和 MaxInfo（211.9%）。

Conclusion: PEEK 证明了通过知识蒸馏实现高效、准确的动态帧采样是可行的，在低帧预算下尤其有效，为视频语言模型的高效推理提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出高效的动态帧采样方法 PEEK，通过知识蒸馏将教师模型的帧重要性排序迁移到轻量时序模型，旨在减少视频语言模型处理的帧数从而提升效率。这属于多模态模型的高效化与轻量化，与 CANONICAL 子主题 "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Efficient frame selection for video captioning is useful but not central to the user's priority subtopics on continuous models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: Efficient video frame sampling, Knowledge distillation for frame ranking, Low computational overhead, Strong performance on ActivityNet Captions, Limited direct relevance to continuous language/multimodal models or autoregressive generation

Abstract: Video-language models can process only a limited number of frames, making frame selection a key bottleneck for efficient video captioning. Most captioning pipelines still rely on uniform sampling, which is computationally cheap but agnostic to visual content. Adaptive frame sampling has recently emerged as a promising approach for selecting the most informative frames from a video; however, existing methods remain computationally expensive. We introduce PEEK, an efficient dynamic frame sampling method that distills caption-conditioned frame relevance rankings from a stronger teacher model into a lightweight temporal model that operates only on visual content. We find that, overall, on ActivityNet Captions and MSR-VTT, our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all evaluated downstream vision language models, especially when only one or two frames are selected for captioning, obtaining the best CIDEr for most frame budgets. On ActivityNet Captions, PEEK is particularly strong, winning 14 out of 16 configurations. Zero-shot evaluation on MSR-VTT shows that our model transfers best at low frame budgets, while results at four and eight frames are more mixed as temporal coverage and visual diversity become increasingly competitive. Compared with recent adaptive baselines, PEEK is both more accurate in the low-budget regime and more efficient: it adds only $5.2\%$ to the captioning time, compared with $65.4\%$ for CSTA and $211.9\%$ for MaxInfo. We release our code and pre-trained checkpoint at .

</details>


### [104] [VisionPulse: Dynamic Visual Sparsity for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31457)
*Hengbo Xu, Shengjie Jin, Yanbiao Ma, Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (68.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisionPulse 提出了一种逐步视觉 token 剪枝框架，在推理过程中动态保留仅 5% 的关键视觉 token，同时将推理轨迹缩短 11.2%，几乎不损失精度，有效缓解了多模态大模型推理时的视觉冗余与计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型推理时视觉 token 数量庞大，现有方法通常在预填充阶段一次性剪枝，忽略了推理过程中视觉证据的动态变化。作者发现视觉证据具有强步骤依赖性，且冗余视觉上下文会引导模型关注无关区域，延长推理轨迹。因此需要一种在推理过程中动态剪枝视觉 token 的方法。

Method: VisionPulse 包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级视觉注意力质量（visual attention mass）估计，利用其与模型有效视觉 token 使用率的强正相关性，动态估计每步的保留预算；(2) 在该预算下仅保留最关键的视觉 token，实现逐步视觉稀疏化。通过强制推理过程中的视觉稀疏性，过滤冗余视觉上下文，同时保留相关视觉证据，自然缩短推理轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，VisionPulse 每步仅保留 5% 的视觉 token，推理轨迹缩短 11.2%，在多个多模态推理基准上精度几乎不变。

Conclusion: VisionPulse 验证了推理过程中视觉证据的动态性，并提出了一种高效的逐步剪枝方案，在保持精度的同时显著降低计算开销，为多模态大模型的高效部署提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VisionPulse，一种在推理过程中逐步剪枝视觉 token 的框架，旨在降低多模态大模型推理开销。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）范畴，具体涉及 token 压缩与稀疏推理，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient multimodal reasoning via dynamic visual sparsity, aligning with user's high priority on model efficiency and continuous multimodal models. Signals: dynamic visual token pruning, step-wise reasoning efficiency, multimodal LLM acceleration, visual redundancy reduction, inference-time optimization

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large multimodal models (LMMs), inference-time overhead has become a key bottleneck for real-world deployment. Existing methods typically prune visual tokens at prefill, assuming the required visual evidence remains static during reasoning. However, we empirically show that visual evidence is strongly step-dependent: only a sparse subset of visual tokens is critical at each decoding step, and the critical set evolves across reasoning. Furthermore, we identify a coupled bottleneck where redundant visual context can steer the model toward query-irrelevant regions, lengthening the reasoning trace. Guided by these insights, we propose VisionPulse, a step-wise visual token pruning framework during reasoning. VisionPulse computes a lightweight visual attention mass to estimate the step-wise retention budget by exploiting its strong positive correlation with LMMs' effective visual token usage and retain only the most critical tokens under this budget. By enforcing visual sparsity during reasoning, VisionPulse filters redundant visual context while preserving relevant visual evidence, shortening reasoning traces naturally. Extensive experiments show that VisionPulse only retains 5% of visual tokens per step with reasoning traces shortened by 11.2%, while keeping accuracy almost unchanged.

</details>

## 空间与 3D 多模态推理 [[Back]](#toc)
