### [105] [VLM3: Vision Language Models Are Native 3D Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30561)
*Zhipeng Cai, Zhuang Liu, Yunyang Xiong, Zechun Liu, Vikas Chandra, Yangyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (69.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VLM3 提出一种极简方法，通过焦距统一、文本像素参考和数据混合缩放，使标准 VLM 无需架构修改、大模型或复杂损失函数，即可在深度估计、像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级 3D 理解等任务上达到专家视觉模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 3D 理解任务仍依赖专家视觉模型和复杂的任务特定设计，而 VLM 虽然在语义理解上表现优异，但在 3D 任务上尚未充分展现能力。作者希望证明 VLM 本身是天然的 3D 学习者，只需简单方法即可解锁其 3D 能力。

Method: 提出 VLM3，核心包含三个关键设计：(1) 焦距统一（focal length unification），将不同焦距的图像归一化以消除尺度歧义；(2) 文本像素参考（text-based pixel reference），用文本 token 表示像素坐标，使 VLM 能通过文本输出进行精确的像素级 3D 预测；(3) 数据混合与缩放（data mixture and scaling），混合多种 3D 相关数据集进行训练。无需改变模型架构、使用大模型、复杂数据增强或回归损失。

Result: VLM3 将 VLM 深度估计精度从 0.84 提升至 0.9（SILog 指标），并在像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级 3D 理解等任务上达到与专家视觉模型相当的精度，同时保持标准 VLM 架构和基于文本的训练范式。

Conclusion: VLM 是天然的 3D 学习者，通过焦距统一、文本像素参考和数据混合缩放这三个简单设计，即可使标准 VLM 掌握多种 3D 任务，无需复杂的架构或损失设计。这为简单可扩展的 3D 学习开辟了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何让标准 VLM 通过简单方法（焦距统一、文本像素参考、数据混合与缩放）掌握深度估计、像素对应、相机位姿估计和物体级 3D 理解等 3D 任务，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的空间与 3D 多模态推理（spatial_3d_reasoning）范畴。

Importance reason: Shows standard VLMs can master 3D tasks without complex designs, aligning with user's interest in unified multimodal models and efficient methods. Signals: unified multimodal model, 3D understanding, scalable method, standard VLM, depth estimation

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) enable a unified model to solve various vision tasks through prompting. They have shown promising performance in semantic understanding. However, 3D understanding still largely relies on expert vision models with complex task-specific designs. The key argument this work wants to make is that VLMs are native 3D learners. Our in-depth large scale study shows that 1) focal length unification, 2) text-based pixel reference and 3) data mixture and scaling, are all you need for effective 3D learning. Model architecture changes, large models, heavy data augmentations, and complex losses including the regression formulation, many of which form the foundation of expert vision models, are actually not necessary conditions. As a result, we propose VLM3, a scalable method with the simplest design that enables standard VLMs to master diverse 3D tasks. VLM3 not only advances the VLM depth estimation accuracy by a large margin (0.84 -> 0.9), but also enables diverse 3D tasks such as pixel correspondence, camera pose estimation and object-level 3D understanding, matching expert vision model accuracy while maintaining standard architectures and text-based training. We believe VLM3 opens up a new paradigm for simple and scalable 3D learning.

</details>


### [106] [Multi-Turn Multi-Agent Dialogue for Collaborative Reconstruction Improves VLM Performance on Spatial Reasoning, But Only Barely](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31387)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti, Sherzod Hakimov, David Schlangen*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (52.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过一个协作式结构搭建任务，系统评估了多种VLM在空间推理、视觉定位和语言引导交互方面的能力。研究发现，VLM在视觉空间推理上仍存在显著困难，详细的文本描述比视觉输入更有效，分解式图像表示能提升性能，但整体表现仍然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在多样化环境中依赖视觉输入理解物体和空间布局，并在人机协作任务中通过语言交流。尽管VLM在视觉解释、问答和指令跟随方面有进展，但其在需要空间推理的协作对话任务中的能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 设计了一个协作式结构搭建任务框架，VLM通过多轮多智能体对话从视觉和文本输入中重建目标结构。评估了多种开源和闭源VLM，比较了不同交互设置、输入模态和图像表示方式对重建成功率的影响。

Result: VLM在视觉空间推理上表现困难；详细的文本表示比视觉输入带来更高的重建成功率；分解式图像表示（如分块图像）能提升性能；但整体上VLM在视觉空间定位和基于指令的生成方面仍存在明显局限。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在协作式空间推理任务中的能力仍然有限，视觉空间定位和指令生成是主要瓶颈。未来需要改进VLM的视觉空间理解能力，以更好地支持人机协作场景。

Classification reason: 论文研究VLM在协作式结构搭建任务中的空间推理能力，涉及视觉解释、空间定位和语言引导交互，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与3D多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）规范子主题。同时与具身世界模型（embodied_world_model）相关，因为任务涉及机器人协作场景中的视觉-语言-动作交互。

Importance reason: Evaluates VLM spatial reasoning but shows limited progress, not directly advancing priority subtopics. Signals: spatial reasoning, VLM evaluation, collaborative dialogue, limited performance, not priority subtopics

Abstract: Robots operating in diverse environments rely on visual input to interpret objects and spatial layouts. In human-collaborative tasks, they are expected to communicate this understanding through language. Vision-language models (VLMs) support robotic tasks involving visual interpretation, question answering, and instruction following, but their capabilities in collaborative dialogue tasks requiring spatial reasoning remain underexplored. We study this gap through a collaborative structure-building task that combines visual interpretation, grounding, language-guided interaction, and action generation. We develop a framework in which VLMs use dialogue to reconstruct a target structure from visual and textual inputs. We evaluate open-weight and closed VLMs across interaction settings, input modalities, and image representations. Results show that spatial reasoning over visual representations remains difficult for the evaluated VLMs. Detailed text representations of the target yield higher reconstruction success across modality conditions, while decomposed image representations improve performance. These findings reveal limits in visual spatial grounding and grounded instruction generation for collaborative VLM agents.

</details>


### [107] [SpatialAct: Probing Spatial Reasoning-to-Action Capabilities of VLM Agents in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31148)
*Tianhui Liu, Jie Feng, Zhiheng Zheng, Shengyuan Wang, Yiming Guo, Yanxin Xi, Hangyu Fan, Yong Li, Pan Hui*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (66.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SpatialAct 是一个基于模拟器的基准，用于探测 VLM 智能体在 3D 场景中的动作条件空间推理能力。实验发现当前 VLM 在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮交互中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠动作，存在明显的推理到动作的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松感知空间布局、形成认知表征、推理空间关系并将推理转化为动作。虽然 VLM 在观察条件下的空间感知和推理任务上表现不错，但它们能否构建连贯的空间理解、据此行动并通过多轮反馈优化动作仍不清楚。本文旨在系统探测 VLM 智能体的动作条件空间推理能力。

Method: 提出 SpatialAct 基准，包含最困难的多轮交互精炼（Multi-turn Interactive Refinement）设置，以及分解后的单步错误检测与修复（Single-step Error Detection and Fix）任务，外加五项基础空间能力任务来诊断模型失败的潜在原因。使用模拟器生成 3D 场景并评估 VLM 智能体的空间推理到动作的能力。

Result: 实验揭示了清晰的推理到动作鸿沟：当前 VLM 在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮反馈中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠动作，性能显著低于人类。VLM 智能体在动作引起的环境变化下缺乏鲁棒的空间状态跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 智能体在 3D 场景中仍缺乏鲁棒的空间状态跟踪能力，即使抽象掉底层控制，也难以在动作引起的环境变化下维持连贯的空间信念。这表明从空间推理到可靠动作执行之间仍存在显著差距，需要进一步研究。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 在 3D 场景中的空间推理到动作的能力，属于空间推理与具身推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "空间与 3D 多模态推理" 高度匹配。同时涉及具身智能环境中的动作条件推理，也与世界模型方向相关。

Importance reason: Probes spatial reasoning-to-action gap in VLM agents, relevant to multimodal understanding and world model priorities. Signals: VLM spatial reasoning benchmark, multi-turn interactive refinement, reasoning-to-action gap, 3D scene understanding, simulator-grounded evaluation

Abstract: Humans can effortlessly perceive spatial layouts, form cognitive representations, reason about spatial relations, and translate such reasoning into actions in everyday 3D environments. Although recent vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance on observation-conditioned spatial perception and reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they can build coherent spatial understanding, act upon it, and refine their actions through multi-turn feedback. To study this problem, we introduce \textbf{SpatialAct}, a simulator-grounded benchmark for probing \textit{action-conditioned spatial reasoning} in 3D scenes. Starting from the most challenging setting, Multi-turn Interactive Refinement, we further design its decomposed counterpart, Single-step Error Detection and Fix, together with five fundamental spatial ability tasks to diagnose the underlying causes of model failures. Experiments reveal a clear reasoning-to-action gap: current VLMs can perform well on isolated spatial reasoning tasks, but struggle to maintain coherent spatial beliefs and produce reliable actions during multi-turn feedback, substantially underperforming humans. These results suggest that current VLM agents still lack robust spatial state tracking under action-induced environment changes, even when low-level control is abstracted away.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-unified_multimodal_model'></div>

## 统一多模态模型 [[Back]](#toc)

理解与生成一体化、统一 token 表征、Any-to-Any 多模态模型。

### [108] [Lumos-Nexus: Efficient Frequency Bridging with Homogeneous Latent Space for Video Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31603)
*Jiazheng Xing, Hangjie Yuan, Lingling Cai, Xinyu Liu, Yujie Wei, Fei Du, Hai Ci, Tao Feng, Jiasheng Tang, Weihua Chen, Fan Wang, Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Lumos-Nexus 提出了一种训练高效的视频统一模型框架，采用两阶段设计：训练时仅对齐轻量生成器与理解模块，推理时通过统一渐进式频率桥接（UPFB）在共享潜空间中逐步切换到高容量预训练生成器，实现粗到细的高保真视频生成。同时提出 VR-Bench 基准评估推理驱动的视频生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 connector 的视频统一模型在指令引导的视频合成方面表现良好，但将大型高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中计算成本过高，限制了可实现的视觉质量。因此需要一种训练高效的框架，既能发展强大的推理驱动生成能力，又能显著提升视觉保真度。

Method: Lumos-Nexus 采用两阶段设计：1）训练阶段，仅将轻量生成器与理解模块对齐，学习接收推理驱动的语义控制信号；2）推理阶段，引入统一渐进式频率桥接（UPFB），在共享潜空间中逐步将生成任务移交给高容量预训练生成器，实现从粗到细的细化，生成高保真视频而不损害推理质量。同时构建了 VR-Bench 基准来评估推理驱动的视频生成能力。

Result: 在 VBench 上，Lumos-Nexus 在视觉真实感和时序连贯性方面取得了显著提升；在 VR-Bench 上展示了强大的基于推理的生成性能。

Conclusion: Lumos-Nexus 通过训练高效的 connector 框架和渐进式频率桥接策略，有效解决了统一视频模型中高保真生成器集成困难的问题，在推理驱动的视频生成任务上实现了视觉质量与推理能力的双赢。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Lumos-Nexus，一个基于 connector 的视频统一模型框架，将理解模块与轻量生成器对齐训练，推理时通过渐进式频率桥接（UPFB）切换到高保真生成器，实现推理驱动的视频生成。这属于理解与生成一体化（统一多模态模型）方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] unified_multimodal_model。同时涉及视频生成，也匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses unified multimodal models and efficient inference, both high-priority subtopics, with a novel frequency bridging method. Signals: unified multimodal model, efficient inference, video generation, reasoning-driven generation, VR-Bench benchmark

Abstract: Connector-based video unified models have demonstrated strong capability in instruction-grounded video synthesis, but integrating a large high-fidelity generator into the unified training loop is computationally prohibitive, limiting achievable visual quality. We therefore propose Lumos-Nexus, a training-efficient unified video generation framework that facilitates the development of strong reasoning-driven generation capabilities while significantly enhancing visual fidelity. Lumos-Nexus adopts a two-stage design: 1) During training, only a lightweight generator is aligned with the understanding block to learn to take in reasoning-driven semantic control. 2) During inference, we introduce Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging (UPFB) to progressively hand off generation to a high-capacity pretrained generator in the shared latent space, enabling coarse-to-fine refinement and producing high-fidelity videos without compromising reasoning quality. To fill the gap in reasoning-driven video generation benchmarks, we introduce VR-Bench, which assesses a model's capability to translate inferred intent into coherent and semantically aligned video content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Lumos-Nexus achieves substantial gains in visual realism and temporal coherence on VBench, while exhibiting strong reasoning-based generative performance on VR-Bench. Code and models are available at .

</details>


### [109] [Representation Forcing for Bottleneck-Free Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31604)
*Yuqing Wang, Zhijie Lin, Ceyuan Yang, Yang Zhao, Fei Xiao, Hao He, Qi Zhao, Zihan Ding, Fuyun Wang, Shuai Wang, Youliang Zhang, Haoqi Fan, Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 技术，通过让解码器自回归预测视觉表征作为中间 token，再在相同骨干网络中进行像素扩散，消除了统一多模态模型对独立预训练 VAE 的依赖，实现了无瓶颈的端到端理解与生成统一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMM）仍依赖冻结的、单独预训练的 VAE 进行图像生成，这引入了结构瓶颈。直接移除 VAE 会导致质量下降，因为模型需要同时从原始像素中学习高层结构和低层细节。作者希望消除这一瓶颈，实现真正的端到端统一模型。

Method: Representation Forcing (RF) 方法：在生成过程中，解码器首先自回归地预测视觉表征（如 DINOv2 特征）作为中间 token，这些 token 保留在上下文中，然后引导同一骨干网络中的像素扩散过程。通过将表征从感知输出转变为生成目标，RF 消除了对外部生成潜空间的需求。

Result: 在图像生成方面，像素空间的 RF 模型达到了与基于 VAE 的最先进统一模型相当的性能。在图像理解方面，像素空间的 RF 模型通常优于其基于 VAE 的变体。RF 同时提升了理解与生成能力。

Conclusion: Representation Forcing 为构建无瓶颈的端到端统一多模态模型提供了有效途径，证明了在像素空间中通过预测视觉表征作为中间 token 可以同时提升理解与生成性能，为未来更简洁的统一多模态架构奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 方法，旨在消除统一多模态模型（UMM）中依赖外部 VAE 的结构瓶颈，实现像素空间中的理解与生成一体化。这直接对应 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model），同时涉及图像生成（multimodal_generation方向）。

Importance reason: Directly addresses bottleneck-free unified multimodal models with a novel representation forcing technique, highly aligned with your priority on unified multimodal models and continuous representations. Signals: Unified multimodal model, bottleneck-free, representation forcing, autoregressive visual tokens, pixel-space diffusion

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to handle perception and generation in a single model. Yet existing UMMs still rely on a frozen, separately pretrained VAE for image generation, imposing a structural bottleneck. Naively removing it introduces a quality gap, as the model must learn both high-level structure and low-level details from raw pixels. In this paper, we propose Representation Forcing (RF), a technique that closes this gap by making representation prediction a native capability of the model. Concretely, RF forces the decoder to autoregressively predict visual representations as intermediate tokens before pixels; these tokens then stay in context to guide pixel diffusion within the same backbone. By turning representations from perception outputs into generation targets, RF eliminates the need for any external generative latent space. We find that RF benefits both understanding and generation. On image generation, our pixel-space model with RF matches state-of-the-art VAE-based unified models. On image understanding, pixel-space RF generally outperforms its VAE-based variant. Together, these results offer an effective step toward end-to-end, bottleneck-free UMMs.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

通用视觉语言模型、MLLM 架构、多模态指令微调与对话。

### [110] [A Pilot Study on Curator-Guided Multilingual Art Description for Blind and Low-Vision Audiences with Small Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31080)
*Iosif Tsangko, Andreas Triantafyllopoulos, George Margetis, Ioana Crihana, Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.HC

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (59.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文探索了策展人引导的多语言艺术描述生成，使用 Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct 小模型为德语、罗马尼亚语和塞尔维亚语的盲人和低视力观众生成描述。通过构建平行 BLV 描述语料库，比较了语言特定 LoRA 适配器与多语言适配器的效果，发现语言特定适配器在罗马尼亚语和塞尔维亚语上表现更稳定，而多语言适配器在德语上仍有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人和低视力（BLV）观众在视觉艺术描述方面长期缺乏支持，尤其是在多语言场景和博物馆环境中，隐私和知识产权限制倾向于使用小型本地部署的 VLM 模型。现有研究缺乏对多语言 BLV 艺术描述的系统性探索。

Method: 构建了面向 BLV 的平行多语言艺术描述语料库（德语、罗马尼亚语、塞尔维亚语），使用 Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct 作为基础模型，比较了语言特定 LoRA 适配器与单一多语言 LoRA 适配器的效果。评估结合了自动词汇/嵌入指标和 LLM-as-Judge 协议，并针对罗马尼亚语进行了小规模 BLV 用户研究校准。

Result: 语言特定适配器在罗马尼亚语和塞尔维亚语上展现出更稳定的可控性和视觉基础描述质量；多语言适配器在德语上仍具有竞争力。研究为小型本地部署 VLM 在多语言无障碍场景中的应用提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 小型本地部署 VLM 在多语言 BLV 艺术描述中具有潜力，语言特定适配器在低资源语言上表现更优。未来需要更大规模的 BLV 用户研究和更广泛的语言覆盖来验证结论的普适性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究使用小型 VLM（Qwen2.5-VL-3B）进行多语言艺术描述生成，属于通用视觉语言模型在多语言、低资源场景下的应用与微调，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on a niche accessibility application with small VLMs, not aligning with the user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Multilingual art description, Blind and low-vision audiences, Small vision-language models, LoRA adapters, Low research value for core priorities

Abstract: Blind and low-vision (BLV) audiences remain underserved by visual art descriptions, particularly across languages and in museum settings where privacy and intellectual-property constraints may favour small on-premise vision-language models (VLMs). This pilot study investigates curator-guided multilingual art description with Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct for German, Romanian, and Serbian. We construct a parallel BLV-oriented caption corpus from artwork images and metadata, and compare language-specific LoRA adapters with a single multilingual adapter under a fixed backbone and training budget. Evaluation combines automatic lexical and embedding-based metrics with an LLM-as-Judge protocol calibrated against a small Romanian BLV pilot study. Under our pilot setup, language-specific adapters show more stable controllability and visually grounded description quality for Romanian and Serbian, while multilingual adaptation remains competitive in German. We frame these findings as deployment-oriented evidence for small on-premise VLMs, and highlight the need for larger BLV user studies and broader language coverage before drawing general conclusions about multilingual accessibility.

</details>


### [111] [Simple Token-Efficient Vision-Language Model for Case-level Pathology Synoptic Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30716)
*Zhiyuan Yang, Jiahao Cheng, Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh, Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 普通相关 (54.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种 token 高效的视觉语言模型，用于从全切片图像（WSI）生成病例级病理学结构化报告。模型采用三组件架构（冻结病理编码器 + 轻量 MLP 对齐器 + LLM 解码器），通过使用 5× 放大倍数下的 512×512 块将序列长度减少最多 64 倍，并引入显式的 WSI 标记 token 区分多切片病例。两阶段监督训练（WSI 描述预训练 + 病例级报告微调）在仅需半张 H100 GPU 的条件下实现了与强基线相当或更优的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从全切片图像（WSI）生成临床可用的病理报告极具挑战性，原因包括：WSI 具有千兆像素分辨率、视觉 token 序列过长、病例级推理复杂（一个病例可能包含多个 WSI，组织异质且发现模糊）。现有方法在有限 GPU 内存下难以实用，亟需一种 token 高效且计算可行的方案。

Method: 采用三组件架构：冻结的病理 patch 编码器（UNI）、轻量两层 MLP 视觉-语言对齐器、大语言模型解码器（Llama-3.1-8B）。引入显式 WSI 标记 token 区分病例内多个切片。使用 5× 放大倍数下的 512×512 块表示每个切片，相比常用的 20× 块将平均序列长度减少最多 64 倍。两阶段监督训练：(1) 对齐器 WSI 描述预训练，使用异质 WSI-文本对；(2) 病例级监督微调，使用病例-报告对进行结构化报告生成。

Result: 在 ROUGE-L/METEOR/BLEU-4 指标上取得高分，同时显著降低内存和运行时间。仅需半张 H100 GPU 即可进行实用训练。AI 评估中，模型持续优于强基线。消融实验刻画了性能-效率权衡，并识别出提升多 WSI 场景鲁棒性的简单选择。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个强大且可复现的基线，用于在有限计算资源下进行高效的病理报告生成，降低了多 WSI VLM 研究的门槛。简单设计（低放大倍数、轻量对齐器、显式 WSI 标记）在效率与性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一种面向病理学全切片图像（WSI）的 token 高效视觉语言模型，用于生成结构化病理报告。这属于通用视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）在医学领域的应用，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical pathology report generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keywords. Signals: medical domain, pathology report generation, token efficiency, low GPU requirement, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Generating clinically useful pathology reports for pathology cases from whole-slide images (WSIs) is challenging due to gigapixel resolution, long visual-token sequences, and the complexity of case-level reasoning, where a single case may contain multiple WSIs with heterogeneous tissues and ambiguous findings. We present a simple token-efficient vision--language model for case-level synoptic report generation that remains practical under constrained GPU memory. Our architecture follows a minimal three-component design: a frozen pathology patch encoder, a lightweight two-layer MLP vision-language aligner, and a large language model decoder, with an explicit WSI marker token to separate slides within a case. Training proceeds in two supervised stages: (1) aligner-only WSI captioning using heterogeneous WSI-text pairs, and (2) case-level supervised fine-tuning on case-report pairs for structured report generation. To reduce sequence length, we represent each slide using $512 \times 512$ patches at $5\times$ magnification, which reduces the average sequence length by up to $64\times$ times compared to the commonly used $20\times$ patches. Combined with efficient training techniques, we enable practical training with only half a NVIDIA H100 GPU. Across both training stages, our approach achieves high ROUGE-L/METEOR/BLEU-4 scores while being substantially more efficient in memory and runtime. In AI-based evaluations, our model is consistently preferred over strong baselines. Extensive ablations characterize performance-efficiency trade-offs and identify simple choices that improve robustness in multi-WSI settings. Overall, this work provides a strong, reproducible baseline for efficient pathology report generation, lowering the barrier to multi-WSI VLM research under limited compute.

</details>


### [112] [Learning to Adapt: Self-Improving Web Agent via Cognitive-Aware Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31365)
*Weile Chen, Bingchen Miao, Qifan Yu, Wendong Bu, Guoming Wang, Wenqiao Zhang, Shengyu Zhang, Juncheng Li, Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SCALE框架，通过Selector、Predictor、Judger三个对抗角色实现Web Agent的自主认知探索与自我改进，并引入SCALE-Hop图探索策略避免局部陷阱，构建SCALE-20k数据集，在多个MLLM上显著提升了Web Agent的性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web Agent依赖手工设计的执行流程或昂贵的专家轨迹，难以适应复杂动态环境，缺乏自主发现自身局限并扩展认知边界的能力。

Method: 提出SCALE框架，包含三个对抗角色：Selector（选择探索方向）、Predictor（预测执行结果）、Judger（评估认知边界）；提出SCALE-Hop图探索策略实现全局规划；基于探索轨迹构建SCALE-20k数据集用于微调。

Result: 在多个真实Web环境中，SCALE显著提升了多种MLLM（如GPT-4V、LLaVA等）作为Web Agent的任务完成率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCALE提供了一种可扩展、可泛化的自主Web Agent构建方案，通过认知感知探索有效提升了智能体在复杂动态环境中的适应能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于MLLM的Web Agent，通过自认知探索与环境交互提升智能体能力，属于通用视觉语言模型与MLLM的应用范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM"。

Importance reason: Focuses on web agent self-improvement via cognitive exploration, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Web agent, MLLM adaptation, cognitive exploration, SCALE framework, graph exploration

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have led to promising progress in web agents. However, existing web agents often rely on handcrafted execution pipelines or expensive expert trajectories, limiting their adaptability to complex, dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we propose SCALE (Self-Cognitive-Aware Learning and Exploration), which leverages three adversarial roles, Selector, Predictor, and Judger to autonomously discover the agent's limitations and expand its cognitive boundaries through environmental exploration. Moreover, we propose SCALE-Hop, a graph exploration strategy that facilitates global planning and helps agents avoid local exploration traps. To further support learning, we construct SCALE-20k, a large-scale dataset collected from 19 real-world websites, containing diverse task types and structured demonstrations generated from SCALE's exploration traces. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the performance and generalization of multiple MLLMs in various web environments. Our framework offers a scalable and generalizable solution for building truly autonomous and adaptive web agents.

</details>


### [113] [DiTTo: Scalable Order-aware All-in-One Image Restoration Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30915)
*Seungho Choi, Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (83.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DiTTo 是一个基于视觉语言模型的可扩展顺序感知图像恢复智能体框架，通过 DiTTo 模拟器高效构建最优恢复动作轨迹数据集，并引入顺序感知恢复对齐（ORA）实现即插即用的专家扩展，在最多五种并发退化的 MiO-100 评测集上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实图像通常同时存在多种退化（如模糊、噪声、低光照等），且去除退化的顺序对最终恢复质量有显著影响。现有基于智能体的图像恢复方法需要 O((N^D)^2) 次专家调用构建训练数据，且智能体与固定专家池耦合，无法灵活扩展新专家。因此需要一种高效且可扩展的智能体框架。

Method: 提出 DiTTo 框架，包含两个组件：(1) DiTTo 模拟器，结合 ∪S-IR（单步恢复动作模拟）和 AiO-IQA（每动作质量预测），将最优恢复动作轨迹数据集（ORTD）构建复杂度降至 O(N^D)；(2) DiTTo 智能体，基于 VLM 在模拟器生成的 ORTD 上进行 SFT 训练，随后进行顺序感知恢复对齐（ORA），沿退化识别、恢复动作排序和输出格式三个独立轴进行对齐，支持即插即用的专家扩展。

Result: 在 MiO-100 评测集（最多五种并发退化）上，DiTTo 智能体在多项多退化图像恢复指标上超越现有基于智能体的方法，达到 SOTA。同时，添加新恢复专家仅需更新轻量级 ORA 阶段，验证了可扩展性。

Conclusion: DiTTo 通过模拟器高效构建训练数据，结合顺序感知对齐，实现了高效、可扩展的基于 VLM 的多退化图像恢复智能体，为实际场景中的复杂图像恢复提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DiTTo，一个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的图像恢复智能体框架，使用 VLM 调度多个恢复专家并按序处理多种退化。这属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 的应用，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" (vlm_mllm_general)。

Importance reason: Proposes a scalable, order-aware agent for multi-degradation image restoration using VLM, with efficient trajectory simulation and plug-and-play expert extension. Signals: vision-language model agent, order-aware restoration, scalable expert extension, efficient ORTD construction, MiO-100 SOTA

Abstract: Real-world images rarely suffer from a single degradation, and the order in which degradations are removed substantially affects the final restoration quality, motivating agent-based image restoration (IR), where a vision-language model schedules a pool of pre-built restoration-experts. However, existing training-based agents require $\mathcal{O}((N^{\mathbf{D}})^{2})$ restoration-expert calls per image to construct the Optimal Restoration-action Trajectory Dataset (ORTD), where $N^{\mathbf{D}}$ denotes the number of degradation types in the universe $\mathbf{D}$, and couple agent training to a fixed restoration-expert pool, preventing extension to newly introduced restoration-experts without full retraining. To overcome these efficiency and extensibility bottlenecks, we propose \textbf{DiTTo}, a novel order-aware image restoration agent framework consisting of the DiTTo Simulator and the DiTTo Agent. The DiTTo Simulator combines $\cup$S-IR for single-step restoration-action simulation and AiO-IQA for per-action quality prediction, reducing ORTD construction to $\mathcal{O}(N^{\mathbf{D}})$ simulator calls per image; the DiTTo Agent is trained by SFT on the simulator-generated ORTD, followed by \textbf{Order-aware Restoration Alignment (ORA)} that aligns degradation identification, restoration-action-ordering, and output format along independent axes. This enables \textbf{plug-and-play scalable extensibility}: adding a new restoration-expert requires updating only the lightweight ORA stage. On the MiO-100 evaluation set with up to five concurrent degradations, our DiTTo Agent achieves state-of-the-art multi-degradation restoration quality among previous agent-based IR methods.

</details>


### [114] [Generating Reports or Repeating Templates? Measuring and Mitigating Template Collapse in 3D CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30984)
*Tom Maye-Lasserre, Yitong Li, Bailiang Jian, Morteza Ghahremani, Benedikt Wiestler, Christian Wachinger*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (67.39) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文识别并系统诊断了3D CT报告生成中VLM的"模板坍塌"问题（模型倾向于生成通用模板而忽略罕见但关键的病理发现），并提出CLarGen解耦框架，将临床检测与语言生成分离，通过潜在查询变换器进行多标签病理检测、病理引导检索和医学语言模型合成报告，显著提升了临床准确性（macro-F1从0.189提升至0.487）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学视觉语言模型虽然能生成流畅的放射学风格文本，但病理检测能力极低且输出多样性不足，倾向于坍塌为通用模板，漏报罕见但关键的病理发现。这种"模板坍塌"源于3D医学影像的独特约束（数据有限、标签严重不平衡、体素编码器信号弱），导致文本生成目标鼓励捷径学习和弱语义对齐的报告生成。

Method: 提出CLarGen解耦框架，将"说什么"（临床检测）与"怎么说"（语言合成）分离。包含三个组件：(i) Latent Query Transformer（LQFormer）进行多标签病理检测；(ii) 病理引导检索（Pathology-guided Retrieval）获取临床匹配的示例；(iii) 医学语言模型基于检测结果和检索上下文合成最终报告。

Result: 在多个SOTA 3D CT报告生成基线上，CLarGen有效缓解了模板坍塌，显著提升了临床准确性（macro-F1 0.487 vs 0.189；CRG 0.472 vs 0.368），同时保持了报告的流畅性。

Conclusion: 明确的、可测量的临床语义对齐对于抗模板坍塌的3D CT报告生成至关重要。解耦检测与生成的设计范式能有效缓解医学VLM中的捷径学习问题。

Classification reason: 该论文研究3D医学视觉语言模型（VLM）在CT报告生成中的模板坍塌问题，属于通用视觉语言模型的应用与改进，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical report generation, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keywords like clinical. Signals: Template collapse in 3D CT report generation, Decoupled detection and generation framework, Clinical accuracy improvement, Medical VLM failure analysis

Abstract: Modern 3D medical vision-language models (VLMs) can generate fluent radiology-style text while exhibit critically low pathology detection and output diversity, collapsing to generic templates that under-report rare yet critical findings. We identify this failure mode as Template Collapse. This failure stems from the unique constraints of 3D medical imaging, e.g., limited data, severe label imbalance, and weak signals from volumetric encoders. Under these constraints, text-generation objectives encourage shortcut learning and fluent but weakly grounded reports. We systematically diagnose the Template Collapse through clinical fidelity, output diversity, normal-template bias, and rare-finding survival. To mitigate it, we propose CLarGen, a decoupled framework that separates what to say (clinical detection) from how to say it (language synthesis). CLarGen uses (i) a Latent Query Transformer for multi-label pathology detection, (ii) pathology-guided retrieval for clinically matched exemplars, and (iii) a medical language model to synthesize the final report from detected findings and retrieved context. Across state-of-the-art 3D CT report generation baselines, CLarGen mitigates Template Collapse and substantially improves clinical accuracy (macro-F1 0.487 vs. 0.189; CRG 0.472 vs. 0.368) while maintaining fluent reporting. Our results suggest that explicit, measurable clinical grounding is essential for template-collapse-resistant 3D CT report generation. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [115] ["Intelegi Româneşte?'' A Recipe for Romanian Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31401)
*Mihai Masala, Marius Leordeanu, Mihai Dascalu, Traian Rebedea*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了如何为低资源语言（罗马尼亚语）构建视觉语言模型，通过机器翻译将英文 VLM 训练和评测数据转化为罗马尼亚语，并消融实验了视觉骨干、语言骨干和 OCR 数据的影响。同时构建了文化原生评测集 HoraVQA，实验表明罗马尼亚语适配的 VLM 在各项基准上一致超越同尺寸模型，甚至超过更大尺寸的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在英语上表现优异，但在低资源语言上性能急剧下降，缺乏大规模图像-文本语料库和文化相关的评测基准。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究如何为罗马尼亚语构建高性能 VLM。

Method: (1) 将英文 VLM 训练和评测语料通过机器翻译转化为罗马尼亚语，包括文本标注和图像内文字；(2) 消融实验对比不同规模的视觉骨干、多语言 vs 罗马尼亚语适配的 LLM 骨干、以及 OCR 风格图像-文本数据的影响；(3) 构建 HoraVQA——基于罗马尼亚日常场景的文化原生评测集。

Result: 罗马尼亚语适配的 VLM 一致优于同尺寸对照模型，在所有评测基准上甚至超越更大尺寸的模型。HoraVQA 提供了文化特定的评测维度。

Conclusion: 通过机器翻译构建训练数据和适配语言特定的 LLM 骨干，可以有效提升低资源语言 VLM 的性能。文化原生评测集对于准确评估低资源语言 VLM 至关重要。

Classification reason: 该论文系统性地构建了面向罗马尼亚语的视觉语言模型（VLM），涵盖数据构建、架构选择与评测，属于通用视觉语言模型与多模态指令微调范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on low-resource language VLM adaptation, not on core priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Low-resource VLM, Romanian language, machine translation pipeline, cultural evaluation, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) largely follow the text-only LLM trajectory, excelling on English benchmarks but sharply degrading on low-resource languages, where neither large-scale image-text corpora nor culturally grounded evaluations exist. We present a systematic study of building a language-specific VLM for Romanian, covering the full pipeline from data construction to architectural choices. We translate established English VLM training and evaluation corpora into Romanian, applying machine translation to textual annotations and to in-image text, preserving visual grounding while adapting the textual content. Using this data, we train and ablate a series of VLMs to isolate the contribution of (i) vision backbones of varying scale and pretraining, (ii) language backbones from multilingual to Romanian-adapted LLMs, and (iii) OCR-style image-text data. We further curate HoraVQA, a culturally native evaluation set grounded in Romanian everyday scenes. Romanian-adapted VLMs consistently outperform their same-sized counterparts and, across all evaluated benchmarks, even surpass models from the next larger size category.

</details>


### [116] [Task-Focused Memorization for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31075)
*Tao Zou, Yichen He, Tian Qiu, Yuan Lin, Hang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (74.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TaskMem，一种基于强化学习的任务聚焦记忆策略学习框架，使多模态智能体能够从无限的多模态观测流中选择性记忆与任务相关的内容。采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段学习如何记忆（优化记忆质量），第二阶段学习记忆什么（通过环境任务定义奖励模型微调适配器）。在 VideoMME、EgoLife、EgoTempo 的流式评测基准上，基于 Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B 的 VQA 准确率分别提升 6.3%、7.0% 和 5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体（如具身智能体）持续感知、推理和行动，接收无限的多模态观测流。现有方法主要关注记忆模块设计及准确性和保真度等基本要求，但关键挑战在于确定"记忆什么"——智能体需要从信息组合爆炸中有选择地保留与角色相关且对未来任务有价值的内容。目前缺乏可学习的记忆策略来动态聚焦任务相关记忆。

Method: 提出 TaskMem（Task-focused Memorization Policy Learning）框架，将记忆生成建模为可学习的记忆策略。采用两阶段训练：Phase One 在基本保真度要求下优化记忆质量，学习如何记忆；Phase Two 在部署后，智能体利用近期环境任务定义奖励模型，通过强化学习微调基础 MLLM 上的适配器，引导记忆策略聚焦任务相关内容。同时将 VideoMME、EgoLife、EgoTempo 重构为流式评测基准，模拟智能体处理流式观测并在线处理任务的真实场景，且仅允许使用记忆回答问题。

Result: 基于 Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B，TaskMem 在 VideoMME、EgoLife、EgoTempo 流式基准上分别将 VQA 准确率提升 6.3%、7.0% 和 5.3%。

Conclusion: 将记忆生成建模为可学习的记忆策略，并通过强化学习实现任务聚焦，能有效提升多模态智能体在流式环境中的长期记忆质量和任务表现。两阶段训练范式（先学如何记忆、后学记忆什么）为多模态智能体的持续学习和记忆管理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态智能体的长期记忆策略，基于 MLLM（Qwen3-VL）构建记忆生成与任务聚焦的强化学习框架，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 范畴，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"。

Importance reason: Strong memory framework for multimodal agents but not aligned with user's priority on continuous language/video generation models. Signals: Task-focused memorization, RL-based policy learning, streaming benchmarks, Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, multimodal agent memory

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for multimodal agents to build coherent experience, accumulate world knowledge, and achieve continual learning. However, constructing effective memory goes beyond memory module design and basic requirements such as accuracy and fidelity; the key challenge lies in determining what to memorize. Multimodal agents, such as embodied agents, continuously perceive, reason, and act in real or virtual environments, receiving an unbounded stream of multimodal observations. From this combinatorial explosion of information, an agent must selectively retain content that is relevant to its role in the environment and valuable for future tasks. To bridge this gap, we frame memory generation as a learnable memorization policy and introduce TaskMem (Task-focused Memorization Policy Learning), a reinforcement-learning-based framework that enables the policy to dynamically adjust its focus to the demands of real tasks encountered in the environment. TaskMem adopts a two-phase training paradigm: Phase One learns how to memorize by optimizing memory quality under fundamental fidelity requirements; Phase Two occurs after deployment, where the agent learns what to memorize by tuning an adapter on its base MLLM, using recent environment tasks to define a reward model that guides the memorization policy toward task-relevant content. To evaluate our approach, we reformulate VideoMME, EgoLife, and EgoTempo into streaming benchmarks that simulate a realistic setting in which an agent processes streaming observations and handles tasks arriving online. To isolate memory assessment, the questions must be answered using only the agent's memory, without access to raw video. Built on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, TaskMem improves VQA accuracy by 6.3%, 7.0%, and 5.3% on these benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [117] [FOCUS: Forcing In-Context Object Localization through Visual Support Constraints and Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31145)
*Mohammed Asad Karim, Vinay Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (79.91) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 FOCUS 框架，通过两阶段训练（显式优化支持框与查询图像间的上下文注意力 + GRPO 强化学习直接最小化定位误差）实现无类别监督的上下文目标定位，使 7B 模型超越 72B 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文定位方法依赖显式类别监督，导致类别偏差（偏向语义先验而非视觉证据），且无法处理无名称或实例特定的物体，限制了在图像编辑、个性化搜索等场景中的应用。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架：第一阶段通过视觉支持约束（Visual Support Constraints）显式优化支持框与查询图像之间的上下文注意力，不使用类别监督；第二阶段使用 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习直接最小化定位误差，强制模型依赖视觉对应关系而非语义先验。

Result: 7B 参数模型在上下文定位任务上超越包括 72B 在内的更大模型，消融实验验证了各组件（视觉支持约束、GRPO 优化）的贡献。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的定位目标函数可以超越模型规模缩放带来的收益，类别无关的视觉对应学习是实现鲁棒实例级上下文定位的关键方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的上下文定位（in-context localization），属于通用视觉语言模型的能力增强与训练方法，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly advances in-context visual grounding without category supervision, relevant to multimodal understanding and MLLM subtopics. Signals: In-context localization, category-agnostic, GRPO reinforcement learning, 7B model outperforms 72B, visual correspondence over semantic priors

Abstract: In-context localization (ICL) seeks to localize a target object specified by a small set of support examples in a query image, operating on the fly without training or parameter updates. Despite rapid advances in vision-language models (VLMs), achieving category-agnostic and visually grounded ICL remains an open problem, even though it is essential for applications such as image editing, personalized visual search, and retrieval. Existing methods are fragile and rely on explicit category supervision, which not only limits applicability in realistic settings with unnamed or instance-specific objects but also introduces category bias that steers predictions toward semantic priors rather than visual evidence. We introduce a two-stage training framework that explicitly optimizes in-context attention between support bounding boxes and query images without category supervision. We further refine localization via reinforcement learning using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to directly minimize localization error. This formulation enforces visual correspondence over semantic priors, yielding robust instance-level localization. Empirically, a 7B-parameter model trained with our objectives outperforms models up to 72B parameters, demonstrating that context-aware localization objectives can surpass scaling alone. Comprehensive ablations validate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [118] [Detect in Any Scene: An Agentic Framework for Object Detection with Experience-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31174)
*Wenlun Zhang, Jun Yin, Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 DetAS，一个基于 MLLM 智能体的目标检测框架，将检测建模为动态决策过程，通过自适应图像恢复和多专家检测器组合，并引入经验积累机制 DetAS-X 实现经验感知推理，在六个基准上平均 F1 提升 28.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测器在真实场景中因图像退化（如低光照、模糊）和物体分布异质性而泛化能力不足。传统方法依赖预定义条件和静态流水线，缺乏对动态环境的适应性。

Method: DetAS 框架包含两个核心组件：1) 自适应性图像恢复模块，动态判断是否需要以及如何增强图像；2) 多专家检测模块，集成多个领域专用检测器并通过实例级推理融合预测。DetAS-X 进一步引入自进化经验收集机制，从少量标注数据中积累节点级决策经验，在推理时实现经验感知推理。

Result: 在六个挑战性基准上，DetAS-X 相比现有基于 MLLM 的检测器平均 F1 提升 28.36%，在 DarkFace 上最高提升 37.01%。

Conclusion: 实验表明智能体式检测框架在复杂动态环境中具有显著优势，为未来目标检测在真实场景中的应用奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DetAS，利用 MLLM 作为中央智能体进行目标检测的决策与工作流编排，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 的应用范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Agentic MLLM detection framework is solid but not aligned with user's priority on continuous/multimodal generation or world models. Signals: MLLM agent for detection, experience-aware reasoning, adaptive image restoration, multi-expert detection, F1 improvement on benchmarks

Abstract: Object detection in real-world scenarios remains challenging due to diverse image degradations and heterogeneous object distributions, which significantly hinder the generalization of existing detectors. Conventional approaches, including scene-specific representation learning and end-to-end pipeline design, are inherently limited by their reliance on predefined conditions and lack adaptability to dynamic environments. In this paper, we propose DetAS, an agentic detection framework that formulates object detection as a dynamic decision process. Instead of relying on static pipelines, DetAS leverages a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a central agent to adaptively compose detection workflows by selecting from a toolbox of restoration modules and specialized detectors. Specifically, DetAS consists of two key components: Self-Adaptive Image Restoration, which dynamically determines whether and how to enhance images for downstream detection, and Multi-Expertise Detection, which integrates multiple domain-specialized detectors and resolves their predictions through instance-level reasoning. To further improve decision quality under fine-grained conditions, we introduce Self-Evolving Experience Harvesting and extend the framework to DetAS-X, which accumulates node-level decision experience from a small set of annotated data and enables experience-aware reasoning during inference. This mechanism allows the system to progressively refine its decision policy and adapt to diverse real-world scenarios. Extensive experiments on six challenging benchmarks demonstrate that DetAS-X significantly outperforms existing MLLM-based detectors, achieving an average improvement of 28.36% in F1 score, with up to 37.01% gain on DarkFace. These results demonstrate the promise of agentic detection and establish a solid foundation for its application in complex and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [119] [Personalize Your Large Vision-language Models With In-context Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31513)
*Yanshu Li, Jiaqian Li, Kuai Yu, Xi Xiao, Dongfang Liu, Tianyang Wang, Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 In-Context Prompt Tuning (ICPT)，一种面向大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的个性化方法。ICPT 使用轻量投影模块从多张参考图像中提取细粒度视觉语义，将其与身份标签映射为连续提示，并自适应确定提示长度。同时引入两种几何正则化方法，解耦关键身份与瞬态环境、分离不同概念以避免语义混淆。实验表明 ICPT 在多种任务和 LVLM 骨干上达到了最先进的个性化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 个性化方法依赖推理时训练，效率低下，且在复杂多图像、多概念场景中难以保持准确性，限制了 LVLM 系统的广泛部署。

Method: 提出 In-Context Prompt Tuning (ICPT)，包含：(1) 轻量投影模块，从多张参考图像中提取细粒度视觉语义，并与身份标签映射为连续提示；(2) 自适应提示长度机制，根据每个概念的视觉复杂度动态确定提示长度；(3) 两种几何正则化——解耦正则化（将关键身份与瞬态环境状态分离）和分离正则化（区分不同概念以避免语义混淆）。

Result: 在多种个性化任务和不同 LVLM 骨干（如 LLaVA）上，ICPT 达到了最先进的个性化精度，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ICPT 通过轻量投影模块和几何正则化，实现了高效、准确的 LVLM 个性化，在复杂多概念场景下表现优异，为 LVLM 的个性化部署提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ICPT（In-Context Prompt Tuning），通过轻量投影模块将参考图像中的细粒度视觉语义转化为连续提示，实现 LVLM 的个性化。属于通用视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）的个性化微调方法，与 canonical 子主题 "vlm_mllm_general" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong personalization method for LVLMs with efficient in-context tuning, but not directly on top-priority continuous multimodal or video generation. Signals: LVLM personalization, in-context prompt tuning, lightweight projection, geometric regularization, multi-concept handling

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong general multimodal capability and are increasingly deployed in downstream systems. This trend has driven growing interest in LVLM personalization, which aims to enable models to quickly and effectively learn out-of-distribution multimodal concepts to meet user-specific needs. However, many existing methods rely on inference-time training, which reduces efficiency. They also struggle to maintain accuracy in complex multi-image, multi-concept settings. These limitations restrict the broader deployment of LVLM-based systems. Therefore, this paper proposes in-context prompt tuning (ICPT). Specifically, ICPT employs a lightweight projection module capable of operating in complex scenarios to extract fine-grained visual semantics from multiple reference images, seamlessly transforming these features alongside identity-label mappings into continuous prompts. To maximize computational efficiency, this module adaptively determines the prompt length based on the intrinsic visual complexity of each concept. Crucially, to overcome the environmental biases and cross-concept interference prevalent in real-world applications, we introduce two novel geometric regularizations. These constraints refine prompt representations by decoupling key identities from transient environmental states and separating concepts to avoid semantic confusion. Extensive experiments show that ICPT achieves state-of-the-art personalization accuracy across diverse tasks and LVLM backbones.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

VQA、文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能。

### [120] [Seeing Before Agreeing: Aligning Multi-Agent Consensus with Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30698)
*Yuhan Wang, Shuochen Chang, Yalin Feng, Dongsheng Ma, Yuanzi Li, Zhengren Wang, Yinglong Yang, Yufei Chen, Yikang Wang, Shaoxu Sun, Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.MA, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出EAGLE，一种无需训练的证据对齐多智能体VQA框架。核心发现是仅靠答案层面的一致不足以保证可靠的多智能体VQA，还需要对齐各智能体依赖的图像区域（视觉证据）。EAGLE显式暴露每个VLM智能体的定位区域作为视觉证据，实现证据互验，并基于证据一致性指导最终决策。在六个VQA基准上取得最佳平均性能，且轻量、可解释、易于部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在VQA任务中仍存在幻觉和盲点。多智能体协作在文本QA中已取得成功，但在多模态领域尚未充分探索。现有方法主要采用文本中心协议，关注文本讨论而忽略视觉信息对齐。作者发现仅答案层面的一致不可靠，需要对齐视觉证据才能实现可信的共识。

Method: EAGLE是一个无需训练的证据中心框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 显式暴露每个VLM智能体回答时所依赖的图像定位区域作为视觉证据；(2) 让智能体之间对彼此的视觉证据进行相互验证；(3) 基于证据一致性（而非仅答案一致性）来指导最终决策。

Result: 在六个VQA基准上的实验表明，EAGLE在跨领域任务中取得了最佳平均性能，同时保持了轻量、可解释和易于部署的特点。

Conclusion: 对齐视觉证据而非仅对齐答案，是多智能体VQA中实现可靠共识的关键。EAGLE通过显式暴露和互验视觉证据，有效提升了多VLM协作的准确性和可解释性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多智能体协作下的视觉问答（VQA），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）规范子主题。论文聚焦于多VLM智能体通过视觉证据对齐来提升VQA可靠性，核心任务仍是VQA。

Importance reason: Novel multi-agent VQA alignment method but outside user's core priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal or generation models. Signals: Multi-agent VQA, Visual evidence alignment, Training-free framework, Interpretable consensus, No direct link to continuous language/video generation

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on visual question answering (VQA). To mitigate individual hallucinations and blind spots, aggregating diverse perspectives via multi-agent collaboration has emerged as a promising paradigm. While this approach has shown great success in textual QA, its potential in the multimodal domain remains under-explored. Existing multi-agent VQA methods predominantly adapt text-centric protocols, focusing on textual discussions while ignoring the alignment of visual information. In this work, we reveal a key insight: answer-level agreement is insufficient for reliable multi-agent VQA; \textit{aligned visual evidence} -- shared support from the image regions agents rely on -- is essential for trustworthy consensus. To leverage this insight, we propose EAGLE (\textbf{E}vidence-\textbf{A}ligned \textbf{G}rounded mu\textbf{L}ti-agent r\textbf{E}asoning), a training-free evidence-centered framework for coordinating multiple VLM agents. EAGLE explicitly exposes each agent's grounding regions as visual evidence, enables mutual verification over the evidence, and uses evidence consistency to guide final decision-making. Experiments on six VQA benchmarks show that EAGLE achieves best average performance across domains while remaining lightweight, interpretable, and practical for deployment.

</details>


### [121] [MechVQA: Benchmarking and Enhancing Multimodal LLMs on Comprehensive Mechanical Drawing Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30794)
*Qian Kou, Xiaofeng Shi, Yulin Li, Xiaosong Qiu, Xinyang Wang, Hua Zhou, Cao Dongxing*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (73.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MechVQA，首个面向机械工程图纸理解的多模态大模型评测数据集，包含 3.3k 高密度图纸和 21K QA 对，覆盖识别、推理、判断三个能力层级共 10 种细粒度任务。基于该数据集，作者进一步训练了 MechVL 模型，通过多阶段训练范式在总得分上超越最强闭源基线 7.57 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在通用 VQA 任务上表现优异，但在机械工程图纸理解上存在明显不足：图纸标注密度高、领域知识薄弱、严格的投影规则和几何约束下的空间关系推理不可靠，导致模型容易遗漏关键信息并给出错误答案。目前缺乏专门针对机械图纸理解的评测基准。

Method: 1) 构建 MechVQA 数据集：通过半自动构建和质量控制流水线，从真实机械图纸中生成 3.3k 高密度图片和 21K QA 对，涵盖识别（如尺寸标注、符号识别）、推理（如几何关系、投影关系）和判断（如公差合规性）三个能力层级共 10 种任务。2) 开发 MechVL 模型：采用多阶段训练范式，在通用 MLLM 基础上进行领域适配微调，建立强领域专用基线。

Result: MechVL 在 MechVQA 总得分上超越最强闭源基线 7.57 个百分点，显著提升了机械图纸理解能力。

Conclusion: MechVQA 为机械工程图纸理解提供了首个综合性评测基准，MechVL 验证了多阶段领域微调的有效性，为 MLLM 在机械设计与检测场景中的部署提供了可复用的基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了机械工程图纸理解数据集 MechVQA 和模型 MechVL，属于特定领域（机械图纸）的视觉问答与文档理解任务，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态 VQA 与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Specialized domain benchmark with limited cross-application to your priority subtopics. Signals: Mechanical drawing VQA, domain-specific benchmark, MLLM evaluation, multi-stage training, weak alignment with continuous/multimodal generation priorities

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated significant achievements in general visual question answering (VQA) tasks. However, they remain brittle on mechanical engineering drawings, where high annotation density and weak domain knowledge, compounded by unreliable spatial relation reasoning under strict projection rules and geometric constraints, make decisive cues easy to miss and frequently lead to wrong answers. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first comprehensive mechanical drawing understanding dataset, MechVQA, created through a semi-automated construction and quality-control pipeline. MechVQA contains 3.3k high-density pictures with 21K question-answer pairs, spanning 10 different fine-grained tasks across three capability levels: Recognition, Reasoning, and Judging, providing a testbed to evaluate and improve MLLM understanding on real-world mechanical drawings. On top of MechVQA, we then develop the MechVL model through a multi-stage training paradigm, building a strong domain-specialized baseline. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that MechVL outperforms the strongest closed-source baseline by 7.57 percentage points on the MechVQA total score, significantly enhancing mechanical drawing understanding ability and providing a reusable foundation for deploying MLLMs in mechanical design and inspection scenarios.

</details>


### [122] [Astra: a generalizable report generation foundation model for 3D computed tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31437)
*Zhuhao Wang, Fang Chen, Chaohui Yu, Zihan Li, Yuchao Zheng, Jing Wang, Xuan Yang, Jia Guo, Zhenlu Yang, Xingju Zheng, Yihua Sun, Haojie Han, Xiaoxiao Qin, Zhan Feng, Wenbo Xiao, Chao Zhu, Yuehua Li, Shipeng Zhang, Hao Luo, Yunsong Peng, Fan Wang, Hongen Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Astra 是一个基于 90,678 对胸腹部 CT-报告数据训练的通用 CT 报告生成基础模型，通过报告风格统一和强化学习诊断一致性优化，在六个外部数据集上实现 SOTA，平均细粒度诊断指标提升 44.1%，并在临床工作流中加速胸部报告起草 29.6%、提升腹部报告完整性 11.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT 报告生成高度依赖放射科医生逐层阅片，耗时且受限于专家经验。现有方法缺乏支持多区域报告且跨机构泛化的基础模型，不同数据集间的报告风格和诊断术语不一致导致联合训练受噪声文本监督影响，限制了模型泛化能力。

Method: Astra 首先构建了包含 90,678 对胸腹部 CT-报告的大规模数据集 CTRgDB，涵盖 353,671 个异常发现和八个器官系统。通过报告风格统一（harmonizing report style）处理跨机构文本不一致性，再使用强化学习（RL）进一步优化诊断一致性。模型基于视觉-语言架构，支持多区域报告生成。

Result: 在 CTRgDB 和六个外部数据集上，Astra 达到 SOTA，平均细粒度诊断指标提升 44.1%（P<0.001）。临床工作流评估中，Astra 辅助使胸部报告起草速度提升 29.6%，腹部报告完整性提升 11.3%。此外，Astra 作为基础模型可提升下游诊断性能，并通过高质量报告合成扩展视觉-语言预训练。

Conclusion: Astra 是一个通用、可泛化的 CT 报告生成基础模型，在跨机构、多区域场景下均表现优异，可作为临床辅助工具和下一代 AI 医疗基础设施。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于3D CT影像的自动报告生成，属于医学视觉理解与文本生成任务，与多模态理解中的视觉问答/文档理解（VQA/报告生成）方向高度相关。虽然具体领域是医学影像，但任务本质是视觉-语言理解与生成，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）最为合适。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical report generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Medical domain, CT report generation, Clinical workflow, Negative keywords present, Low personal relevance

Abstract: CT interpretation requires radiologists to review hundreds of volumetric slices per examination, making reporting time-consuming and highly expertise-dependent. Automated CT report generation offers a promising route to improving clinical efficiency, yet the field still lacks a generalizable CT report generation foundation model that supports multi-region reporting and remains robust across external real-world cohorts. Intrinsic inconsistencies in reporting style and diagnostic terminology across cohorts make naive joint training prone to noisy textual supervision, thereby limiting model generalizability. Here we present Astra, a generalizable CT report generation foundation model trained on 90,678 thoracoabdominal CT-report pairs (CTRgDB) with 353,671 abnormalities spanning eight organ systems. By harmonizing report style and further refining diagnostic consistency via reinforcement learning, Astra achieves style-consistent and diagnostically accurate report generation across diverse anatomical regions and institutions. Evaluating on CTRgDB and six external cohorts, Astra achieves state-of-the-art performance with a 44.1% average improvement in fine-grained diagnostic metrics (P<0.001). In real-world clinical workflows, Astra assistance accelerates chest report drafting by 29.6% and improves abdominal report completeness by 11.3% (P<0.001). Furthermore, Astra also demonstrates broad utility as a foundation for CT AI development, improving downstream diagnostic performance and scaling vision-language pretrain through high-quality report synthesis. Overall, Astra serves as a broadly accessible clinical assistant and a pivotal infrastructure for the next generation of AI-powered healthcare.

</details>


### [123] [Recognizing Co-Speech Gestures in-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31589)
*Sindhu B Hegde, K R Prajwal, Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了 GRW（Gesture Recognition in the Wild）数据集，包含 156,688 个手动标注的视频片段，覆盖 150 个词汇的手势语义标注。基于该数据集，训练视频模型完成三个任务：手势语义分类、手势对应词汇识别、手势时序定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型难以捕捉与语音语义相关的伴随手势（co-speech gestures），主要瓶颈在于缺乏精确标注的训练数据。现有数据集规模小、词汇覆盖有限，无法支撑大规模手势识别研究。

Method: 构建 GRW 数据集：从大规模视频中收集并手动标注 156,688 个视频片段，每个片段标注手势对应的具体词汇（共 150 个词）及帧级时间边界。基于该数据集，训练视频模型完成三个任务：(a) 二分类判断手势是否为语义性手势；(b) 多分类识别手势对应的词汇；(c) 时序定位手势起止帧。

Result: 建立了三个任务的基准评测结果，展示了 GRW 数据集在规模、多样性和标注精度上的优势。实验表明，在该数据集上训练的模型能够有效识别语义性伴随手势并定位其时间边界。

Conclusion: GRW 数据集填补了大规模语义手势识别数据集的空白，为多模态模型理解人类非语言交流提供了重要资源。该工作有望推动手势识别、多模态理解和人机交互等领域的发展。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自然场景中伴随语音的手势识别，将手势映射到特定语义词汇并进行时序定位，属于多模态理解中视觉与语言对齐的任务。虽然现有 canonical subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解" 不完全匹配，但它是所有现有 subtopic 中最为接近的——该论文本质上是一个视觉-语言映射与理解任务（将视觉手势识别为语义词汇），与 VQA 共享"视觉输入→语义输出"的范式。其他 subtopic（如视频理解、多模态推理）也部分相关，但该论文的核心是"识别手势对应的词汇"，更接近视觉问答/理解任务。

Importance reason: Dataset and task are niche to gesture recognition, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: gesture recognition dataset, co-speech gesture, multimodal understanding, not autoregressive or diffusion, no continuous token or world model

Abstract: While humans naturally gesture during speech, only a sparse subset of these movements are visually depictive and semantically linked to specific spoken words. Current multimodal models struggle to capture these semantic co-speech gestures, heavily bottlenecked by a lack of precisely annotated training data. To address this, we introduce the Gesture Recognition in the Wild (GRW) dataset, the first large-scale benchmark designed to map unconstrained human gestures to specific words with frame-accurate temporal boundaries. Comprising 156,688 manually annotated video clips, GRW spans a highly diverse 150-word taxonomy of physical actions, spatial descriptors, and abstract concepts. We leverage GRW to train video models to (a) classify gestures as semantic or not, (b) recognize the word corresponding to a co-speech gesture, and (c) temporally localize the gesture. We also use GRW to establish benchmarks for these three tasks.

</details>

### [124] [Towards Effective Long-Video Event Prediction via Multi-Level Event Semantics Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31069)
*Bo Peng, YuanJie Lyu, PengGang Qin, Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VISTA 框架，通过多层级事件语义挖掘（字符级视觉提示、知识增强迭代检索、类人提议-检索策略）实现长视频事件预测，解决了现有长视频语言模型在事件预测任务上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频语言模型（LVLM）在问答和摘要任务上表现良好，但无法泛化到事件预测任务，因为它们既不能精确提取事件相关细节，也无法对事件发展进行细粒度分析。长视频事件预测面临多模态上下文庞大、叙事复杂等挑战，目前尚缺乏有效方法。

Method: VISTA 框架包含三个层级：1）字符级视觉提示（character-centric visual prompt）精确提取事件相关视觉细节，增强细节级语义；2）知识增强迭代检索策略（knowledge-enhanced iterative retrieval）引导 LLM 逐步构建逻辑连贯的事件链，改善事件级叙事；3）类人提议-检索策略（propose-then-retrieve）生成多样化的未来提议并整合多层级线索，产生鲁棒准确的预测。

Result: 在真实世界长视频数据集上的大量实验验证了 VISTA 在长视频事件预测任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: VISTA 通过多层级事件语义挖掘有效解决了长视频事件预测问题，为长视频理解与未来事件预测提供了新的框架思路。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦长视频事件预测，属于长视频理解与时序推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on long-video event prediction, not directly on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: long-video event prediction, multi-level semantics, character-centric visual prompt, knowledge-enhanced retrieval, propose-then-retrieve

Abstract: Accurately predicting future events is fundamental to content understanding and decision-making across various domains. While prior research has primarily focused on text or short-video scenarios, long-video event prediction, characterized by vast multimodal context and more complex narratives, remains underexplored. Meanwhile, although recent Long-Video Language Models (LVLMs), built on Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), have shown promise in long-video question answering and summarization, they struggle to generalize to event prediction, as they can neither precisely extract event-related details nor perform fine-grained analysis of event development. To address this gap, we propose VISTA, a multi-level event semantics mining framework for long-video event prediction. Initially, VISTA applies a character-centric visual prompt to precisely extract event-related visual details, enhancing detail-level semantics; subsequently, it employs a knowledge-enhanced iterative retrieval strategy, guiding the LLM to progressively construct logically coherent event chains, thereby improving event-level narratives; ultimately, VISTA adopts a human-like propose-then-retrieve strategy to generate diverse future-oriented proposals and integrate multi-level clues, producing robust and accurate predictions. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of VISTA for long-video event prediction.

</details>


### [125] [Polyphony: Diffusion-based Dual-Hand Action Segmentation with Alternating Vision Transformer and Semantic Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31115)
*Hao Zheng, Hu Wang, Tiantian Zheng, Prajjwal Bhattarai, Tuka Alhanai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Polyphony 提出一种三阶段方法用于双手动作分割：交替训练的双手视觉 Transformer 平衡梯度贡献、语义特征条件化增强细粒度动作区分、扩散分割头融合双手特征，在 HA-ViD、ATTACH 和 Breakfast 数据集上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手动作分割面临双手依赖复杂、视觉不对称、主导手梯度垄断以及细粒度语义模糊等挑战，现有方法难以同时处理这些问题。

Method: (1) Alternating Dual-Hand Vision Transformer：交替训练左右手 mini-batch 以平衡梯度贡献，共享时空编码器；(2) Semantic Feature Conditioning：用结构化组合动作描述对齐视觉特征，增强语义判别；(3) Diffusion-Based Segmentation：跨手特征融合 + 自适应损失加权。

Result: 在 HA-ViD 和 ATTACH 数据集上提升高达 16.8 个点，在 Breakfast 数据集上达到 82.5%，超越使用 12 倍大 backbone 的先前最佳方法。

Conclusion: Polyphony 通过交替训练、语义条件化和扩散分割有效解决了双手动作分割中的关键挑战，统一单 backbone 模型即可超越需要独立 per-hand 模型的基线方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究双手动作分割（dual-hand action segmentation），属于视频理解中的时序密集预测任务，与 [CANONICAL] 视频理解与时序推理 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on dual-hand action segmentation, which is not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: SOTA on dual-hand datasets, diffusion-based segmentation head, alternating vision transformer, semantic feature conditioning, not in user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Dual-hand action segmentation, densely predicting actions for both hands from untrimmed videos, is essential for understanding complex bimanual activities. However, it poses several unique challenges: complex inter-hand dependencies, visual asymmetry between hands, representation conflicts where the dominant hand monopolizes gradients, and semantic ambiguity in fine-grained actions. We propose Polyphony, a three-stage method to address these challenges through: (1) an Alternating Dual-Hand Vision Transformer that alternates training between left- and right-hand mini-batches to ensure balanced gradient contributions from both hands while sharing a spatio-temporal encoder; (2) Semantic Feature Conditioning that aligns visual features with structured, compositional action descriptions to enhance discrimination of semantically similar actions; and (3) Diffusion-Based Segmentation with cross-hand feature fusion for inter-hand coordination and adaptive loss weighting for balancing performance. Polyphony achieves state-of-the-art on both dual-hand datasets (HA-ViD, ATTACH) with improvements up to 16.8 points, and on the single-stream Breakfast dataset (82.5%), outperforming the prior best method that uses a 12x larger backbone. Notably, our unified model with a single shared backbone surpasses baselines requiring separate per-hand models. Code is at .

</details>


### [126] [HiERO-StepG @ Ego4D Step Grounding Challenge: hierarchical activity understanding enables zero-shot step grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31227)
*Andrea Zenotto, Simone Alberto Peirone, Francesca Pistilli, Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HiERO-StepG，一种零样本的步骤定位方法，利用弱监督层次化表征学习（HiERO）从无标注视频中自动发现程序性活动的层次结构，通过聚类检测步骤，并在 Ego4D Step Grounding 挑战赛中取得第二名（R@1 IoU=0.3 达 56.27%），无需任何任务特定微调或程序级标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的步骤定位方法依赖大量细粒度标注，扩展性差。而程序性活动（如烹饪、维修）天然具有层次结构，作者希望利用这种结构从无标注视频中自动学习步骤表征，实现零样本步骤定位。

Method: 基于 HiERO 框架，通过弱监督学习将功能相关的动作在特征空间中映射到相近位置，仅利用细粒度动作级叙述（narrations）。然后通过简单聚类检测步骤边界。针对 Ego4D 挑战赛，进一步引入粗细粒度步骤分配一致性约束、严格时序单调性约束，以及后处理去噪策略。

Result: 在 Ego4D Step Grounding 挑战赛全球排行榜上以 R@1 (IoU=0.3) 56.27% 的成绩排名第二，且完全零样本，无需程序级标注。

Conclusion: 程序性活动的层次结构可以从无标注视频中自然涌现，弱监督层次化表征学习结合简单聚类即可实现有效的零样本步骤定位，为减少标注依赖提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文关注 Ego4D 第一视角视频中的步骤定位（step grounding），属于视频时序理解与定位任务，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Zero-shot step grounding is a niche video understanding task with limited direct relevance to the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: zero-shot step grounding, hierarchical activity understanding, Ego4D challenge, weak supervision, no task-specific fine-tuning

Abstract: Procedural activities follow well-defined structures: whether we consider a cooking recipe or a mechanic repairing a car, these activities naturally decompose in a hierarchy of steps and sub-steps. Traditional approaches for step grounding require extensive annotations and scale poorly. Instead, we argue that such hierarchical structure can emerge naturally from uncurated videos of human activities through recurring patterns of co-occurring actions and activities. Our approach builds on HiERO, a weakly-supervised representation learning approach that maps close in the feature space actions that are functionally related to each other, leveraging only fine-grained action-level narrations. In this feature space, procedure steps can be detected by a simple clustering, with no additional task-specific fine-tuning. For the Ego4D Step Grounding challenge, we augment this approach by ensuring fine and coarse level agreement in step assignments, enforcing strict temporal monotonicity of the grounded steps and post-processing the detected steps to reduce the impact of noisy predictions. We call this approach HiERO-StepG and it achieves 56.27 % on the R@1 (IoU = 0.3) metric on the global leaderboard at submission time, ranking second while being completely zero-shot and not requiring procedure-specific annotations. Project page: .

</details>


### [127] [EGOSTREAM: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Streaming Episodic Memory in Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31557)
*Rosario Forte, Giuseppe Lando, Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EGOSTREAM 是一个面向第一视角流式视频的情节记忆诊断基准，包含 2250 个问题，覆盖 7 个认知维度，并引入 Answer Validity Window (AVW) 概念以区分模型遗忘与自然世界状态变化。在统一 Qwen3-VL 框架下评测多种记忆管理机制，发现不同方法在准确率相近时记忆特征差异显著，且所有方法均远低于实时处理速度，最高准确率仅约 45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频基准缺乏对模型"记住了什么、记住了多久"的诊断能力，无法区分模型遗忘与场景自然变化导致的答案失效，亟需一个细粒度的诊断工具来评估自主智能体的连续情节记忆能力。

Method: 构建 EGOSTREAM 基准：从 Ego4D 中筛选 2,250 个问题，按细节、空间、时间、事件、社交、因果和前瞻记忆 7 个认知维度分类；提出 Answer Validity Window (AVW) 概念定义答案的有效时间窗口，扩展为 8,528 个召回条件评估。在统一 Qwen3-VL 骨干上集成滑动窗口、attention sink、KV-cache 剪枝/合并/卸载等记忆管理机制进行系统评测。

Result: 不同记忆机制在相同准确率下表现出截然不同的记忆特征：token 剪枝比 token 合并更好地保留细粒度细节和时序结构；量化卸载可改善超长期召回；所有方法均远低于实时处理速度（>1s/帧），最高准确率约 45%。

Conclusion: EGOSTREAM 揭示了当前流式 MLLM 在情节记忆方面的关键缺陷，为未来架构改进提供了诊断性测试平台。

Classification reason: 该论文关注第一视角（egocentric）视频中的流式情节记忆评测，属于视频理解与时序推理方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视频理解与时序推理"（video_understanding_temporal）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong diagnostic benchmark for episodic memory in streaming video, but not aligned with user's priority on generation or continuous models. Signals: episodic memory benchmark, egocentric video, streaming MLLM, memory management mechanisms, low accuracy ceiling

Abstract: Continuous episodic memory is a core capability for autonomous agents operating in dynamic, real-world environments, yet current streaming video benchmarks provide limited tools for diagnosing what models remember and for how long. We introduce \egostream, a diagnostic benchmark for streaming episodic memory evaluation in egocentric vision. \egostream organizes 2,250 curated questions along seven cognitive dimensions: detail, spatial, temporal, event, social, causal, and prospective memory. We introduce the Answer Validity Window (AVW), which specifies the temporal span an answer remains valid as the observed scene evolves. This allows us to expand the questions into 8,528 recall-conditioned evaluations, enabling controlled testing from instant to ultra-long-term recall while separating genuine model forgetting from natural world-state changes. We rigorously establish baseline performance through a unified streaming MLLM framework that compares several state-of-the-art memory-management mechanisms, covering sliding windows, attention sinks, KV-cache pruning, merging, and offloading. Experiments within a unified Qwen3-VL backbone reveal that comparable aggregate accuracies mask starkly different memory profiles. For instance, token pruning preserves fine-grained details and temporal structure significantly better than token merging, while quantized offloading rescues ultra-long-term recall. Ultimately, all mechanisms operate well below real-time (>1s per frame), and top performing methods ceil at about 45\% accuracy, exposing critical gaps in current architectures. \egostream provides the diagnostic testbed needed to close these gaps.

</details>


### [128] [Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31598)
*Cristobal Eyzaguirre, Jiajun Wu, Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: StateKV 是一种推理时方法，通过固定容量的重要性驱动循环状态在帧间传递跨帧上下文，将预训练长视频 VLM 的视频预填充从二次复杂度降至线性，无需微调或架构改动，在多个长视频基准和模型上接近全自注意力性能并优于滑动窗口方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频 VLM 的视频编码器依赖时空自注意力，计算和延迟随帧数二次增长，难以扩展到长视频和流式场景。已有的效率方法（如帧/ token 丢弃、粗粒度注意力近似）虽提升可扩展性但往往损失精度。

Method: 提出 StateKV，一种推理时方法。它维护一个固定容量的重要性驱动循环状态（recurrent state）来跨帧传递上下文，同时保留第二个完整的逐帧缓存用于解码。视频预填充阶段通过循环状态线性地处理帧序列，避免二次自注意力计算。

Result: 在三个长视频基准上，覆盖三个模型家族（七个模型）和多种规模，StateKV 精度接近全自注意力，且一致优于主流的滑动窗口/基于近期性的流式近似方法。同时，StateKV 降低了视频预填充的 FLOPs 成本，使得在固定计算预算下可以运行更大模型以获得更强精度。

Conclusion: StateKV 提供了一种实用的、无需训练的推理时方案，使预训练长视频 VLM 能够以线性时间处理长视频，为可扩展的长视频理解迈出了实际的一步。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦长视频理解中 VLM 的推理效率问题，属于视频理解与时序推理（video_understanding_temporal）这一规范子主题。论文提出的 StateKV 方法针对长视频 VLM 的预填充阶段进行线性时间优化，核心场景是长视频理解，因此映射到该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for long video understanding, a key priority in model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: linear scaling video prefill, inference-time method, no fine-tuning needed, strong benchmark results, practical for large models

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-continuous_multimodal_reasoning'></div>

## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [129] [ReGuLaR: Relation-Grounded Latent Reasoning for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30587)
*Zihu Wang, Karthik Somayaji N.S, Peng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 高优先级 (89.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ReGuLaR 提出了一种关系锚定的连续潜空间推理框架，通过训练阶段的 ReGFormer 模块将潜状态显式锚定到图像中与问题相关的物体及其关系上，在推理时无需该模块即可生成答案，并在多个基准上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LVLM 的 CoT 推理使用离散文本作为中间步骤，难以编码连续的视觉证据；而现有的连续潜空间推理方法未能充分将潜状态与视觉证据中的组合性和关系性结构联系起来。

Method: 提出 ReGuLaR 框架：1）训练阶段引入 ReGFormer 模块，将潜状态聚焦于问题相关的物体边界框和物体间关系；2）构建 RGROUNDING-351K 数据集，标注关键物体边界框和物体间关系；3）推理时无需 ReGFormer，模型直接进行潜空间推理并生成答案。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上，ReGuLaR 一致优于现有方法，取得 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 将连续潜空间推理显式锚定到视觉关系结构上能有效提升 LVLM 的推理能力，且训练时的关系引导可以在推理时移除，不影响推理效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ReGuLaR，一种将连续潜空间推理与视觉关系结构显式关联的框架，属于多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning 的范畴，与 canonical 子主题"连续多模态推理与潜空间思维"高度匹配。同时论文也涉及视觉语言模型（VLM/MLLM）的推理能力提升，因此也匹配多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous latent reasoning for vision-language models, a core priority subtopic with high weight. Signals: continuous latent reasoning, relation-grounded, state-of-the-art, multimodal understanding, new dataset

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly improved the reasoning ability of large vision-language models (LVLMs) by verbalizing intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, such discrete textual rationales are often insufficient for encoding continuous visual evidence. Recent work addresses this limitation by moving reasoning into continuous latent space. Despite promising progress, existing methods leave latent reasoning insufficiently connected to the compositional and relational structure of visual evidence. To address this gap, we introduce ReGuLaR, a relation grounded latent reasoning framework that explicitly grounds latent states in these critical yet overlooked visual evidence. ReGuLaR uses a training-time ReGFormer to focus latent reasoning on question-relevant objects and inter-object relations, while at inference time the model reasons and generates answers without invoking the ReGFormer. To support training ReGuLaR, we construct RGROUNDING-351K, a real-world vision-language dataset annotated with key object bounding boxes and inter-object relations. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks show that ReGuLaR consistently outperforms existing approaches and achieves state-of-the-art performance. We include our code in the submission and will release the code and training data publicly upon acceptance.

</details>
